* correccion: declarar clase zsm_os en mensajes y usar tipo numerico abap de seis posiciones.

MODULE status_0100 OUTPUT.
 SET PF-STATUS 'MAIN'. SET TITLEBAR 'T100'. cl_app=>screen( ).
ENDMODULE.
*
* @001 modulo user_command_0100: despachar eventos de los controles y procesar la salida de pantalla.
*
MODULE user_command_0100 INPUT.
 cl_gui_cfw=>dispatch( ).
 CASE sy-ucomm.
  WHEN 'BACK' OR 'EXIT' OR 'CANC'.
   cl_app=>close( ). LEAVE TO SCREEN 0.
 ENDCASE.
ENDMODULE.
CLASS cl_app IMPLEMENTATION.
*
* @001 metodo value: obtener un componente dinamico y representar su valor sin agrupacion numerica.
*
  METHOD value.
    FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_f> TYPE any.
    DATA: v_lo_type TYPE REF TO cl_abap_typedescr,
      v_l_buffer TYPE c LENGTH 256.
    ASSIGN COMPONENT v_i_field OF STRUCTURE wa_i_row TO <fs_f>.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0. RETURN. ENDIF.
    v_lo_type = cl_abap_typedescr=>describe_by_data( <fs_f> ).
    CASE v_lo_type->type_kind.
      WHEN cl_abap_typedescr=>typekind_packed OR
           cl_abap_typedescr=>typekind_float OR
           cl_abap_typedescr=>typekind_int OR
           cl_abap_typedescr=>typekind_int1 OR
           cl_abap_typedescr=>typekind_int2.
        WRITE <fs_f> TO v_l_buffer NO-GROUPING.
        v_r_value = v_l_buffer.
        CONDENSE v_r_value NO-GAPS.
        REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF ',' IN v_r_value WITH '.'.
        IF v_r_value CP '*-'.
          REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '-' IN v_r_value WITH ''.
          CONCATENATE '-' v_r_value INTO v_r_value.
        ENDIF.
      WHEN OTHERS.
        v_r_value = <fs_f>.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo literal: escapar comillas para construir condiciones de lectura dinamicas.
*
  METHOD literal.
    v_r_value = v_i_value.
    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '''' IN v_r_value WITH ''''''.
    CONCATENATE '''' v_r_value '''' INTO v_r_value.
  ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo fields: obtener los componentes de la tabla incluyendo ampliaciones del diccionario.
*
  METHOD fields.
    DATA: v_lo_table TYPE REF TO cl_abap_tabledescr,
      v_lo_line TYPE REF TO cl_abap_structdescr,
      it_l_view TYPE abap_component_view_tab,
      wa_l_view LIKE LINE OF it_l_view,
      wa_l_component LIKE LINE OF it_r_fields.
    v_lo_table ?= cl_abap_typedescr=>describe_by_data( it_data ).
    v_lo_line ?= v_lo_table->get_table_line_type( ).
    it_l_view = v_lo_line->get_included_view( ).
    LOOP AT it_l_view INTO wa_l_view.
      CLEAR wa_l_component.
      wa_l_component-name = wa_l_view-name.
      wa_l_component-type = wa_l_view-type.
      APPEND wa_l_component TO it_r_fields.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo linked: leer registros relacionados por claves verificadas y respetar el limite de detalle.
*
  METHOD linked.
    FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_out> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_tmp> TYPE STANDARD TABLE,
      <fs_parent> TYPE any, <fs_source> TYPE any, <fs_source2> TYPE any.
    DATA: v_lr_tmp TYPE REF TO data, it_l_keys TYPE ty_keys,
      wa_l_key TYPE ty_key, v_l_a TYPE string, v_l_b TYPE string,
      v_l_where TYPE string, v_l_base TYPE string, v_l_count TYPE i,
      v_l_limit TYPE i, v_lx TYPE REF TO cx_root, v_l_msg TYPE string,
      v_l_tabclass TYPE dd02l-tabclass.
    SELECT SINGLE tabclass INTO v_l_tabclass FROM dd02l
      WHERE tabname = v_i_table AND as4local = 'A'.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR v_l_tabclass <> 'TRANSP'.
      log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = v_i_table
        v_i_message = 'Tabla transparente activa no disponible; revisar SE11' ).
      CREATE DATA v_rr_data TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF aufk.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    CREATE DATA v_rr_data TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF (v_i_table).
    CREATE DATA v_lr_tmp TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF (v_i_table).
    ASSIGN v_rr_data->* TO <fs_out>. ASSIGN v_lr_tmp->* TO <fs_tmp>.
    IF it_parent IS INITIAL. RETURN. ENDIF.
    LOOP AT it_parent ASSIGNING <fs_parent>.
      ASSIGN COMPONENT v_i_source OF STRUCTURE <fs_parent> TO <fs_source>.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = v_i_table
          v_i_message = |Campo fuente no existe: { v_i_source }| ). RETURN.
      ENDIF.
      IF <fs_source> IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
      v_l_a = value( wa_i_row = <fs_parent> v_i_field = v_i_source ).
      IF v_l_a IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
      v_l_a = literal( v_l_a ).
      CONCATENATE v_i_target '=' v_l_a INTO wa_l_key-value SEPARATED BY space.
      IF v_i_source2 IS NOT INITIAL.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT v_i_source2 OF STRUCTURE <fs_parent> TO <fs_source2>.
        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = v_i_table
            v_i_message = |Segunda clave fuente no existe: { v_i_source2 }| ). RETURN.
        ENDIF.
        v_l_b = value( wa_i_row = <fs_parent> v_i_field = v_i_source2 ).
        IF v_l_b IS INITIAL.
          log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = v_i_table
            v_i_message = 'Segunda clave de relacion ausente; no se hace lectura parcial' ).
          RETURN.
        ENDIF.
        v_l_b = literal( v_l_b ).
        CONCATENATE wa_l_key-value 'AND' v_i_target2 '=' v_l_b
          INTO wa_l_key-value SEPARATED BY space.
      ENDIF.
      CONCATENATE '(' wa_l_key-value ')' INTO wa_l_key-value.
      INSERT wa_l_key INTO TABLE it_l_keys.
    ENDLOOP.
    LOOP AT it_l_keys INTO wa_l_key.
      IF v_l_base IS INITIAL. v_l_base = wa_l_key-value.
      ELSE. CONCATENATE v_l_base 'OR' wa_l_key-value INTO v_l_base SEPARATED BY space.
      ENDIF.
      ADD 1 TO v_l_count.
      AT LAST. v_l_count = 100. ENDAT.
      IF v_l_count < 100. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
      CONCATENATE '(' v_l_base ')' INTO v_l_where.
      IF v_i_extra IS NOT INITIAL.
        CONCATENATE v_l_where 'AND' v_i_extra INTO v_l_where SEPARATED BY space.
      ENDIF.
      v_l_limit = p_det + 1.
      TRY.
          CLEAR <fs_tmp>.
          SELECT * FROM (v_i_table) INTO TABLE <fs_tmp>
            UP TO v_l_limit ROWS WHERE (v_l_where).
          APPEND LINES OF <fs_tmp> TO <fs_out>.
          IF lines( <fs_out> ) > p_det.
            CLEAR <fs_out>.
            log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = v_i_table
              v_i_message = 'Limite de detalle por paquete excedido; reducir P_PACK o ampliar P_DET' ).
            RETURN.
          ENDIF.
        CATCH cx_root INTO v_lx.
          v_l_msg = v_lx->get_text( ).
          log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = v_i_table v_i_message = v_l_msg ).
          CLEAR <fs_out>. RETURN.
      ENDTRY.
      CLEAR: v_l_base, v_l_count.
    ENDLOOP.
    SORT <fs_out>.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM <fs_out> COMPARING ALL FIELDS.
  ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo auth: resolver campos del objeto de autorizacion y comprobar los valores de la orden.
*
  METHOD auth.
    DATA: wa_l_obj TYPE tobj, v_l_field TYPE string,
      v_l_idx TYPE n LENGTH 1, v_l_component TYPE string,
      v_l_val TYPE string, v_l_order TYPE aufnr.
    TYPES: BEGIN OF ty_auth, field TYPE c LENGTH 10,
      val TYPE c LENGTH 40, END OF ty_auth.
    DATA: it_l_auth TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_auth,
      wa_l_auth TYPE ty_auth.
    FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_field> TYPE any.
    v_r_ok = abap_false.
    SELECT SINGLE * INTO wa_l_obj FROM tobj WHERE objct = v_i_object.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'AUTH'
        v_i_message = |Objeto de autorizacion inexistente: { v_i_object }| ). RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    DO 10 TIMES.
      IF sy-index = 10. v_l_component = 'FIEL0'.
      ELSE.
        v_l_idx = sy-index. CONCATENATE 'FIEL' v_l_idx INTO v_l_component.
      ENDIF.
      ASSIGN COMPONENT v_l_component OF STRUCTURE wa_l_obj TO <fs_field>.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR <fs_field> IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
      v_l_field = <fs_field>. CLEAR v_l_val.
      CASE v_l_field.
        WHEN 'ACTVT'. v_l_val = '03'.
        WHEN 'AUFART' OR 'AUART'. v_l_val = value( wa_i_row = wa_i_aufk v_i_field = 'AUART' ).
        WHEN 'IWERK' OR 'INGRP'. v_l_val = value( wa_i_row = wa_i_afih v_i_field = v_l_field ).
        WHEN 'SWERK'. v_l_val = value( wa_i_row = wa_i_iloa v_i_field = 'SWERK' ).
        WHEN 'QMART'.
          IF wa_i_qmel IS SUPPLIED. v_l_val = value( wa_i_row = wa_i_qmel v_i_field = 'QMART' ). ENDIF.
        WHEN 'BEGRP' OR 'WERKS' OR 'KOKRS' OR 'KOSTL' OR 'AUFNR'.
          v_l_val = value( wa_i_row = wa_i_aufk v_i_field = v_l_field ).
          IF v_l_val IS INITIAL.
            v_l_val = value( wa_i_row = wa_i_iloa v_i_field = v_l_field ).
          ENDIF.
        WHEN OTHERS.
          log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'AUTH'
            v_i_message = |Campo de autorizacion sin mapeo: { v_i_object }/{ v_l_field }| ).
          RETURN.
      ENDCASE.
      wa_l_auth-field = v_l_field. wa_l_auth-val = v_l_val.
      APPEND wa_l_auth TO it_l_auth.
    ENDDO.
    DATA: a1 TYPE ty_auth, a2 TYPE ty_auth, a3 TYPE ty_auth,
      a4 TYPE ty_auth, a5 TYPE ty_auth, a6 TYPE ty_auth,
      a7 TYPE ty_auth, a8 TYPE ty_auth, a9 TYPE ty_auth, a10 TYPE ty_auth.
    READ TABLE it_l_auth INDEX 1 INTO a1. READ TABLE it_l_auth INDEX 2 INTO a2.
    READ TABLE it_l_auth INDEX 3 INTO a3. READ TABLE it_l_auth INDEX 4 INTO a4.
    READ TABLE it_l_auth INDEX 5 INTO a5. READ TABLE it_l_auth INDEX 6 INTO a6.
    READ TABLE it_l_auth INDEX 7 INTO a7. READ TABLE it_l_auth INDEX 8 INTO a8.
    READ TABLE it_l_auth INDEX 9 INTO a9. READ TABLE it_l_auth INDEX 10 INTO a10.
    CASE lines( it_l_auth ).
      WHEN 1.
        AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT v_i_object ID a1-field FIELD a1-val.
      WHEN 2.
        AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT v_i_object ID a1-field FIELD a1-val ID a2-field FIELD a2-val.
      WHEN 3.
        AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT v_i_object ID a1-field FIELD a1-val ID a2-field FIELD a2-val ID a3-field FIELD a3-val.
      WHEN 4.
        AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT v_i_object ID a1-field FIELD a1-val ID a2-field FIELD a2-val ID a3-field FIELD a3-val ID a4-field
         FIELD a4-val.
      WHEN 5.
        AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT v_i_object ID a1-field FIELD a1-val ID a2-field FIELD a2-val ID a3-field FIELD a3-val ID a4-field
         FIELD a4-val ID a5-field FIELD a5-val.
      WHEN 6.
        AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT v_i_object ID a1-field FIELD a1-val ID a2-field FIELD a2-val ID a3-field FIELD a3-val ID a4-field
         FIELD a4-val ID a5-field FIELD a5-val ID a6-field FIELD a6-val.
      WHEN 7.
        AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT v_i_object ID a1-field FIELD a1-val ID a2-field FIELD a2-val ID a3-field FIELD a3-val ID a4-field
         FIELD a4-val ID a5-field FIELD a5-val ID a6-field FIELD a6-val ID a7-field FIELD a7-val.
      WHEN 8.
        AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT v_i_object ID a1-field FIELD a1-val ID a2-field FIELD a2-val ID a3-field FIELD a3-val ID a4-field
         FIELD a4-val ID a5-field FIELD a5-val ID a6-field FIELD a6-val ID a7-field FIELD
      a7-val ID a8-field FIELD a8-val.
      WHEN 9.
        AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT v_i_object ID a1-field FIELD a1-val ID a2-field FIELD a2-val ID a3-field FIELD a3-val ID a4-field
         FIELD a4-val ID a5-field FIELD a5-val ID a6-field FIELD a6-val ID a7-field FIELD
      a7-val ID a8-field FIELD a8-val ID a9-field FIELD a9-val.
      WHEN 10.
        AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT v_i_object ID a1-field FIELD a1-val ID a2-field FIELD a2-val ID a3-field FIELD a3-val ID a4-field
         FIELD a4-val ID a5-field FIELD a5-val ID a6-field FIELD a6-val ID a7-field FIELD
      a7-val ID a8-field FIELD a8-val ID a9-field FIELD a9-val ID a10-field FIELD a10-val.
      WHEN OTHERS. RETURN.
    ENDCASE.
    IF sy-subrc = 0. v_r_ok = abap_true. ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo serialize: convertir registros en lineas tabuladas con escape de celdas.
*
  METHOD serialize.
    DATA: it_l_fields TYPE abap_component_tab, wa_l_field LIKE LINE OF it_l_fields,
      v_l_line TYPE string, v_l_cell TYPE string.
    FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_row> TYPE any.
    it_l_fields = fields( it_data ).
    LOOP AT it_l_fields INTO wa_l_field.
      IF sy-tabix = 1. v_l_line = wa_l_field-name.
      ELSE. CONCATENATE v_l_line wa_l_field-name INTO v_l_line
        SEPARATED BY cl_abap_char_utilities=>horizontal_tab. ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    APPEND v_l_line TO it_r_lines.
    LOOP AT it_data ASSIGNING <fs_row>.
      CLEAR v_l_line.
      LOOP AT it_l_fields INTO wa_l_field.
        v_l_cell = value( wa_i_row = <fs_row> v_i_field = CONV string( wa_l_field-name ) ).
        REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '"' IN v_l_cell WITH '""'.
        CONCATENATE '"' v_l_cell '"' INTO v_l_cell.
        IF sy-tabix = 1. v_l_line = v_l_cell.
        ELSE. CONCATENATE v_l_line v_l_cell INTO v_l_line
          SEPARATED BY cl_abap_char_utilities=>horizontal_tab. ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
      APPEND v_l_line TO it_r_lines.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo xml: escapar caracteres reservados para conservar el contenido en el archivo excel.
*
  METHOD xml.
    v_r_text = v_i_text.
    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '&' IN v_r_text WITH '&amp;'.
    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '<' IN v_r_text WITH '&lt;'.
    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '>' IN v_r_text WITH '&gt;'.
    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '"' IN v_r_text WITH '&quot;'.
    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline IN v_r_text WITH '&#10;'.
    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF cl_abap_char_utilities=>horizontal_tab IN v_r_text WITH '&#9;'.
    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF REGEX '[[:cntrl:]]' IN v_r_text WITH space.
  ENDMETHOD.
*
* @006 metodo save: guardar directamente en el pc mediante sap gui.
*

*
* @006 implementacion anterior en servidor, conservada solo como referencia.
*
* METHOD save.
*     DATA: v_l_line TYPE string, v_l_msg TYPE string, v_lx TYPE REF TO cx_root,
*       v_l_open TYPE abap_bool.
*     v_r_ok = abap_false.
*     TRY.
*       CALL FUNCTION 'AUTHORITY_CHECK_DATASET'
*         EXPORTING program = sy-repid activity = 'WRITE' filename = v_i_path
*         EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
*       IF sy-subrc <> 0.
*         log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'FILE' v_i_message = |Sin permiso escritura: { v_i_path }| ). RETURN.
*       ENDIF.
*       IF v_i_isbin = abap_true.
*         OPEN DATASET v_i_path FOR OUTPUT IN BINARY MODE MESSAGE v_l_msg.
*       ELSE.
*         OPEN DATASET v_i_path FOR OUTPUT IN TEXT MODE ENCODING UTF-8 MESSAGE v_l_msg.
*       ENDIF.
*       IF sy-subrc <> 0.
*         log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'FILE' v_i_message = v_l_msg ). RETURN.
*       ENDIF.
*       v_l_open = abap_true.
*       IF v_i_isbin = abap_true. TRANSFER v_i_binary TO v_i_path.
*       ELSE.
*         LOOP AT it_lines INTO v_l_line. TRANSFER v_l_line TO v_i_path. ENDLOOP.
*       ENDIF.
*       CLOSE DATASET v_i_path. v_l_open = abap_false. v_r_ok = abap_true.
*     CATCH cx_root INTO v_lx.
*       IF v_l_open = abap_true. CLOSE DATASET v_i_path. ENDIF.
*       v_l_msg = v_lx->get_text( ).
*       log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'FILE' v_i_message = v_l_msg ).
*     ENDTRY.
*   ENDMETHOD.
*
* @006 implementacion local para pc.
*
  METHOD save.
    DATA: v_l_binary TYPE xstring, it_l_binary TYPE solix_tab,
      v_l_size TYPE i, v_l_text TYPE string,
      v_lo_utf8 TYPE REF TO cl_abap_conv_out_ce,
      v_lx TYPE REF TO cx_root, v_l_rc TYPE sysubrc.
    v_r_ok = abap_false.
    IF sy-batch = abap_true OR v_i_path IS INITIAL.
      log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'PC'
        v_i_message = 'Salida local requiere SAP GUI y una ruta de archivo' ).
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    TRY.
      IF v_i_isbin = abap_true.
        v_l_binary = v_i_binary.
      ELSE.
        CONCATENATE LINES OF it_lines INTO v_l_text
          SEPARATED BY cl_abap_char_utilities=>cr_lf.
        IF it_lines IS NOT INITIAL.
          v_l_text = v_l_text && cl_abap_char_utilities=>cr_lf.
        ENDIF.
        v_lo_utf8 = cl_abap_conv_out_ce=>create( encoding = 'UTF-8' ).
        v_lo_utf8->write( data = v_l_text ).
        v_l_binary = v_lo_utf8->get_buffer( ).
      ENDIF.
      CALL FUNCTION 'SCMS_XSTRING_TO_BINARY'
        EXPORTING buffer = v_l_binary
        IMPORTING output_length = v_l_size
        TABLES binary_tab = it_l_binary
        EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'PC'
          v_i_message = 'No se pudo preparar el contenido para descarga local' ).
        RETURN.
      ENDIF.
      cl_gui_frontend_services=>gui_download(
        EXPORTING filename = v_i_path filetype = 'BIN'
          bin_filesize = v_l_size
        CHANGING data_tab = it_l_binary
        EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1 ).
      v_l_rc = sy-subrc.
      IF v_l_rc <> 0.
        log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'PC'
          v_i_message = |Fallo guardado local, codigo { v_l_rc }: { v_i_path }| ).
        RETURN.
      ENDIF.
      v_r_ok = abap_true.
    CATCH cx_root INTO v_lx.
      log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'PC' v_i_message = v_lx->get_text( ) ).
    ENDTRY.
  ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo log: registrar la incidencia de ejecucion y actualizar contadores de resultado.
*
 METHOD log.
  DATA: wa_l_log TYPE ty_log, wa_l_msg TYPE bal_s_msg,
   v_l_text TYPE string, v_l_detail TYPE abap_bool.
  wa_l_log-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_log-datum = sy-datum.
  wa_l_log-uzeit = sy-uzeit. wa_l_log-uname = sy-uname.
  wa_l_log-batch = v_g_batch. wa_l_log-aufnr = v_i_order.
  wa_l_log-entity = v_i_entity. wa_l_log-type = v_i_type.
  wa_l_log-message = v_i_message. APPEND wa_l_log TO it_g_log.
  IF v_i_type = 'E' OR v_i_type = 'A'. ADD 1 TO v_g_errors. v_g_failed = abap_true.
  ELSEIF v_i_type = 'W'. ADD 1 TO v_g_warnings. ENDIF.
  IF v_g_handle IS INITIAL. RETURN. ENDIF.
* Informacion tecnica repetitiva no se convierte en miles de mensajes SAP.
  IF v_i_type = 'I' OR v_i_type = 'S'.
   CASE v_i_entity.
    WHEN 'INICIO' OR 'RESUMEN' OR 'PAQUETE' OR 'CIERRE' OR 'ARCHIVO'
      OR 'RESULTADO_ORDEN' OR 'RESPONSABLE'.
    WHEN OTHERS. RETURN.
   ENDCASE.
  ENDIF.
  v_l_text = v_i_message.
  IF v_i_order IS NOT INITIAL. v_l_text = |Orden { v_i_order }: { v_i_message }|. ENDIF.
  v_l_detail = xsdbool( v_i_type = 'I' AND
   ( v_i_entity = 'RESULTADO_ORDEN' OR v_i_entity = 'RESPONSABLE' ) ).
  bal_message( v_i_type = v_i_type v_i_text = v_l_text v_i_detail = v_l_detail ).
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo bal_start: crear el log de aplicacion del paquete de extraccion.
*

 METHOD bal_message.
  DATA: wa_l_msg TYPE bal_s_msg, v_l_text TYPE string, v_l_piece TYPE c LENGTH 200,
   v_l_len TYPE i, v_l_next TYPE abap_bool.
  IF v_g_handle IS INITIAL. bal_start( ). ENDIF.
  v_l_text = v_i_text.
  REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF cl_abap_char_utilities=>horizontal_tab IN v_l_text WITH space.
  REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline IN v_l_text WITH space.
  DO.
   CLEAR: wa_l_msg, v_l_piece.
   IF v_l_next = abap_true. v_l_text = 'Continuacion: ' && v_l_text. ENDIF.
   v_l_len = strlen( v_l_text ).
   IF v_l_len > 200. v_l_piece = v_l_text(200). v_l_text = v_l_text+200.
   ELSE. v_l_piece = v_l_text. CLEAR v_l_text. ENDIF.
   wa_l_msg-msgty = v_i_type. wa_l_msg-msgid = 'ZSM_OS'. wa_l_msg-msgno = '000'.
   wa_l_msg-msgv1 = v_l_piece(50). wa_l_msg-msgv2 = v_l_piece+50(50).
   wa_l_msg-msgv3 = v_l_piece+100(50). wa_l_msg-msgv4 = v_l_piece+150(50).
   IF v_i_detail = abap_true. wa_l_msg-detlevel = '1'.
   ELSE. wa_l_msg-detlevel = '0'. ENDIF.
   CASE v_i_type.
    WHEN 'E' OR 'A' OR 'X'. wa_l_msg-probclass = '1'.
    WHEN 'W'. wa_l_msg-probclass = '2'.
    WHEN OTHERS. wa_l_msg-probclass = '4'.
   ENDCASE.
   CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_LOG_MSG_ADD'
    EXPORTING i_log_handle = v_g_handle i_s_msg = wa_l_msg EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. v_g_failed = abap_true. EXIT. ENDIF.
   IF v_l_text IS INITIAL. EXIT. ENDIF.
   v_l_next = abap_true.
  ENDDO.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD bal_start.
  DATA wa_l_log TYPE bal_s_log.
  wa_l_log-object = 'ZSM_OS'. wa_l_log-subobject = 'EXTRACCION'.
  wa_l_log-extnumber = |{ v_g_run }/{ v_g_batch }|.
  wa_l_log-aluser = sy-uname. wa_l_log-alprog = sy-repid.
  wa_l_log-aldate = sy-datum. wa_l_log-altime = sy-uzeit.
  CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_LOG_CREATE'
   EXPORTING i_s_log = wa_l_log IMPORTING e_log_handle = v_g_handle
   EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE e003(zsm_os).
  ENDIF.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo bal_save: persistir el log y liberar sus datos de memoria.
*
 METHOD bal_save.
  DATA it_l_handles TYPE bal_t_logh.
  IF v_g_handle IS INITIAL. RETURN. ENDIF.
  APPEND v_g_handle TO it_l_handles.
  CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_DB_SAVE' EXPORTING i_t_log_handle = it_l_handles
   EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. v_g_failed = abap_true.
     MESSAGE e004(zsm_os).
  ENDIF.
  COMMIT WORK AND WAIT.
  CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_LOG_REFRESH' EXPORTING i_log_handle = v_g_handle
   EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  CLEAR v_g_handle.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @006 metodo path: componer el nombre dentro de la carpeta local seleccionada.
*

*
* @006 implementacion anterior en servidor, conservada solo como referencia.
*
* METHOD path.
*   DATA v_l_leaf TYPE string.
*   v_l_leaf = |{ v_g_run }_{ v_g_batch }_{ v_i_entity }.{ v_i_ext }|.
*   CALL FUNCTION 'FILE_GET_NAME'
*    EXPORTING logical_filename = p_lfile parameter_1 = v_l_leaf
*    IMPORTING file_name = v_r_path
*    EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
*   IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR v_r_path IS INITIAL OR v_r_path CS '..'.
*    v_g_failed = abap_true. MESSAGE e005(zsm_os) WITH p_lfile.
*   ENDIF.
*  ENDMETHOD.
*
* @006 implementacion local para pc.
*
 METHOD path.
  DATA: v_l_leaf TYPE string, v_l_offset TYPE i.
  IF v_g_pc_dir IS INITIAL OR sy-batch = abap_true.
    v_g_failed = abap_true.
    MESSAGE 'Seleccione una carpeta del PC; esta version solo funciona en dialogo' TYPE 'E'.
  ENDIF.
  v_l_leaf = |{ v_g_run }_{ v_g_batch }_{ v_i_entity }.{ v_i_ext }|.
  v_l_offset = strlen( v_g_pc_dir ) - 1.
  IF v_g_pc_dir+v_l_offset(1) = '/' OR v_g_pc_dir+v_l_offset(1) = '\'.
    v_r_path = v_g_pc_dir && v_l_leaf.
  ELSE.
    v_r_path = v_g_pc_dir && '/' && v_l_leaf.
  ENDIF.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo emit: generar un archivo e incorporarlo al manifiesto de la ejecucion.
*

* Evidencia historica en BAL, sin archivos TXT auxiliares en el PC.
 METHOD evidence_bal.
  DATA: v_l_label TYPE string, wa_l_valid TYPE ty_valida,
   v_l_errors TYPE i, v_l_warnings TYPE i.
* Los TXT internos ya no se descargan ni se vuelcan como filas fragmentadas en BAL.
* Se conservan sus lecturas/calculos; aqui se registran resultados comprensibles.
  CASE v_i_entity.
   WHEN 'CONFIRMACIONES'. v_l_label = 'Confirmaciones revisadas'.
   WHEN 'CONTROL_TRABAJO'. v_l_label = 'Registros de control de trabajo revisados'.
   WHEN 'ESTADOS_DESCRITOS'. v_l_label = 'Estados de orden interpretados'.
   WHEN 'CLASIFICACION'. v_l_label = 'Registros de clasificacion recuperados'.
   WHEN 'VALIDACION_PLANTILLA'.
    LOOP AT it_g_validas INTO wa_l_valid.
     IF wa_l_valid-tipo = 'E'. ADD 1 TO v_l_errors.
     ELSEIF wa_l_valid-tipo = 'W'. ADD 1 TO v_l_warnings.
     ELSE. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
* Tipo BAL original; no altera el control de extraccion que ya resolvio VAL_ERR.
     bal_message( v_i_type = wa_l_valid-tipo
      v_i_text = |Validacion orden { wa_l_valid-aufnr }, hoja { wa_l_valid-hoja }, posicion { wa_l_valid-posicion }, cam| &&
       |po { wa_l_valid-campo }: { wa_l_valid-mensaje }| ).
    ENDLOOP.
    log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'RESUMEN'
     v_i_message = |Paquete { v_g_batch }: incidencias de validacion, errores { v_l_errors }, avisos { v_l_warnings }| ).
    RETURN.
   WHEN OTHERS. RETURN.
  ENDCASE.
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'RESUMEN'
   v_i_message = |Paquete { v_g_batch }: { v_l_label }: { v_i_records }| ).
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD emit.
  DATA: wa_l_file TYPE ty_file, v_l_ok TYPE abap_bool, v_l_ext TYPE string.
  IF v_i_kind = 'TXT'.
   evidence_bal( v_i_entity = v_i_entity v_i_records = v_i_records it_lines = it_lines ).
   RETURN.
  ENDIF.
  v_l_ext = v_i_kind. TRANSLATE v_l_ext TO LOWER CASE.
  wa_l_file-batch = v_g_batch. wa_l_file-kind = v_i_kind.
  wa_l_file-entity = v_i_entity. wa_l_file-records = v_i_records.
  wa_l_file-path = path( v_i_entity = v_i_entity v_i_ext = v_l_ext ).
  v_l_ok = save( v_i_path = wa_l_file-path v_i_binary = v_i_binary
   it_lines = it_lines v_i_isbin = xsdbool( v_i_kind <> 'TXT' ) ).
  IF v_l_ok = abap_true. wa_l_file-state = 'OK'.
  ELSE. wa_l_file-state = 'ERROR'. v_g_failed = abap_true. ENDIF.
  APPEND wa_l_file TO it_g_files.
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'ARCHIVO'
   v_i_message = |{ wa_l_file-state } { wa_l_file-path }| ).
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD authorized.

*****  ENDIF.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo template: recuperar y verificar la plantilla del repositorio de objetos binarios.
*
 METHOD template.
  DATA: wa_l_key TYPE wwwdatatab, it_l_mime TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF w3mime,
   v_l_size TYPE i, v_l_param TYPE wwwparams-value, v_lo_zip TYPE REF TO cl_abap_zip,
   v_l_xml TYPE xstring, v_l_text TYPE string.
  wa_l_key-relid = 'MI'. wa_l_key-objid = p_mime.
  CALL FUNCTION 'WWWDATA_IMPORT' EXPORTING key = wa_l_key
   TABLES mime = it_l_mime EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR it_l_mime IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e006(zsm_os) WITH p_mime.
 ENDIF.
      CALL FUNCTION 'WWWPARAMS_READ'
   EXPORTING relid = 'MI' objid = p_mime name = 'filesize'
   IMPORTING value = v_l_param EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR v_l_param IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e007(zsm_os).
  ENDIF.
  v_l_size = v_l_param.
  CALL FUNCTION 'SCMS_BINARY_TO_XSTRING'
   EXPORTING input_length = v_l_size IMPORTING buffer = v_g_template
   TABLES binary_tab = it_l_mime EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. MESSAGE e007(zsm_os). ENDIF.
  CREATE OBJECT v_lo_zip.
  v_lo_zip->load( EXPORTING zip = v_g_template EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1 ).
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. MESSAGE e007(zsm_os). ENDIF.
  v_lo_zip->get( EXPORTING name = 'xl/workbook.xml'
   IMPORTING content = v_l_xml EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1 ).
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. MESSAGE e007(zsm_os). ENDIF.
  v_l_text = cl_abap_codepage=>convert_from( source = v_l_xml ).
* @007 validar version y cabecera real; no depender de mayusculas del titulo.
  v_lo_zip->get( EXPORTING name = 'xl/sharedStrings.xml'
   IMPORTING content = v_l_xml EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1 ).
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. MESSAGE e008(zsm_os). ENDIF.
  v_l_text = cl_abap_codepage=>convert_from( source = v_l_xml ).
  IF v_l_text NS 'SAP S/4HANA 2025'.
   MESSAGE 'Actualice ZSM_SRVO_TEMPLATE en SMW0 con la plantilla oficial 2025' TYPE 'E'.
  ENDIF.
  DATA: v_l_sheetno TYPE i, v_l_path TYPE string.
  DO 10 TIMES.
   v_l_sheetno = sy-index + 2.
   v_l_path = |xl/worksheets/sheet{ v_l_sheetno }.xml|.
   v_lo_zip->get( EXPORTING name = v_l_path
    IMPORTING content = v_l_xml EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1 ).
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. MESSAGE e008(zsm_os). ENDIF.
   v_l_text = cl_abap_codepage=>convert_from( source = v_l_xml ).
   IF v_l_text NS '<row r="5"' OR v_l_text NS '<row r="8"'.
    MESSAGE e008(zsm_os).
   ENDIF.
  ENDDO.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo iso: obtener la representacion iso del idioma sap sin homologacion de negocio.
*
 METHOD iso.
  DATA v_l_iso TYPE t002-laiso.
  CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_ISOLA_OUTPUT'
   EXPORTING input = v_i_language IMPORTING output = v_l_iso
   EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  IF sy-subrc = 0. v_r_iso = v_l_iso. ENDIF.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo sheets_init: preparar las hojas y columnas de la plantilla recibida.
*
 METHOD bapi_operations.
  DATA: it_l_return TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapiret2,
   wa_l_return TYPE bapiret2, v_l_msg TYPE string,
   v_l_type TYPE bapiret2-type.
  CLEAR: it_e_operations, v_e_ok.
  CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_ALM_ORDER_GET_DETAIL'
   EXPORTING number = v_i_order
   TABLES et_operations = it_e_operations return = it_l_return
   EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
   log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'BAPI_OPS' v_i_order = v_i_order
    v_i_message = 'Fallo de llamada BAPI_ALM_ORDER_GET_DETAIL; no se usa fallback silencioso' ).
   RETURN.
  ENDIF.
  v_e_ok = abap_true.
  LOOP AT it_l_return INTO wa_l_return.
   v_l_type = wa_l_return-type. v_l_msg = wa_l_return-message.
   IF v_l_msg IS INITIAL.
    v_l_msg = |{ wa_l_return-id }/{ wa_l_return-number } { wa_l_return-message_v1 } { wa_l_return-message_v2 }|.
   ENDIF.
   IF v_l_type = 'A' OR v_l_type = 'X' OR v_l_type = 'E'.
    v_e_ok = abap_false. v_l_type = 'E'.
   ELSEIF v_l_type <> 'W' AND v_l_type <> 'I' AND v_l_type <> 'S'.
    v_l_type = 'I'.
   ENDIF.
   log( v_i_type = v_l_type v_i_entity = 'BAPI_OPS'
    v_i_order = v_i_order v_i_message = v_l_msg ).
  ENDLOOP.
  IF v_e_ok = abap_false. CLEAR it_e_operations. RETURN. ENDIF.
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'BAPI_OPS' v_i_order = v_i_order
   v_i_message = |Operaciones BAPI recuperadas: { lines( it_e_operations ) }| ).
 ENDMETHOD.


* @014 ampliar fuentes sin alterar seleccion ni numeracion de posiciones.

 METHOD wip_read.
  DATA: it_l_keys TYPE ty_keys, wa_l_key TYPE ty_key, v_l_where TYPE string,
   v_l_value TYPE string, v_l_limit TYPE i, v_lx TYPE REF TO cx_root,
   wa_l_dfies TYPE dfies, it_l_dfies TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF dfies.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_parent> TYPE any, <fs_rows> TYPE STANDARD TABLE.
  v_g_wread_ok = abap_false.
  TRY.
   CREATE DATA v_rr_data TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF (v_i_table).
   ASSIGN v_rr_data->* TO <fs_rows>.
   IF it_parent IS INITIAL. v_g_wread_ok = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
   CALL FUNCTION 'DDIF_FIELDINFO_GET'
    EXPORTING tabname = v_i_table langu = sy-langu
    TABLES dfies_tab = it_l_dfies EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. RETURN. ENDIF.
   READ TABLE it_l_dfies INTO wa_l_dfies WITH KEY fieldname = v_i_target.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. RETURN. ENDIF.
   LOOP AT it_parent ASSIGNING <fs_parent>.
    v_l_value = value( wa_i_row = <fs_parent> v_i_field = v_i_source ).
    IF v_l_value IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    wa_l_key-value = v_l_value. INSERT wa_l_key INTO TABLE it_l_keys.
   ENDLOOP.
   IF it_l_keys IS INITIAL. v_g_wread_ok = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
* Lectura por grupos de claves acotados para no construir una sentencia enorme.
   DATA: v_l_idx TYPE i, v_l_count TYPE i, v_lr_part TYPE REF TO data.
   FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_part> TYPE STANDARD TABLE.
   CREATE DATA v_lr_part TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF (v_i_table).
   ASSIGN v_lr_part->* TO <fs_part>.
   LOOP AT it_l_keys INTO wa_l_key.
    IF v_l_count = 0. v_l_where = |{ v_i_target } IN (|.
    ELSE. v_l_where = v_l_where && ','. ENDIF.
    v_l_where = v_l_where && literal( wa_l_key-value ).
    ADD 1 TO v_l_count. ADD 1 TO v_l_idx.
    IF v_l_count < 100 AND v_l_idx < lines( it_l_keys ). CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    v_l_where = v_l_where && ')'.
    IF v_i_extra IS NOT INITIAL. v_l_where = v_l_where && ' AND (' && v_i_extra && ')'. ENDIF.
    v_l_limit = p_det + 1 - lines( <fs_rows> ).
    IF v_l_limit <= 0. RETURN. ENDIF.
    CLEAR <fs_part>.
    SELECT * FROM (v_i_table) INTO TABLE <fs_part> UP TO v_l_limit ROWS WHERE (v_l_where).
    APPEND LINES OF <fs_part> TO <fs_rows>.
    IF lines( <fs_rows> ) > p_det.
     log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'WIP_LECTURA'
      v_i_message = |{ v_i_table } supera P_DET; consulta WIP queda incompleta, no se presume ausencia| ).
     RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR: v_l_count, v_l_where.
   ENDLOOP.
   v_g_wread_ok = abap_true.
  CATCH cx_root INTO v_lx.
   log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'WIP_LECTURA'
    v_i_message = |No se completo { v_i_table }: { v_lx->get_text( ) }| ).
  ENDTRY.
 ENDMETHOD.


 METHOD wip_decide.
  DATA: v_l_flags TYPE string, v_l_approval TYPE string, v_l_key TYPE string.
  CLEAR: wa_c_wip-escenario, wa_c_wip-alerta.
  wa_c_wip-decision = 'R'. wa_c_wip-motivo = 'DATOS_PENDIENTES'.
  IF wa_c_wip-x1 = 'X'.
   wa_c_wip-escenario = 'X'. wa_c_wip-decision = 'N'. wa_c_wip-motivo = 'X1_BORRADO'.
   IF wa_c_wip-f2 = 'X' OR wa_c_wip-f3 = 'X' OR wa_c_wip-f6 = 'X'.
    wa_c_wip-alerta = 'X'.
   ELSEIF wa_c_wip-f2 = 'U' OR wa_c_wip-f3 = 'U' OR wa_c_wip-f6 = 'U'.
    wa_c_wip-alerta = 'U'.
   ENDIF.
   RETURN.
  ENDIF.
  IF wa_c_wip-f1 IS INITIAL.
   IF wa_c_wip-f3 = 'X'. wa_c_wip-escenario = 'D'.
   ELSEIF wa_c_wip-f3 = 'U'. RETURN.
   ELSEIF wa_c_wip-f2 = 'X' OR wa_c_wip-f6 = 'X'.
    IF wa_c_wip-s_clsd = 'X'. wa_c_wip-escenario = 'G'.
    ELSE. wa_c_wip-escenario = 'A'. ENDIF.
   ELSEIF wa_c_wip-f2 = 'U' OR wa_c_wip-f6 = 'U'. RETURN.
   ELSEIF wa_c_wip-f7 = 'X' OR wa_c_wip-f8 = 'X'. wa_c_wip-escenario = 'F'.
   ELSEIF wa_c_wip-f7 = 'U' OR wa_c_wip-f8 = 'U'. RETURN.
   ELSE. wa_c_wip-escenario = 'C'. ENDIF.
  ELSE.
   v_l_flags = wa_c_wip-f2 && wa_c_wip-f3 && wa_c_wip-f4 && wa_c_wip-f5 && wa_c_wip-f6.
   IF v_l_flags CS 'X'. wa_c_wip-escenario = 'B'.
   ELSEIF v_l_flags CS 'U'. RETURN.
   ELSEIF wa_c_wip-f7 = 'X' OR wa_c_wip-f8 = 'X'. wa_c_wip-escenario = 'F'.
   ELSEIF wa_c_wip-f7 = 'U' OR wa_c_wip-f8 = 'U'. RETURN.
   ELSEIF wa_c_wip-f9 = 'X'. wa_c_wip-escenario = 'E'.
   ELSEIF wa_c_wip-f9 = 'U'. RETURN.
   ELSE. wa_c_wip-escenario = 'H'. ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
  CASE wa_c_wip-escenario.
   WHEN 'A'. wa_c_wip-motivo = 'A_TECO_SALDO'.
   WHEN 'B'. wa_c_wip-motivo = 'B_ABIERTA_PEND'.
   WHEN 'C'. wa_c_wip-motivo = 'C_CERRADA_OK'.
   WHEN 'D'. wa_c_wip-motivo = 'D_FACT_PEND'.
   WHEN 'E'. wa_c_wip-motivo = 'E_VACIA'.
   WHEN 'F'. wa_c_wip-motivo = 'F_CONTRATO'.
   WHEN 'G'. wa_c_wip-motivo = 'G_CLSD_SALDO'.
   WHEN 'H'. wa_c_wip-motivo = 'H_ABIERTA_SIN_PEND'.
  ENDCASE.
  IF wa_c_wip-escenario = 'G' OR wa_c_wip-escenario = 'H'. RETURN. ENDIF.
  IF wa_c_wip-escenario = 'C'. wa_c_wip-decision = 'N'. RETURN. ENDIF.
* Puede conocerse escenario B/D con un pendiente cierto, pero no autorizar
* migracion mientras existan otros indicadores financieros/comerciales U.
  v_l_flags = wa_c_wip-f2 && wa_c_wip-f3 && wa_c_wip-f4 && wa_c_wip-f5 &&
   wa_c_wip-f6 && wa_c_wip-f7 && wa_c_wip-f8 && wa_c_wip-f9.
  IF v_l_flags CS 'U'. wa_c_wip-motivo = 'DATOS_PENDIENTES'. RETURN. ENDIF.
  wa_c_wip-decision = 'R'. wa_c_wip-motivo = 'APROBACION_PENDIENTE'.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD wip_prepare.
  DATA: it_l_order TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF aufk, wa_l_order TYPE aufk,
   it_l_jest TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF jest, wa_l_jest TYPE jest,
   it_l_afko TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afko, wa_l_afko TYPE afko,
   it_l_afvc TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afvc, wa_l_afvc TYPE afvc,
   it_l_ojest TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF jest,
   it_l_resb TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF resb, wa_l_resb TYPE resb,
   it_l_afru TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afru,
   wa_l_wip TYPE ty_wip,
   wa_l_pending TYPE ty_op_pending, v_lr TYPE REF TO data,
   v_l_deleted TYPE abap_bool, v_l_cnf TYPE abap_bool,
   v_l_extra TYPE string, v_l_table TYPE tabname,
   v_l_versn TYPE string, v_l_wrttp TYPE string,
   v_l_field TYPE string, v_l_idx TYPE n LENGTH 3,
   v_l_num TYPE p LENGTH 16 DECIMALS 2,
   v_l_count TYPE i, v_l_unknown TYPE abap_bool,
   v_lx TYPE REF TO cx_root, v_l_msg TYPE string.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_tab> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_cost> TYPE any,
   <fs_amount> TYPE any, <fs_wip> TYPE ty_wip.
  CLEAR: it_g_wip, it_g_pending, v_g_wread_ok.
  it_l_order = it_orders.
  IF it_l_order IS INITIAL. RETURN. ENDIF.
* Dejar evidencia incluso si una lectura secundaria interrumpe el paquete.
  LOOP AT it_l_order INTO wa_l_order.
   CLEAR wa_l_wip. MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_l_order TO wa_l_wip.
   wa_l_wip-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_wip-batch = v_g_batch.
   wa_l_wip-fecha_corte = p_corte. wa_l_wip-simul = p_simul.
   wa_l_wip-s_teco = 'U'. wa_l_wip-s_clsd = 'U'. wa_l_wip-x1 = 'U'.
   wa_l_wip-f1 = 'U'. wa_l_wip-f2 = 'U'. wa_l_wip-f3 = 'U'.
   wa_l_wip-f4 = 'U'. wa_l_wip-f5 = 'U'. wa_l_wip-f6 = 'U'.
   wa_l_wip-f7 = 'U'. wa_l_wip-f8 = 'U'. wa_l_wip-f9 = 'U'.
   wa_l_wip-decision = 'R'. wa_l_wip-motivo = 'NO_EVALUADA'.
   wa_l_wip-detalle = 'Lecturas de clasificacion no completadas'.
   APPEND wa_l_wip TO it_g_wip.
  ENDLOOP.
  v_lr = wip_read( v_i_table = 'JEST' it_parent = it_l_order
   v_i_source = 'OBJNR' v_i_target = 'OBJNR' v_i_extra = |INACT = ' '| ).
  IF v_g_wread_ok = abap_false. RETURN. ENDIF.
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_tab>.
  it_l_jest = <fs_tab>.
  v_lr = wip_read( v_i_table = 'AFKO' it_parent = it_l_order
   v_i_source = 'AUFNR' v_i_target = 'AUFNR' ).
  IF v_g_wread_ok = abap_false. RETURN. ENDIF.
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_tab>.
  it_l_afko = <fs_tab>.
  v_lr = wip_read( v_i_table = 'AFVC' it_parent = it_l_afko
   v_i_source = 'AUFPL' v_i_target = 'AUFPL' ).
  IF v_g_wread_ok = abap_false. RETURN. ENDIF.
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_tab>.
  it_l_afvc = <fs_tab>.
  v_lr = wip_read( v_i_table = 'JEST' it_parent = it_l_afvc
   v_i_source = 'OBJNR' v_i_target = 'OBJNR' v_i_extra = |INACT = ' '| ).
  IF v_g_wread_ok = abap_false. RETURN. ENDIF.
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_tab>.
  it_l_ojest = <fs_tab>.
  v_lr = wip_read( v_i_table = 'RESB' it_parent = it_l_order
   v_i_source = 'AUFNR' v_i_target = 'AUFNR' ).
  IF v_g_wread_ok = abap_false. RETURN. ENDIF.
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_tab>.
  it_l_resb = <fs_tab>.
  v_lr = wip_read( v_i_table = 'AFRU' it_parent = it_l_order
   v_i_source = 'AUFNR' v_i_target = 'AUFNR' ).
  IF v_g_wread_ok = abap_false. RETURN. ENDIF.
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_tab>.
  it_l_afru = <fs_tab>.
  CLEAR it_g_wip.
  LOOP AT it_l_order INTO wa_l_order.
   CLEAR wa_l_wip. MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_l_order TO wa_l_wip.
   wa_l_wip-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_wip-batch = v_g_batch.
   wa_l_wip-fecha_corte = p_corte. wa_l_wip-simul = p_simul.
   wa_l_wip-f2 = 'U'. wa_l_wip-f3 = 'U'. wa_l_wip-f6 = 'U'.
   wa_l_wip-f7 = 'U'. wa_l_wip-f8 = 'U'.
   IF wa_l_order-loekz IS NOT INITIAL. wa_l_wip-x1 = 'X'. ENDIF.
   LOOP AT it_l_jest INTO wa_l_jest WHERE objnr = wa_l_order-objnr.
    CASE wa_l_jest-stat.
     WHEN 'I0045'. wa_l_wip-s_teco = 'X'.
     WHEN 'I0046'. wa_l_wip-s_clsd = 'X'.
     WHEN 'I0076' OR 'I0013'. wa_l_wip-x1 = 'X'.
    ENDCASE.
   ENDLOOP.
   IF wa_l_wip-s_teco IS INITIAL AND wa_l_wip-s_clsd IS INITIAL. wa_l_wip-f1 = 'X'. ENDIF.
   LOOP AT it_l_afko INTO wa_l_afko WHERE aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr.
    LOOP AT it_l_afvc INTO wa_l_afvc WHERE aufpl = wa_l_afko-aufpl.
     CLEAR: v_l_deleted, v_l_cnf, wa_l_pending.
     LOOP AT it_l_ojest INTO wa_l_jest WHERE objnr = wa_l_afvc-objnr.
      IF wa_l_jest-stat = 'I0009'. v_l_cnf = abap_true. ENDIF.
      IF wa_l_jest-stat = 'I0076' OR wa_l_jest-stat = 'I0013'. v_l_deleted = abap_true. ENDIF.
     ENDLOOP.
     wa_l_pending-aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr.
     wa_l_pending-vornr = wa_l_afvc-vornr. wa_l_pending-objnr = wa_l_afvc-objnr.
     IF wa_l_afvc-objnr IS INITIAL.
      wa_l_pending-pendiente = 'U'.
      IF wa_l_wip-f1 = 'X' AND wa_l_wip-f4 IS INITIAL. wa_l_wip-f4 = 'U'. ENDIF.
     ELSEIF v_l_cnf = abap_false AND v_l_deleted = abap_false.
      wa_l_pending-pendiente = 'X'.
      IF wa_l_wip-f1 = 'X'. wa_l_wip-f4 = 'X'. ENDIF.
     ENDIF.
     APPEND wa_l_pending TO it_g_pending.
    ENDLOOP.
   ENDLOOP.
* F4 segun el anexo; su interpretacion funcional queda pendiente de validacion.

   IF wa_l_wip-f1 = 'X'.
    LOOP AT it_l_resb INTO wa_l_resb WHERE aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr
     AND xloek = space AND kzear = space.
     IF wa_l_resb-bdmng > wa_l_resb-enmng. wa_l_wip-f5 = 'X'. EXIT. ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
   ENDIF.
   SELECT SINGLE waers INTO wa_l_wip-moneda FROM tka01 WHERE kokrs = wa_l_order-kokrs.
* Fuentes financieras/comerciales no verificadas: U, nunca evidencia manual.
   wa_l_wip-detalle = `Consulta parcial: F2/F3/F6/F7/F8 pendientes. F4/F5/F9 calculados segun regla del anexo` &&
    `; sin homologaciones ni decision de carga` .
* F9 literal del anexo: ausencia de AFRU; anulaciones requieren regla aparte.
   wa_l_wip-f9 = 'X'.
   READ TABLE it_l_afru TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr.
   IF sy-subrc = 0. CLEAR wa_l_wip-f9. ENDIF.
   LOOP AT it_l_resb INTO wa_l_resb WHERE aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr.
    IF wa_l_resb-enmng > 0. CLEAR wa_l_wip-f9. EXIT. ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
   DATA v_l_objnr TYPE coep-objnr.
   CLEAR v_l_objnr.
   TRY.
    SELECT SINGLE objnr INTO v_l_objnr FROM coep WHERE objnr = wa_l_order-objnr.
    IF sy-subrc = 0. CLEAR wa_l_wip-f9. ENDIF.
   CATCH cx_root INTO v_lx.
    wa_l_wip-f9 = 'U'.
    wa_l_wip-detalle = wa_l_wip-detalle && ' No se completo consulta COEP'.
   ENDTRY.
   IF wa_l_wip-f3 = 'X' OR wa_l_wip-f4 = 'X' OR wa_l_wip-f5 = 'X'. CLEAR wa_l_wip-f9.
   ELSEIF wa_l_wip-f9 = 'X' AND ( wa_l_wip-f3 = 'U' OR wa_l_wip-f4 = 'U' ). wa_l_wip-f9 = 'U'. ENDIF.
   APPEND wa_l_wip TO it_g_wip.
  ENDLOOP.
  wip_ampliar( it_orders = it_l_order ).
* los indicadores no comprobables permanecen U; no se inventa contenido funcional.
  LOOP AT it_g_wip ASSIGNING <fs_wip>.
   READ TABLE it_l_order INTO wa_l_order WITH KEY aufnr = <fs_wip>-aufnr.
   IF sy-subrc = 0 AND wa_l_order-autyp <> '30'.
    <fs_wip>-decision = 'N'. <fs_wip>-motivo = 'FUERA_CATEGORIA_30'.
    CONTINUE.
   ENDIF.
   wip_decide( CHANGING wa_c_wip = <fs_wip> ).
   IF <fs_wip>-motivo = 'DATOS_PENDIENTES'.
    v_l_extra = ''.
    DO 9 TIMES.
     v_l_field = |F{ sy-index }|.
     ASSIGN COMPONENT v_l_field OF STRUCTURE <fs_wip> TO <fs_amount>.
     IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <fs_amount> = 'U'.
      IF v_l_extra IS NOT INITIAL. v_l_extra = v_l_extra && ','. ENDIF.
      v_l_extra = v_l_extra && v_l_field.
     ENDIF.
    ENDDO.
    <fs_wip>-detalle = <fs_wip>-detalle && | Indicadores indeterminados: { v_l_extra }.|.
   ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
 ENDMETHOD.


 METHOD wip_save.
  DATA: wa_l_eval TYPE ty_eval, wa_l_wip TYPE ty_wip,
   it_l_lines TYPE ty_strings, v_l_binary TYPE xstring,
   v_l_n TYPE i, v_l_r TYPE i.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_wip> TYPE ty_wip.
  LOOP AT it_g_wip ASSIGNING <fs_wip>.
   READ TABLE it_g_eval INTO wa_l_eval WITH KEY aufnr = <fs_wip>-aufnr.
   IF sy-subrc = 0.
    <fs_wip>-extraccion_actual = wa_l_eval-estado.
    <fs_wip>-motivo_extraccion = wa_l_eval-motivo.
   ENDIF.
   IF <fs_wip>-decision = 'N'. ADD 1 TO v_l_n. ELSE. ADD 1 TO v_l_r. ENDIF.
   IF lines( it_g_wip_view ) < p_prev. APPEND <fs_wip> TO it_g_wip_view. ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  it_l_lines = serialize( it_g_wip ).
  emit( v_i_entity = 'WIP_CONSULTA' v_i_kind = 'TXT'
   v_i_records = lines( it_g_wip ) v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
  ADD v_l_n TO v_g_wip_n. ADD v_l_r TO v_g_wip_r.
  IF it_g_fuentes IS NOT INITIAL.
   it_l_lines = serialize( it_g_fuentes ).
   emit( v_i_entity = 'WIP_FUENTES' v_i_kind = 'TXT'
    v_i_records = lines( it_g_fuentes ) v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
  ENDIF.
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'WIP_CONSULTA'
   v_i_message = |Consulta parcial { lines( it_g_wip ) } = N { v_l_n } + R { v_l_r }. No decide la descarga actual; M no| &&
    | se autoriza sin definiciones| ).
 ENDMETHOD.


 METHOD wip_show.
  DATA: v_lo_alv TYPE REF TO cl_salv_table, v_lx TYPE REF TO cx_salv_msg,
   lo_column TYPE REF TO cl_salv_column, it_l_labels TYPE ty_strings,
   v_l_labels TYPE string, v_l_item TYPE string, v_l_field TYPE lvc_fname,
   v_l_caption TYPE string, v_l_short TYPE scrtext_s,
   v_l_medium TYPE scrtext_m, v_l_long TYPE scrtext_l.
  IF it_g_wip_view IS INITIAL.
   MESSAGE 'Sin muestra WIP; revisar seleccion y Detalle SLG1' TYPE 'S'. RETURN.
  ENDIF.
  TRY.
   cl_salv_table=>factory( IMPORTING r_salv_table = v_lo_alv CHANGING t_table = it_g_wip_view ).
   v_lo_alv->get_functions( )->set_all( abap_true ).
   v_l_labels = `RUNID=Identificacion de ejecucion|BATCH=Paquete|AUFNR=Orden ECC|AUART=Clase de orden|W` &&
    `ERKS=Centro|BUKRS=Sociedad|OBJNR=Objeto ECC|S_TECO=Cierre tecnico activo|S_CLSD=Cierre` &&
    ` comercial activo|X1=Marcada para borrado|F1=F1 Sin cierre|F2=F2 Saldo de costos reale` &&
    `s|F3=F3 Facturacion pendiente|F4=F4 Operaciones pendientes|F5=F5 Componentes pendiente` &&
    `s|F6=F6 Saldo WIP|F7=F7 Liquidacion a terceros|F8=F8 Contrato o garantia vigente|F9=F9` &&
    ` Sin movimientos|SALDO_COSTOS=Saldo costos moneda CO|SALDO_WIP=Saldo WIP determinado|M` &&
    `ONEDA=Moneda de los saldos|FECHA_CORTE=Fecha de corte|SIMUL=Modo simulado|ESCENARIO=Es` &&
    `cenario WIP|DECISION=Decision WIP M N R|MOTIVO=Motivo de decision WIP|ALERTA=Alerta WI` &&
    `P|DETALLE=Explicacion y pendientes WIP|EXTRACCION_ACTUAL=Filtro tecnico A N R|MOTIVO_E` &&
    `XTRACCION=Motivo del filtro de extraccion` .
   SPLIT v_l_labels AT '|' INTO TABLE it_l_labels.
   LOOP AT it_l_labels INTO v_l_item.
    SPLIT v_l_item AT '=' INTO v_l_field v_l_caption.
    TRY.
     lo_column = v_lo_alv->get_columns( )->get_column( v_l_field ).
     v_l_short = v_l_caption. v_l_medium = v_l_caption. v_l_long = v_l_caption.
     lo_column->set_short_text( v_l_short ).
     lo_column->set_medium_text( v_l_medium ).
     lo_column->set_long_text( v_l_long ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.
* Campos ausentes en una variante no alteran la consulta.
    ENDTRY.
   ENDLOOP.
   v_lo_alv->get_columns( )->set_optimize( abap_true ).
   v_lo_alv->get_display_settings( )->set_list_header(
    'WIP consulta: X=Si; vacio=No/no aplica; U=Indeterminado; R=Revisar' ).
   v_lo_alv->display( ).
  CATCH cx_salv_msg INTO v_lx.
   MESSAGE v_lx->get_text( ) TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
  ENDTRY.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD wip_fields.
  CALL FUNCTION 'DDIF_FIELDINFO_GET'
   EXPORTING tabname = v_i_table langu = sy-langu
   TABLES dfies_tab = it_r_fields EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. CLEAR it_r_fields. ENDIF.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD wip_source.
  DATA wa_l_source TYPE ty_fuente_wip.
  IF lines( it_g_fuentes ) >= p_det.
   IF v_g_fuentes_limite = abap_false.
    v_g_fuentes_limite = abap_true.
    log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'WIP_FUENTES'
     v_i_message = 'Evidencias alcanzan P_DET; archivo de fuentes incompleto. No usarlo como conciliacion total' ).
   ENDIF.
   RETURN.
  ENDIF.
  wa_l_source-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_source-batch = v_g_batch.
  wa_l_source-aufnr = v_i_order. wa_l_source-indicador = v_i_indicator.
  wa_l_source-tabla = v_i_table. wa_l_source-clave = v_i_key.
  wa_l_source-valor = v_i_value. wa_l_source-resultado = v_i_result.
  wa_l_source-detalle = v_i_detail.
  APPEND wa_l_source TO it_g_fuentes.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD wip_ampliar.
  DATA: it_l_order TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF aufk, wa_l_order TYPE aufk,
   v_lr TYPE REF TO data, v_lr_afpo TYPE REF TO data, v_lr_vbkd TYPE REF TO data,
   v_lr_fplt TYPE REF TO data, v_lr_up TYPE REF TO data, v_lr_vbak TYPE REF TO data,
   v_lr_flow TYPE REF TO data, v_lr_contract TYPE REF TO data,
   v_lr_cobrb TYPE REF TO data, v_lr_cosb TYPE REF TO data,
   it_l_fields TYPE ddfields, wa_l_field TYPE dfies,
   v_l_table TYPE tabname, v_l_field TYPE string, v_l_index TYPE n LENGTH 3,
   v_l_objnr TYPE string, v_l_aufnr TYPE aufnr, v_l_doc TYPE string,
   v_l_pos TYPE string, v_l_plan TYPE string, v_l_status TYPE string,
   v_l_contract TYPE string, v_l_from TYPE sydatum, v_l_to TYPE sydatum,
   v_l_amount TYPE decfloat34, v_l_part TYPE decfloat34,
   v_l_currdec TYPE i, v_l_dbdec TYPE i, v_l_valid TYPE abap_bool,
   v_l_cosp_ok TYPE abap_bool, v_l_coss_ok TYPE abap_bool,
   v_l_up_ok TYPE abap_bool, v_l_plan_ok TYPE abap_bool,
   v_l_has_status TYPE abap_bool, v_l_has_planstat TYPE abap_bool,
   v_l_x TYPE REF TO cx_root, v_l_count TYPE i,
   it_l_sums TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_wip.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_w> TYPE ty_wip, <fs_sum> TYPE ty_wip,
   <fs_data> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_row> TYPE any, <fs_amount> TYPE any,
   <fs_afpo> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_ap> TYPE any,
   <fs_up> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_uprow> TYPE any,
   <fs_kd> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_kdrow> TYPE any,
   <fs_plan> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_planrow> TYPE any,
   <fs_vbak> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_vbakrow> TYPE any,
   <fs_flow> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_flowrow> TYPE any,
   <fs_contracts> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_contractrow> TYPE any,
   <fs_cobrb> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_cosb> TYPE STANDARD TABLE.
  CLEAR: it_g_fuentes, v_g_fuentes_limite.
  it_l_order = it_orders. IF it_l_order IS INITIAL. RETURN. ENDIF.
  it_l_sums = it_g_wip.
  LOOP AT it_l_sums ASSIGNING <fs_sum>. <fs_sum>-saldo_costos = '0'. ENDLOOP.
* f2 implementa la formula del anexo, con valores por defecto 04/000.
* leer solo la moneda co wkg y verificar los dieciseis campos antes de sumar.
  DO 2 TIMES.
   CLEAR v_l_count.
   IF sy-index = 1. v_l_table = 'COSP'. ELSE. v_l_table = 'COSS'. ENDIF.
   it_l_fields = wip_fields( v_l_table ). v_l_valid = abap_true.
   DO 16 TIMES.
    v_l_index = sy-index. v_l_field = 'WKG' && v_l_index.
    READ TABLE it_l_fields INTO wa_l_field WITH KEY fieldname = v_l_field.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0. v_l_valid = abap_false. EXIT. ENDIF.
    IF wa_l_field-datatype <> 'CURR'. v_l_valid = abap_false. EXIT. ENDIF.
    v_l_dbdec = wa_l_field-decimals.
   ENDDO.
   IF v_l_valid = abap_true.
    v_lr = wip_read( v_i_table = v_l_table it_parent = it_l_order
     v_i_source = 'OBJNR' v_i_target = 'OBJNR' v_i_extra = |WRTTP = '04' AND VERSN = '000'| ).
    v_l_valid = v_g_wread_ok.
    IF v_l_valid = abap_true.
     ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_data>.
     v_l_count = lines( <fs_data> ).
     TRY.
      LOOP AT <fs_data> ASSIGNING <fs_row>.
       v_l_objnr = value( wa_i_row = <fs_row> v_i_field = 'OBJNR' ).
       READ TABLE it_l_sums ASSIGNING <fs_sum> WITH KEY objnr = v_l_objnr.
       IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
       v_l_amount = <fs_sum>-saldo_costos.
       DO 16 TIMES.
        v_l_index = sy-index. v_l_field = 'WKG' && v_l_index.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT v_l_field OF STRUCTURE <fs_row> TO <fs_amount>.
        IF sy-subrc <> 0. v_l_valid = abap_false. EXIT. ENDIF.
        v_l_amount = v_l_amount + <fs_amount>.
       ENDDO.
       <fs_sum>-saldo_costos = |{ v_l_amount NUMBER = RAW }|.
      ENDLOOP.
     CATCH cx_root INTO v_l_x. v_l_valid = abap_false. ENDTRY.
    ENDIF.
   ENDIF.
   IF v_l_table = 'COSP'. v_l_cosp_ok = v_l_valid. ELSE. v_l_coss_ok = v_l_valid. ENDIF.
   wip_source( v_i_order = space v_i_indicator = 'F2' v_i_table = v_l_table
    v_i_key = 'WRTTP=04;VERSN=000;WKG001..016' v_i_value = |OK={ v_l_valid }; registros={ v_l_count }|
    v_i_result = 'LECTURA_FORMULA_ANEXO'
    v_i_detail = `Formula implementada con valores por defecto del anexo; conciliar con CO antes de usar` &&
     `la como criterio definitivo` ).
  ENDDO.
  LOOP AT it_g_wip ASSIGNING <fs_w>.
   IF v_l_cosp_ok = abap_true AND v_l_coss_ok = abap_true AND <fs_w>-moneda IS NOT INITIAL.
    READ TABLE it_l_sums INTO DATA(wa_l_sum) WITH KEY aufnr = <fs_w>-aufnr.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
     TRY.
      v_l_amount = wa_l_sum-saldo_costos.
      v_l_currdec = 2.
      SELECT SINGLE currdec INTO v_l_currdec FROM tcurx WHERE currkey = <fs_w>-moneda.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0. v_l_currdec = 2. ENDIF.
      v_l_amount = v_l_amount * ( 10 ** ( v_l_dbdec - v_l_currdec ) ).
      <fs_w>-saldo_costos = |{ v_l_amount NUMBER = RAW }|.
      IF abs( v_l_amount ) > p_toler. <fs_w>-f2 = 'X'. ELSE. CLEAR <fs_w>-f2. ENDIF.
     CATCH cx_root INTO v_l_x. <fs_w>-f2 = 'U'. CLEAR <fs_w>-saldo_costos. ENDTRY.
    ENDIF.
   ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
* fuentes comerciales del anexo. ausencia de enlace no prueba ausencia de pendientes.
  v_lr_afpo = wip_read( v_i_table = 'AFPO' it_parent = it_l_order
   v_i_source = 'AUFNR' v_i_target = 'AUFNR' ).
  IF v_g_wread_ok = abap_true.
   ASSIGN v_lr_afpo->* TO <fs_afpo>.
   v_lr_up = wip_read( v_i_table = 'VBUP' it_parent = <fs_afpo>
    v_i_source = 'KDAUF' v_i_target = 'VBELN' ).
   v_l_up_ok = v_g_wread_ok.
   IF v_l_up_ok = abap_true. ASSIGN v_lr_up->* TO <fs_up>. ENDIF.
   it_l_fields = wip_fields( 'VBUP' ).
   READ TABLE it_l_fields TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY fieldname = 'FKSAA'.
   v_l_has_status = xsdbool( sy-subrc = 0 ).
   IF v_l_has_status = abap_false.
    wip_source( v_i_order = space v_i_indicator = 'F3' v_i_table = 'VBUP'
     v_i_key = 'FKSAA' v_i_value = '' v_i_result = 'CAMPO_NO_DISPONIBLE'
     v_i_detail = 'El campo indicado por el anexo no existe en DDIC. No se sustituye por otro estado sin validar su significado' ).
   ENDIF.
   v_lr_vbkd = wip_read( v_i_table = 'VBKD' it_parent = <fs_afpo>
    v_i_source = 'KDAUF' v_i_target = 'VBELN' ).
   IF v_g_wread_ok = abap_true.
    ASSIGN v_lr_vbkd->* TO <fs_kd>.
    v_lr_fplt = wip_read( v_i_table = 'FPLT' it_parent = <fs_kd>
     v_i_source = 'FPLNR' v_i_target = 'FPLNR' ).
    v_l_plan_ok = v_g_wread_ok.
    IF v_l_plan_ok = abap_true. ASSIGN v_lr_fplt->* TO <fs_plan>. ENDIF.
    it_l_fields = wip_fields( 'FPLT' ).
    READ TABLE it_l_fields TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY fieldname = 'FKSAF'.
    v_l_has_planstat = xsdbool( sy-subrc = 0 ).
    IF v_l_has_planstat = abap_false.
     wip_source( v_i_order = space v_i_indicator = 'F3' v_i_table = 'FPLT'
      v_i_key = 'FKSAF' v_i_value = '' v_i_result = 'CAMPO_NO_DISPONIBLE'
      v_i_detail = 'El campo de plan propuesto por el anexo no existe en DDIC; mantener F3 desconocido si no hay otro positivo' ).
    ENDIF.
   ENDIF.
   v_lr_vbak = wip_read( v_i_table = 'VBAK' it_parent = <fs_afpo>
    v_i_source = 'KDAUF' v_i_target = 'VBELN' ).
   IF v_g_wread_ok = abap_true. ASSIGN v_lr_vbak->* TO <fs_vbak>. ENDIF.
   v_lr_flow = wip_read( v_i_table = 'VBFA' it_parent = <fs_afpo>
    v_i_source = 'KDAUF' v_i_target = 'VBELN' v_i_extra = |VBTYP_V = 'G'| ).
   IF v_g_wread_ok = abap_true.
    ASSIGN v_lr_flow->* TO <fs_flow>.
    v_lr_contract = wip_read( v_i_table = 'VBAK' it_parent = <fs_flow>
     v_i_source = 'VBELV' v_i_target = 'VBELN' ).
    IF v_g_wread_ok = abap_true. ASSIGN v_lr_contract->* TO <fs_contracts>. ENDIF.
   ENDIF.
   DATA: v_lr_vbuk TYPE REF TO data, v_l_vbuk_ok TYPE abap_bool,
    v_l_has_headstat TYPE abap_bool.
   FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_vbuk> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_vbukrow> TYPE any.
   it_l_fields = wip_fields( 'VBUK' ).
   READ TABLE it_l_fields TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY fieldname = 'FKSAK'.
   v_l_has_headstat = xsdbool( sy-subrc = 0 ).
   IF v_l_has_headstat = abap_true.
    v_lr_vbuk = wip_read( v_i_table = 'VBUK' it_parent = <fs_afpo>
     v_i_source = 'KDAUF' v_i_target = 'VBELN' ).
    v_l_vbuk_ok = v_g_wread_ok.
    IF v_l_vbuk_ok = abap_true. ASSIGN v_lr_vbuk->* TO <fs_vbuk>. ENDIF.
   ELSE.
    wip_source( v_i_order = space v_i_indicator = 'F3' v_i_table = 'VBUK'
     v_i_key = 'FKSAK' v_i_value = '' v_i_result = 'CAMPO_NO_DISPONIBLE'
     v_i_detail = 'Campo de cabecera indicado en anexo no disponible; no inventar sustituto' ).
   ENDIF.
   LOOP AT <fs_afpo> ASSIGNING <fs_ap>.
    v_l_aufnr = value( wa_i_row = <fs_ap> v_i_field = 'AUFNR' ).
    READ TABLE it_g_wip ASSIGNING <fs_w> WITH KEY aufnr = v_l_aufnr.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    v_l_doc = value( wa_i_row = <fs_ap> v_i_field = 'KDAUF' ).
    v_l_pos = value( wa_i_row = <fs_ap> v_i_field = 'KDPOS' ).
    IF v_l_doc IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    IF v_l_vbuk_ok = abap_true AND v_l_has_headstat = abap_true.
     LOOP AT <fs_vbuk> ASSIGNING <fs_vbukrow>.
      IF value( wa_i_row = <fs_vbukrow> v_i_field = 'VBELN' ) <> v_l_doc. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
      v_l_status = value( wa_i_row = <fs_vbukrow> v_i_field = 'FKSAK' ).
      IF v_l_status = 'A' OR v_l_status = 'B'. <fs_w>-f3 = 'X'. ENDIF.
      wip_source( v_i_order = v_l_aufnr v_i_indicator = 'F3' v_i_table = 'VBUK'
       v_i_key = v_l_doc v_i_value = v_l_status v_i_result = 'ESTADO_CABECERA'
       v_i_detail = 'FKSAK A/B positivo segun anexo; otros resultados no descartan pendientes DIP' ).
     ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
    IF v_l_up_ok = abap_true AND v_l_has_status = abap_true.
     LOOP AT <fs_up> ASSIGNING <fs_uprow>.
      IF value( wa_i_row = <fs_uprow> v_i_field = 'VBELN' ) <> v_l_doc OR
         value( wa_i_row = <fs_uprow> v_i_field = 'POSNR' ) <> v_l_pos. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
      v_l_status = value( wa_i_row = <fs_uprow> v_i_field = 'FKSAA' ).
      IF v_l_status = 'A' OR v_l_status = 'B'. <fs_w>-f3 = 'X'. ENDIF.
      wip_source( v_i_order = v_l_aufnr v_i_indicator = 'F3' v_i_table = 'VBUP'
       v_i_key = |{ v_l_doc }/{ v_l_pos }| v_i_value = v_l_status
       v_i_result = 'ESTADO_ITEM' v_i_detail =
        'FKSAA del item asociado; A/B pendiente segun anexo. No sustituye el alcance DIP por confirmar' ).
     ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
    IF v_l_plan_ok = abap_true AND v_l_has_planstat = abap_true AND <fs_kd> IS ASSIGNED.
     LOOP AT <fs_kd> ASSIGNING <fs_kdrow>.
      IF value( wa_i_row = <fs_kdrow> v_i_field = 'VBELN' ) <> v_l_doc. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
      v_l_status = value( wa_i_row = <fs_kdrow> v_i_field = 'POSNR' ).
      IF v_l_status <> v_l_pos AND v_l_status <> '000000'. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
      v_l_plan = value( wa_i_row = <fs_kdrow> v_i_field = 'FPLNR' ).
      IF v_l_plan IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
      LOOP AT <fs_plan> ASSIGNING <fs_planrow>.
       IF value( wa_i_row = <fs_planrow> v_i_field = 'FPLNR' ) <> v_l_plan. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
       v_l_status = value( wa_i_row = <fs_planrow> v_i_field = 'FKSAF' ).
       IF v_l_status = 'A' OR v_l_status = 'B'. <fs_w>-f3 = 'X'. ENDIF.
       wip_source( v_i_order = v_l_aufnr v_i_indicator = 'F3' v_i_table = 'FPLT'
        v_i_key = v_l_plan v_i_value = v_l_status v_i_result = 'ESTADO_PLAN'
        v_i_detail = 'FKSAF A/B como pendiente segun anexo; corte no reconstruye facturacion historica' ).
      ENDLOOP.
     ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
* no asumir que cualquier pedido es contrato: guardar vigencias como evidencia.
    IF <fs_vbak> IS ASSIGNED.
     LOOP AT <fs_vbak> ASSIGNING <fs_vbakrow>.
      IF value( wa_i_row = <fs_vbakrow> v_i_field = 'VBELN' ) <> v_l_doc. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
      wip_source( v_i_order = v_l_aufnr v_i_indicator = 'F8' v_i_table = 'VBAK'
       v_i_key = v_l_doc v_i_value = value( wa_i_row = <fs_vbakrow> v_i_field = 'VBTYP' )
       v_i_result = 'VINCULO_POR_VALIDAR'
       v_i_detail = |Vigencia { value( wa_i_row = <fs_vbakrow> v_i_field = 'GUEBG' ) } a | &&
        |{ value( wa_i_row = <fs_vbakrow> v_i_field = 'GUEEN' ) }; confirmar contrato/garantia y enlace antes d| && |e asignar F8|
        ).
      IF value( wa_i_row = <fs_vbakrow> v_i_field = 'VBTYP' ) = 'G'.
       v_l_from = value( wa_i_row = <fs_vbakrow> v_i_field = 'GUEBG' ).
       v_l_to = value( wa_i_row = <fs_vbakrow> v_i_field = 'GUEEN' ).
       CALL FUNCTION 'DATE_CHECK_PLAUSIBILITY' EXPORTING date = v_l_from EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
       IF sy-subrc = 0.
        CALL FUNCTION 'DATE_CHECK_PLAUSIBILITY' EXPORTING date = v_l_to EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND v_l_from <= p_corte AND v_l_to >= p_corte. <fs_w>-f8 = 'X'. ENDIF.
       ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
     ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
    IF <fs_flow> IS ASSIGNED AND <fs_contracts> IS ASSIGNED.
     LOOP AT <fs_flow> ASSIGNING <fs_flowrow>.
      IF value( wa_i_row = <fs_flowrow> v_i_field = 'VBELN' ) <> v_l_doc. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
      IF value( wa_i_row = <fs_flowrow> v_i_field = 'POSNN' ) <> v_l_pos. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
      v_l_contract = value( wa_i_row = <fs_flowrow> v_i_field = 'VBELV' ).
      LOOP AT <fs_contracts> ASSIGNING <fs_contractrow>.
       IF value( wa_i_row = <fs_contractrow> v_i_field = 'VBELN' ) <> v_l_contract. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
       IF value( wa_i_row = <fs_contractrow> v_i_field = 'VBTYP' ) <> 'G'. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
       v_l_from = value( wa_i_row = <fs_contractrow> v_i_field = 'GUEBG' ).
       v_l_to = value( wa_i_row = <fs_contractrow> v_i_field = 'GUEEN' ).
       CALL FUNCTION 'DATE_CHECK_PLAUSIBILITY' EXPORTING date = v_l_from EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
       IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
       CALL FUNCTION 'DATE_CHECK_PLAUSIBILITY' EXPORTING date = v_l_to EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
       IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
       IF v_l_from <= p_corte AND v_l_to >= p_corte. <fs_w>-f8 = 'X'. ENDIF.
       wip_source( v_i_order = v_l_aufnr v_i_indicator = 'F8' v_i_table = 'VBAK'
        v_i_key = |{ v_l_doc }/{ v_l_pos } <- { v_l_contract }| v_i_value = CONV string( <fs_w>-f8 )
        v_i_result = 'CONTRATO_EN_FLUJO'
        v_i_detail = |VBFA contrato G; vigencia { v_l_from DATE = ISO } a { v_l_to DATE = ISO }; garantiza solo esta ruta co| &&
         |ntractual, no descarta otras garantias| ).
      ENDLOOP.
     ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.
* f6 y f7: recuperar datos reales disponibles sin convertirlos en reglas supuestas.
  v_lr_cobrb = wip_read( v_i_table = 'COBRB' it_parent = it_l_order
   v_i_source = 'OBJNR' v_i_target = 'OBJNR' ).
  IF v_g_wread_ok = abap_true.
   ASSIGN v_lr_cobrb->* TO <fs_cobrb>.
   LOOP AT <fs_cobrb> ASSIGNING <fs_row>.
    v_l_objnr = value( wa_i_row = <fs_row> v_i_field = 'OBJNR' ).
    READ TABLE it_l_order INTO wa_l_order WITH KEY objnr = v_l_objnr.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    wip_source( v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr v_i_indicator = 'F7' v_i_table = 'COBRB'
     v_i_key = |{ v_l_objnr }/{ value( wa_i_row = <fs_row> v_i_field = 'LFDNR' ) }|
     v_i_value = value( wa_i_row = <fs_row> v_i_field = 'KONTY' )
     v_i_result = 'RECEPTOR_POR_VALIDAR'
     v_i_detail = |Vigencia { value( wa_i_row = <fs_row> v_i_field = 'GABJA' ) }/| &&
      |{ value( wa_i_row = <fs_row> v_i_field = 'GABPE' ) } a | && |{ value( wa_i_row = <fs_row> v_i_field = 'GBISJ' ) }/| &&
      |{ value( wa_i_row = <fs_row> v_i_field = 'GBISP' ) }; lista terceros y periodo fiscal pendientes| ).
   ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.
  v_lr_cosb = wip_read( v_i_table = 'COSB' it_parent = it_l_order
   v_i_source = 'OBJNR' v_i_target = 'OBJNR' ).
  IF v_g_wread_ok = abap_true.
   ASSIGN v_lr_cosb->* TO <fs_cosb>.
   LOOP AT <fs_cosb> ASSIGNING <fs_row>.
    v_l_objnr = value( wa_i_row = <fs_row> v_i_field = 'OBJNR' ).
    READ TABLE it_l_order INTO wa_l_order WITH KEY objnr = v_l_objnr.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    wip_source( v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr v_i_indicator = 'F6' v_i_table = 'COSB'
     v_i_key = |{ v_l_objnr }/{ value( wa_i_row = <fs_row> v_i_field = 'GJAHR' ) }|
     v_i_value = |VERSN={ value( wa_i_row = <fs_row> v_i_field = 'VERSN' ) }; WRTTP=| &&
      |{ value( wa_i_row = <fs_row> v_i_field = 'WRTTP' ) }|
     v_i_result = 'RA_POR_VALIDAR'
     v_i_detail = `Registro COSB encontrado; confirmar RA_VERSN, categorias y campos de importe. No equiv` &&
      `ale a saldo WIP confirmado` ).
   ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.
  LOOP AT it_g_wip ASSIGNING <fs_w>.
   <fs_w>-detalle = |F2 formula 04/000 con lectura completa; F3/F8 solo positivos comprobados; F6/F7 y garantias pendientes| &&
    |. Ver WIP_FUENTES y validar con CO/SD.| .
   IF <fs_w>-f3 = 'X'. CLEAR <fs_w>-f9. ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD plantilla_issue.
  DATA wa_l_err TYPE ty_valida.
  wa_l_err-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_err-batch = v_g_batch.
  wa_l_err-aufnr = v_i_order. wa_l_err-hoja = v_i_sheet.
  wa_l_err-posicion = v_i_position. wa_l_err-campo = v_i_field.
  wa_l_err-valor = v_i_value. wa_l_err-tipo = v_i_type.
  wa_l_err-mensaje = v_i_message. APPEND wa_l_err TO it_g_validas.
 ENDMETHOD.


* @021 Controles reproducibles del anexo; no define equivalencias ni habilita migracion WIP.
 METHOD annex_validate.
  DATA: wa_l_sheet TYPE ty_sheet, wa_l_row TYPE ty_row, wa_l_order TYPE aufk,
   wa_l_link TYPE ty_poslink, wa_l_wip TYPE ty_wip,
   wa_l_diag TYPE ty_diag, it_l_diag TYPE ty_diags,
   it_l_links TYPE ty_poslinks, it_l_afvv TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afvv,
   wa_l_afvv TYPE afvv, it_l_resb TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF resb, wa_l_resb TYPE resb,
   v_lr TYPE REF TO data, v_l_no TYPE i, v_l_current TYPE aufnr,
   v_l_number TYPE decfloat34, v_l_expected TYPE decfloat34,
   v_l_binary TYPE xstring, it_l_lines TYPE ty_strings,
   v_l_order TYPE aufnr, v_l_pos TYPE string, v_l_issue TYPE string,
   v_lx TYPE REF TO cx_root.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_t> TYPE STANDARD TABLE.
  it_l_links = it_g_poslink.
  IF it_l_links IS NOT INITIAL.
   v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'AFVV' it_parent = it_l_links
    v_i_source = 'AUFPL' v_i_target = 'AUFPL' ).
   ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>.
   IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_afvv = <fs_t>.
   v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'RESB' it_parent = it_l_links
    v_i_source = 'RSNUM' v_i_target = 'RSNUM' ).
   ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>.
   IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_resb = <fs_t>.
   SORT it_l_afvv BY aufpl aplzl. SORT it_l_resb BY rsnum rspos.
  ENDIF.
  LOOP AT it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet.
   SORT wa_l_sheet-rows BY c01 c02.
   CLEAR: v_l_current, v_l_no.
   LOOP AT wa_l_sheet-rows INTO wa_l_row.
    CLEAR: v_l_issue, wa_l_diag.
    v_l_order = wa_l_row-c01. v_l_pos = wa_l_row-c02.
    wa_l_diag-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_diag-batch = v_g_batch.
    wa_l_diag-aufnr = v_l_order. wa_l_diag-entidad = 'ID_ORIGEN'.
    wa_l_diag-clave = |Hoja { wa_l_sheet-number }; clave { wa_l_row-c01 }|.
    wa_l_diag-resultado = 'COINCIDE_ECC'.
    READ TABLE it_g_origin INTO wa_l_order WITH KEY aufnr = v_l_order.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR wa_l_row-c01 <> CONV string( wa_l_order-aufnr ).
     v_l_issue = 'ID externo no coincide exactamente con AUFK-AUFNR de origen'.
     wa_l_diag-resultado = 'ERROR_ID_ORIGEN'.
    ENDIF.
    wa_l_diag-detalle = 'Valida la clave ECC completa; no certifica numeracion definitiva en S/4'.
    APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_l_diag.
    IF v_l_issue IS NOT INITIAL.
     plantilla_issue( v_i_order = v_l_order v_i_sheet = wa_l_sheet-title
      v_i_position = '' v_i_field = 'SERVICEORDER' v_i_value = wa_l_row-c01
      v_i_type = 'E' v_i_message = v_l_issue ).
    ENDIF.
    IF wa_l_sheet-number <> 5. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    IF v_l_current <> v_l_order. v_l_current = v_l_order. CLEAR v_l_no. ENDIF.
    ADD 1 TO v_l_no. CLEAR v_l_issue.
    TRY.
     v_l_number = wa_l_row-c02.
     IF v_l_number <> v_l_no. v_l_issue = 'Posiciones no consecutivas por orden desde 1'. ENDIF.
    CATCH cx_root INTO v_lx. v_l_issue = 'Numero de posicion no numerico'. ENDTRY.
    READ TABLE it_g_wip INTO wa_l_wip WITH KEY aufnr = v_l_order.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR wa_l_wip-escenario <> 'B'.
     v_l_issue = 'A.5: posicion preparada fuera del escenario B'.
    ENDIF.
    READ TABLE it_l_links INTO wa_l_link WITH KEY aufnr = v_l_order posicion = wa_l_row-c02.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
     v_l_issue = 'Posicion sin traza de operacion o componente de origen'.
    ELSEIF wa_l_link-clase = 'COMPONENTE'.
     READ TABLE it_l_resb INTO wa_l_resb WITH KEY
      rsnum = wa_l_link-rsnum rspos = wa_l_link-rspos BINARY SEARCH.
     IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      v_l_issue = 'Componente de origen no encontrado por RSNUM/RSPOS'.
     ELSEIF wa_l_resb-xloek IS NOT INITIAL OR wa_l_resb-kzear IS NOT INITIAL.
      v_l_issue = 'Componente borrado o con salida final no corresponde a posicion pendiente'.
     ELSE.
      v_l_expected = wa_l_resb-bdmng - wa_l_resb-enmng.
      TRY.
       v_l_number = wa_l_row-c07.
       IF v_l_expected <= 0 OR v_l_number <> v_l_expected.
        v_l_issue = 'Cantidad de componente distinta de BDMNG menos ENMNG'.
       ENDIF.
      CATCH cx_root INTO v_lx. v_l_issue = 'Cantidad de componente no numerica'. ENDTRY.
     ENDIF.
    ELSEIF wa_l_link-clase = 'OPERACION'.
     READ TABLE it_l_afvv INTO wa_l_afvv WITH KEY
      aufpl = wa_l_link-aufpl aplzl = wa_l_link-aplzl BINARY SEARCH.
     IF sy-subrc = 0.
      TRY.
       v_l_number = wa_l_row-c09.
       IF v_l_number <> wa_l_afvv-dauno.
        v_l_issue = 'Duracion distinta de AFVV-DAUNO de la operacion'.
       ELSEIF wa_l_row-c10 <> unidad_iso( v_i_unit = CONV string( wa_l_afvv-daune ) v_i_iso = '' ).
        v_l_issue = 'Unidad de duracion distinta de AFVV-DAUNE convertida a ISO'.
       ENDIF.
      CATCH cx_root INTO v_lx. v_l_issue = 'Duracion no numerica'. ENDTRY.
     ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR wa_l_diag. wa_l_diag-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_diag-batch = v_g_batch.
    wa_l_diag-aufnr = v_l_order. wa_l_diag-entidad = 'ANEXO_A5'.
    wa_l_diag-clave = |Posicion { v_l_pos }|. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'CONTROL_ORIGEN_OK'.
    wa_l_diag-detalle = 'Consecutivo, escenario y valores ECC contrastados; sin conversion de negocio'.
    IF v_l_issue IS NOT INITIAL.
     wa_l_diag-resultado = 'ERROR_ORIGEN'. wa_l_diag-detalle = v_l_issue.
     plantilla_issue( v_i_order = v_l_order v_i_sheet = wa_l_sheet-title
      v_i_position = v_l_pos v_i_field = 'A5_ORIGEN' v_i_value = wa_l_row-c02
      v_i_type = 'E' v_i_message = v_l_issue ).
    ENDIF.
    APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_l_diag.
   ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF it_l_diag IS NOT INITIAL.
   it_l_lines = serialize( it_l_diag ).
   emit( v_i_entity = 'AUDITORIA_ANEXO' v_i_kind = 'TXT'
    v_i_records = lines( it_l_diag ) v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
  ENDIF.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD plantilla_audit.
  DATA: wa_l_row TYPE ty_row, v_l_number TYPE decfloat34,
   v_l_start TYPE sydatum, v_l_end TYPE sydatum, v_l_date TYPE sydatum,
   v_l_col TYPE n LENGTH 2, v_l_field TYPE string, v_l_position TYPE string,
   v_l_order TYPE aufnr, v_l_index TYPE i, v_lx TYPE REF TO cx_root,
   it_l_lines TYPE ty_strings, v_l_binary TYPE xstring,
   wa_l_v TYPE ty_valida, it_l_keys TYPE ty_keys, wa_l_key TYPE ty_key,
   it_l_invalid TYPE ty_keys, wa_l_invalid TYPE ty_key.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_sheet> TYPE ty_sheet, <fs_head> TYPE ty_sheet,
   <fs_positions> TYPE ty_sheet, <fs_cell> TYPE any,
   <fs_eval> TYPE ty_eval, <fs_w> TYPE ty_wip.
  CLEAR it_g_validas.
  origin_validate( ).
  annex_validate( ).
  IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
  READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_head> WITH KEY number = 3.
  READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_positions> WITH KEY number = 5.
  LOOP AT it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet>.
   LOOP AT <fs_sheet>-rows INTO wa_l_row.
    v_l_order = wa_l_row-c01. CLEAR v_l_position.
    IF <fs_sheet>-number >= 5 AND <fs_sheet>-number <= 9. v_l_position = wa_l_row-c02. ENDIF.
    IF v_l_order IS INITIAL.
     plantilla_issue( v_i_order = v_l_order v_i_sheet = CONV string( <fs_sheet>-title )
      v_i_position = v_l_position v_i_field = 'SERVICEORDER' v_i_value = ''
      v_i_type = 'E' v_i_message = 'Clave de orden vacia' ).
    ENDIF.
    IF <fs_sheet>-number <> 3.
     READ TABLE <fs_head>-rows TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY c01 = wa_l_row-c01.
     IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      plantilla_issue( v_i_order = v_l_order v_i_sheet = CONV string( <fs_sheet>-title )
       v_i_position = v_l_position v_i_field = 'SERVICEORDER' v_i_value = wa_l_row-c01
       v_i_type = 'E' v_i_message = 'Detalle sin cabecera correspondiente' ).
     ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF <fs_sheet>-number >= 6 AND <fs_sheet>-number <= 9.
     READ TABLE <fs_positions>-rows TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY c01 = wa_l_row-c01 c02 = wa_l_row-c02.
     IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      plantilla_issue( v_i_order = v_l_order v_i_sheet = CONV string( <fs_sheet>-title )
       v_i_position = v_l_position v_i_field = 'SERVICEORDERITEM' v_i_value = wa_l_row-c02
       v_i_type = 'E' v_i_message = 'Detalle sin posicion correspondiente' ).
     ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF <fs_sheet>-number = 3 OR <fs_sheet>-number = 5.
     wa_l_key-value = |{ <fs_sheet>-number }/{ wa_l_row-c01 }/{ v_l_position }|.
     INSERT wa_l_key INTO TABLE it_l_keys.
     IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      plantilla_issue( v_i_order = v_l_order v_i_sheet = CONV string( <fs_sheet>-title )
       v_i_position = v_l_position v_i_field = 'CLAVE' v_i_value = wa_l_key-value
       v_i_type = 'E' v_i_message = 'Clave duplicada' ).
     ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF <fs_sheet>-number = 3.
     IF wa_l_row-c03 = 'X'.
      plantilla_issue( v_i_order = v_l_order v_i_sheet = 'Cab.' v_i_position = ''
       v_i_field = 'NRANGE_IND' v_i_value = wa_l_row-c03 v_i_type = 'W'
       v_i_message = 'Numeracion interna: clave origen no garantiza conservar numero definitivo' ).
     ENDIF.
     DO 3 TIMES.
      CASE sy-index. WHEN 1. v_l_index = 9. WHEN 2. v_l_index = 12. WHEN 3. v_l_index = 16. ENDCASE.
      v_l_col = v_l_index. v_l_field = 'C' && v_l_col.
      ASSIGN COMPONENT v_l_field OF STRUCTURE wa_l_row TO <fs_cell>.
      IF <fs_cell> IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
      v_l_date = <fs_cell>.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DATE_CHECK_PLAUSIBILITY' EXPORTING date = v_l_date EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
       plantilla_issue( v_i_order = v_l_order v_i_sheet = 'Cab.' v_i_position = ''
        v_i_field = v_l_field v_i_value = CONV string( <fs_cell> ) v_i_type = 'E'
        v_i_message = 'Fecha no valida' ).
      ENDIF.
     ENDDO.
     IF wa_l_row-c09 IS NOT INITIAL AND wa_l_row-c12 IS NOT INITIAL AND wa_l_row-c12 < wa_l_row-c09.
      plantilla_issue( v_i_order = v_l_order v_i_sheet = 'Cab.' v_i_position = ''
       v_i_field = 'REQUESTEDSERVICEENDDATE' v_i_value = wa_l_row-c12 v_i_type = 'E'
       v_i_message = 'Fin anterior al inicio' ).
     ENDIF.
    ELSEIF <fs_sheet>-number = 5.
     IF wa_l_row-c04 IS INITIAL.
      plantilla_issue( v_i_order = v_l_order v_i_sheet = 'Posicion' v_i_position = v_l_position
       v_i_field = 'SERVICEORDERITEMDESCRIPTION' v_i_value = '' v_i_type = 'E'
       v_i_message = 'Descripcion obligatoria sin dato de origen; orden pasa a revision' ).
     ENDIF.
* Producto servicio y categoria S/4 no se deducen de STEUS ni de ASSEMBLY.
* Sus vacios quedan detallados en COBERTURA_CAMPOS; no bloquear por homologaciones.
     IF wa_l_row-c08 IS INITIAL.
      plantilla_issue( v_i_order = v_l_order v_i_sheet = 'Posicion' v_i_position = v_l_position
       v_i_field = 'QUANTITYUNIT' v_i_value = '' v_i_type = 'W'
       v_i_message = 'Unidad de cantidad vacia en la extraccion ECC; se conserva vacia sin valor por defecto' ).
     ENDIF.
     TRY.
      v_l_number = wa_l_row-c07.
      IF v_l_number <= 0.
       plantilla_issue( v_i_order = v_l_order v_i_sheet = 'Posicion' v_i_position = v_l_position
        v_i_field = 'QUANTITY' v_i_value = wa_l_row-c07 v_i_type = 'E'
        v_i_message = 'Cantidad no positiva; revisar regla de llenado' ).
      ENDIF.
     CATCH cx_root INTO v_lx.
      plantilla_issue( v_i_order = v_l_order v_i_sheet = 'Posicion' v_i_position = v_l_position
       v_i_field = 'QUANTITY' v_i_value = wa_l_row-c07 v_i_type = 'E'
       v_i_message = 'Cantidad no numerica' ).
     ENDTRY.
    ENDIF.
    DO <fs_sheet>-columns TIMES.
     v_l_col = sy-index. v_l_field = 'C' && v_l_col.
     ASSIGN COMPONENT v_l_field OF STRUCTURE wa_l_row TO <fs_cell>.
     IF sy-subrc = 0 AND strlen( <fs_cell> ) > 32767.
      plantilla_issue( v_i_order = v_l_order v_i_sheet = CONV string( <fs_sheet>-title )
       v_i_position = v_l_position v_i_field = v_l_field v_i_value = '[texto extenso]'
       v_i_type = 'E' v_i_message = 'Celda excede limite XLSX 32767 caracteres; no truncar silenciosamente' ).
     ENDIF.
    ENDDO.
   ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
* errores tecnicos de archivo excluyen toda la orden; avisos de mapeo siguen visibles.
  LOOP AT it_g_validas INTO wa_l_v WHERE tipo = 'E'.
   IF wa_l_v-aufnr IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
   wa_l_invalid-value = wa_l_v-aufnr. INSERT wa_l_invalid INTO TABLE it_l_invalid.
  ENDLOOP.
  LOOP AT it_l_invalid INTO wa_l_invalid.
   v_l_order = wa_l_invalid-value.
   LOOP AT it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet>.
    DELETE <fs_sheet>-rows WHERE c01 = wa_l_invalid-value.
   ENDLOOP.
   DELETE it_g_head WHERE aufnr = v_l_order.
   DELETE it_g_notes WHERE aufnr = v_l_order.
   READ TABLE it_g_eval ASSIGNING <fs_eval> WITH KEY aufnr = v_l_order.
   IF sy-subrc = 0.
    IF <fs_eval>-estado = 'A' AND v_g_total > 0. SUBTRACT 1 FROM v_g_total. ENDIF.
    <fs_eval>-estado = 'R'. <fs_eval>-motivo = 'VAL_ERR'.
    <fs_eval>-detalle = `Error de estructura o valor en plantilla; orden completa retirada de la descarga. Ver ` &&
     `VALIDACION_PLANTILLA` .
   ENDIF.
   READ TABLE it_g_wip ASSIGNING <fs_w> WITH KEY aufnr = v_l_order.
   IF sy-subrc = 0.
    <fs_w>-decision = 'R'. <fs_w>-motivo = 'VAL_ERR'.
    <fs_w>-detalle = <fs_w>-detalle && ' Error tecnico de plantilla; ver VALIDACION_PLANTILLA'.
   ENDIF.
   log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'VAL_ERR' v_i_order = v_l_order
    v_i_message = 'Orden retirada de todas las hojas por error tecnico de plantilla; se continua con las demas' ).
  ENDLOOP.
  LOOP AT it_g_validas INTO wa_l_v.
   IF lines( it_g_validas_view ) < p_det. APPEND wa_l_v TO it_g_validas_view. ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF it_g_validas IS NOT INITIAL.
   it_l_lines = serialize( it_g_validas ).
   emit( v_i_entity = 'VALIDACION_PLANTILLA' v_i_kind = 'TXT'
    v_i_records = lines( it_g_validas ) v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
  ENDIF.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD plantilla_show.
  DATA: v_lo_list TYPE REF TO cl_salv_table, v_lx TYPE REF TO cx_salv_msg.
  IF it_g_validas_view IS INITIAL. MESSAGE 'Sin incidencias de plantilla en la muestra de esta corrida' TYPE 'S'. RETURN. ENDIF.
  TRY.
   cl_salv_table=>factory( IMPORTING r_salv_table = v_lo_list CHANGING t_table = it_g_validas_view ).
   v_lo_list->get_functions( )->set_all( abap_true ).
   v_lo_list->get_columns( )->set_optimize( abap_true ).
   v_lo_list->display( ).
  CATCH cx_salv_msg INTO v_lx. MESSAGE v_lx->get_text( ) TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. ENDTRY.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD cabecera_socios.
  TYPES: BEGIN OF ty_rol, parvw TYPE parvw, campo TYPE c LENGTH 3, END OF ty_rol.
  DATA: it_l_roles TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_rol, wa_l_role TYPE ty_rol,
   it_l_part TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ihpa,
   it_l_ids TYPE ty_keys, wa_l_id TYPE ty_key, wa_l_part TYPE ihpa,
   wa_l_order TYPE aufk, wa_l_row TYPE ty_row, v_l_pos TYPE i.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_sheet> TYPE ty_sheet, <fs_row> TYPE ty_row,
   <fs_head> TYPE ty_head, <fs_cell> TYPE any.
* funciones estandar de origen; conservar IDs origen, sin afirmar homologacion BP.
  it_l_part = it_partners.
  wa_l_role-parvw = 'AG'. wa_l_role-campo = 'C22'. APPEND wa_l_role TO it_l_roles.
  wa_l_role-parvw = 'WE'. wa_l_role-campo = 'C23'. APPEND wa_l_role TO it_l_roles.
  wa_l_role-parvw = 'RE'. wa_l_role-campo = 'C24'. APPEND wa_l_role TO it_l_roles.
  wa_l_role-parvw = 'RG'. wa_l_role-campo = 'C25'. APPEND wa_l_role TO it_l_roles.
  wa_l_role-parvw = 'AP'. wa_l_role-campo = 'C26'. APPEND wa_l_role TO it_l_roles.
  READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet> WITH KEY number = 3.
  LOOP AT <fs_sheet>-rows ASSIGNING <fs_row>.
   SELECT SINGLE * INTO wa_l_order FROM aufk WHERE aufnr = <fs_row>-c01.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
   LOOP AT it_l_roles INTO wa_l_role.
    CLEAR it_l_ids.
    LOOP AT it_l_part INTO wa_l_part WHERE objnr = wa_l_order-objnr
     AND parvw = wa_l_role-parvw AND kzloesch = space.
     IF wa_l_part-parnr IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
     wa_l_id-value = wa_l_part-parnr. INSERT wa_l_id INTO TABLE it_l_ids.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF lines( it_l_ids ) = 1.
     READ TABLE it_l_ids INTO wa_l_id INDEX 1.
     ASSIGN COMPONENT wa_l_role-campo OF STRUCTURE <fs_row> TO <fs_cell>.
     IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF <fs_cell> IS INITIAL.
       <fs_cell> = wa_l_id-value.
       log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'SOCIOS_CAB' v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
        v_i_message = |Campo { wa_l_role-campo } desde IHPA { wa_l_role-parvw }={ wa_l_id-value }; ID origen sin homologacion| ).
      ELSEIF <fs_cell> <> wa_l_id-value.
       log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'SOCIOS_CAB' v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
        v_i_message = |Conflicto { wa_l_role-campo }: valor actual { <fs_cell> } vs IHPA { wa_l_id-value }; no se sobrescribe| ).
      ENDIF.
     ENDIF.
    ELSEIF lines( it_l_ids ) > 1.
     log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'SOCIOS_CAB' v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
