      v_i_message = |Funcion { wa_l_role-parvw } con varios interlocutores; no elegir uno arbitrariamente| ).
    ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
   READ TABLE it_g_head ASSIGNING <fs_head> WITH KEY aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr.
   IF sy-subrc = 0.
    <fs_head>-mc_sold_to = <fs_row>-c22. <fs_head>-mc_ship_to = <fs_row>-c23.
    <fs_head>-mc_bill_to = <fs_row>-c24. <fs_head>-mc_payer = <fs_row>-c25.
    <fs_head>-mc_contact = <fs_row>-c26.
   ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
 ENDMETHOD.


* @017 Numeracion consecutiva; cada clave fuente queda en TRAZA_POSICIONES.
 METHOD position_for_operation.
  DATA: wa_l_link TYPE ty_poslink, v_l_count TYPE i.
  LOOP AT it_g_poslink INTO wa_l_link WHERE aufnr = v_i_order
   AND clase = 'OPERACION' AND vornr = v_i_activity.
   ADD 1 TO v_l_count. v_r_position = wa_l_link-posicion.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF v_l_count <> 1. CLEAR v_r_position. ENDIF.
 ENDMETHOD.


* Recuento despues de VAL_ERR: distinguir preparadas de realmente entregadas.
 METHOD positions_final_diag.
  DATA: wa_l_order TYPE aufk, wa_l_link TYPE ty_poslink, wa_l_row TYPE ty_row,
   wa_l_diag TYPE ty_diag, v_l_prepared TYPE i, v_l_delivered TYPE i,
   v_l_removed TYPE i.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_sheet> TYPE ty_sheet.
  READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet> WITH KEY number = 5.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. RETURN. ENDIF.
  LOOP AT it_g_origin INTO wa_l_order.
   CLEAR: v_l_prepared, v_l_delivered.
   LOOP AT it_g_poslink INTO wa_l_link WHERE aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr.
    ADD 1 TO v_l_prepared.
   ENDLOOP.
   LOOP AT <fs_sheet>-rows INTO wa_l_row WHERE c01 = wa_l_order-aufnr.
    ADD 1 TO v_l_delivered.
   ENDLOOP.
   v_l_removed = v_l_prepared - v_l_delivered.
   CLEAR wa_l_diag. wa_l_diag-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_diag-batch = v_g_batch.
   wa_l_diag-aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr. wa_l_diag-entidad = 'CONCILIACION_FINAL'.
   IF v_l_removed >= 0. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'CUADRA'.
   ELSE. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'NO_CUADRA'. v_g_failed = abap_true. ENDIF.
   wa_l_diag-detalle = |Preparadas { v_l_prepared } = entregables { v_l_delivered } | &&
    |+ retiradas { v_l_removed }. Si P_SIMUL=X no se genera Cockpit; revisar VAL_ERR.|.
   APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_g_diag.
  ENDLOOP.
 ENDMETHOD.

* @018 Estados: los E se interpretan con perfil STSMA, nunca solo con ESTAT.
 METHOD states_read.
  DATA: it_l_orders TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF aufk, wa_l_order TYPE aufk,
   it_l_jest TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF jest, wa_l_jest TYPE jest,
   it_l_jsto TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF jsto, wa_l_jsto TYPE jsto,
   it_l_sys TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF tj02t, wa_l_sys TYPE tj02t,
   it_l_user TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF tj30t, wa_l_user TYPE tj30t,
   it_l_rules TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF tj30, wa_l_rule TYPE tj30,
   it_l_rows TYPE ty_status_reads, wa_l_row TYPE ty_status_read,
   v_lr TYPE REF TO data.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_t> TYPE STANDARD TABLE.
  CREATE DATA v_rr_data LIKE it_l_rows.
  it_l_orders = it_orders.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'JEST' it_parent = it_l_orders
   v_i_source = 'OBJNR' v_i_target = 'OBJNR' v_i_extra = |INACT = ' '| ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_jest = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'JSTO' it_parent = it_l_orders
   v_i_source = 'OBJNR' v_i_target = 'OBJNR' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_jsto = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'TJ02T' it_parent = it_l_jest
   v_i_source = 'STAT' v_i_target = 'ISTAT'
   v_i_extra = |SPRAS = { literal( CONV string( sy-langu ) ) }| ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_sys = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'TJ30' it_parent = it_l_jsto
   v_i_source = 'STSMA' v_i_target = 'STSMA' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_rules = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'TJ30T' it_parent = it_l_jsto
   v_i_source = 'STSMA' v_i_target = 'STSMA'
   v_i_extra = |SPRAS = { literal( CONV string( sy-langu ) ) }| ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_user = <fs_t>.
  LOOP AT it_l_jest INTO wa_l_jest.
   READ TABLE it_l_orders INTO wa_l_order WITH KEY objnr = wa_l_jest-objnr.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
   CLEAR wa_l_row. MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_l_jest TO wa_l_row.
   wa_l_row-aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr. wa_l_row-spras = sy-langu.
   wa_l_row-evidencia = 'SIN_TEXTO_EN_IDIOMA'.
   IF wa_l_jest-stat(1) = 'I'.
    wa_l_row-tipo = 'SISTEMA'.
    READ TABLE it_l_sys INTO wa_l_sys WITH KEY istat = wa_l_jest-stat.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
     wa_l_row-txt04 = wa_l_sys-txt04. wa_l_row-txt30 = wa_l_sys-txt30.
     wa_l_row-evidencia = 'TEXTO_SISTEMA'.
    ENDIF.
   ELSEIF wa_l_jest-stat(1) = 'E'.
    wa_l_row-tipo = 'USUARIO'.
    READ TABLE it_l_jsto INTO wa_l_jsto WITH KEY objnr = wa_l_jest-objnr.
    IF sy-subrc = 0. wa_l_row-stsma = wa_l_jsto-stsma. ENDIF.
    IF wa_l_row-stsma IS INITIAL. wa_l_row-evidencia = 'SIN_PERFIL_STSMA'.
    ELSE.
     READ TABLE it_l_rules INTO wa_l_rule WITH KEY stsma = wa_l_row-stsma estat = wa_l_jest-stat.
     IF sy-subrc <> 0. wa_l_row-evidencia = 'ESTADO_NO_CONFIGURADO'.
     ELSE.
      READ TABLE it_l_user INTO wa_l_user WITH KEY stsma = wa_l_row-stsma estat = wa_l_jest-stat.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
       wa_l_row-txt04 = wa_l_user-txt04. wa_l_row-txt30 = wa_l_user-txt30.
       wa_l_row-evidencia = 'TEXTO_USUARIO'.
      ENDIF.
     ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
   ELSE. wa_l_row-tipo = 'DESCONOCIDO'. ENDIF.
   APPEND wa_l_row TO it_l_rows.
  ENDLOOP.
  ASSIGN v_rr_data->* TO <fs_t>. <fs_t> = it_l_rows.
 ENDMETHOD.

* @018 Clasificacion segun fuente explicita de la ET: OBJEK=AUFNR, KLART=018.
* No probar claves alternativas como si fueran equivalentes sin evidencia.
 METHOD classes_read.
  DATA: it_l_orders TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF aufk, wa_l_order TYPE aufk,
   it_l_ausp TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ausp, wa_l_ausp TYPE ausp,
   it_l_cabn TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF cabn, wa_l_cabn TYPE cabn,
   it_l_cabnt TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF cabnt, wa_l_cabnt TYPE cabnt,
   it_l_rows TYPE ty_class_reads, wa_l_row TYPE ty_class_read,
   v_lr TYPE REF TO data, v_l_objek TYPE ausp-objek,
   v_l_name TYPE cabn-atnam, v_l_label TYPE cabnt-atbez,
   v_l_conflict TYPE abap_bool.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_t> TYPE STANDARD TABLE.
  CREATE DATA v_rr_data LIKE it_l_rows. it_l_orders = it_orders.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'AUSP' it_parent = it_l_orders
   v_i_source = 'AUFNR' v_i_target = 'OBJEK'
   v_i_extra = |KLART = { literal( CONV string( p_klart ) ) } AND LKENZ = ' '| ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_ausp = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'CABN' it_parent = it_l_ausp
   v_i_source = 'ATINN' v_i_target = 'ATINN' v_i_extra = |LKENZ = ' '| ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_cabn = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'CABNT' it_parent = it_l_ausp
   v_i_source = 'ATINN' v_i_target = 'ATINN'
   v_i_extra = |SPRAS = { literal( CONV string( sy-langu ) ) }| ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_cabnt = <fs_t>.
  LOOP AT it_l_orders INTO wa_l_order.
   v_l_objek = wa_l_order-aufnr.
   LOOP AT it_l_ausp INTO wa_l_ausp WHERE objek = v_l_objek.
    CLEAR: wa_l_row, v_l_name, v_l_label, v_l_conflict.
    wa_l_row-aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_l_ausp TO wa_l_row.
    LOOP AT it_l_cabn INTO wa_l_cabn WHERE atinn = wa_l_ausp-atinn.
     IF v_l_name IS INITIAL. v_l_name = wa_l_cabn-atnam.
     ELSEIF v_l_name <> wa_l_cabn-atnam. v_l_conflict = abap_true. ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF v_l_conflict = abap_false. wa_l_row-atnam = v_l_name. ENDIF.
    LOOP AT it_l_cabnt INTO wa_l_cabnt WHERE atinn = wa_l_ausp-atinn.
     IF v_l_label IS INITIAL. v_l_label = wa_l_cabnt-atbez.
     ELSEIF v_l_label <> wa_l_cabnt-atbez. CLEAR v_l_label. EXIT. ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    wa_l_row-atbez = v_l_label.
    wa_l_row-atflv = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_ausp v_i_field = 'ATFLV' ).
    wa_l_row-atflb = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_ausp v_i_field = 'ATFLB' ).
    wa_l_row-unidad = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_ausp v_i_field = 'ATAWE' ).
    wa_l_row-tipo_valor = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_ausp v_i_field = 'ATCOD' ).
    IF v_l_conflict = abap_true. wa_l_row-evidencia = 'METADATO_AMBIGUO'.
    ELSEIF wa_l_row-atnam IS INITIAL. wa_l_row-evidencia = 'SIN_CABN'.
    ELSE. wa_l_row-evidencia = 'VALOR_ORIGEN'. ENDIF.
    APPEND wa_l_row TO it_l_rows.
   ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
  ASSIGN v_rr_data->* TO <fs_t>. <fs_t> = it_l_rows.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD origin_reports.
  DATA: v_lr TYPE REF TO data, it_l_lines TYPE ty_strings, v_l_binary TYPE xstring,
   it_l_orders TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF aufk, wa_l_order TYPE aufk,
   wa_l_diag TYPE ty_diag, v_l_objek TYPE ausp-objek.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_t> TYPE STANDARD TABLE.
  v_lr = states_read( it_orders ).
  IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. it_l_lines = serialize( <fs_t> ).
  emit( v_i_entity = 'ESTADOS_DESCRITOS' v_i_kind = 'TXT'
   v_i_records = lines( <fs_t> ) v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
  v_lr = classes_read( it_orders ).
  IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>.
  it_l_orders = it_orders.
  LOOP AT it_l_orders INTO wa_l_order.
   v_l_objek = wa_l_order-aufnr.
   CLEAR wa_l_diag. wa_l_diag-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_diag-batch = v_g_batch.
   wa_l_diag-aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr. wa_l_diag-entidad = 'CLASIFICACION'.
   READ TABLE <fs_t> TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY ('OBJEK') = v_l_objek.
   IF sy-subrc = 0. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'CON_VALORES'.
   ELSE. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'SIN_DATOS_CLAVE_ET'. ENDIF.
   wa_l_diag-detalle = |AUSP-OBJEK=AUFNR; KLART={ p_klart }; no afirmar NO_APLICA; | &&
    |si el cliente usa INOB u otra clave, validar ese enlace antes de sustituirlo.|.
   APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_g_diag.
  ENDLOOP.
  it_l_lines = serialize( <fs_t> ).
  emit( v_i_entity = 'CLASIFICACION' v_i_kind = 'TXT'
   v_i_records = lines( <fs_t> ) v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
 ENDMETHOD.

* Resultado X=existe, espacio=no existe, U=no comprobable. Cache por corrida.
 METHOD origin_exists.
  DATA: wa_l_cache TYPE ty_exists_cache, it_l_fields TYPE ddfields,
   v_l_where TYPE string, v_l_count TYPE i, v_lx TYPE REF TO cx_root.
  v_r_result = 'U'.
  wa_l_cache-clave = |{ v_i_table }/{ v_i_field }/{ v_i_value }/{ v_i_extra }|.
  READ TABLE it_g_exists INTO wa_l_cache WITH TABLE KEY clave = wa_l_cache-clave.
  IF sy-subrc = 0. v_r_result = wa_l_cache-resultado. RETURN. ENDIF.
  it_l_fields = wip_fields( v_i_table ).
  READ TABLE it_l_fields TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY fieldname = v_i_field.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
   v_l_where = v_i_field && ' = ' && literal( v_i_value ).
   IF v_i_extra IS NOT INITIAL. v_l_where = v_l_where && ' AND ' && v_i_extra. ENDIF.
   TRY.
    SELECT COUNT( * ) INTO v_l_count FROM (v_i_table) WHERE (v_l_where).
    v_r_result = xsdbool( v_l_count > 0 ).
   CATCH cx_root INTO v_lx. v_r_result = 'U'. ENDTRY.
  ENDIF.
  wa_l_cache-clave = |{ v_i_table }/{ v_i_field }/{ v_i_value }/{ v_i_extra }|.
  wa_l_cache-resultado = v_r_result. INSERT wa_l_cache INTO TABLE it_g_exists.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD origin_validate.
  DATA: wa_l_order TYPE aufk, wa_l_link TYPE ty_poslink, wa_l_row TYPE ty_row,
   v_l_result TYPE char1, v_l_other TYPE char1, v_l_type TYPE char1,
   v_l_field TYPE string, v_l_extra TYPE string, v_l_value TYPE string,
   v_l_idx TYPE i, v_l_col TYPE n LENGTH 2, v_l_position TYPE string.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_sheet> TYPE ty_sheet, <fs_cell> TYPE any.
  LOOP AT it_g_origin INTO wa_l_order WHERE werks IS NOT INITIAL.
   v_l_result = origin_exists( v_i_table = 'T001W' v_i_field = 'WERKS'
    v_i_value = CONV string( wa_l_order-werks ) ).
   IF v_l_result <> 'X'.
    v_l_type = 'E'. IF v_l_result = 'U'. v_l_type = 'W'. ENDIF.
    plantilla_issue( v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr v_i_sheet = 'Origen ECC' v_i_position = ''
     v_i_field = 'AUFK-WERKS' v_i_value = CONV string( wa_l_order-werks ) v_i_type = v_l_type
     v_i_message = 'Centro no encontrado o no comprobable en T001W; no valida centro destino' ).
   ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  LOOP AT it_g_poslink INTO wa_l_link WHERE clase = 'COMPONENTE'.
   v_l_result = origin_exists( v_i_table = 'MARA' v_i_field = 'MATNR'
    v_i_value = CONV string( wa_l_link-material ) ).
   IF v_l_result = 'X' AND wa_l_link-werks IS NOT INITIAL.
    v_l_extra = |WERKS = { literal( CONV string( wa_l_link-werks ) ) }|.
    v_l_result = origin_exists( v_i_table = 'MARC' v_i_field = 'MATNR'
     v_i_value = CONV string( wa_l_link-material ) v_i_extra = v_l_extra ).
   ELSEIF wa_l_link-werks IS INITIAL. v_l_result = 'U'. ENDIF.
   IF v_l_result <> 'X'.
    v_l_type = 'E'. IF v_l_result = 'U'. v_l_type = 'W'. ENDIF.
    plantilla_issue( v_i_order = wa_l_link-aufnr v_i_sheet = 'Posicion'
     v_i_position = CONV string( wa_l_link-posicion ) v_i_field = 'RESB-MATNR/WERKS'
     v_i_value = |{ wa_l_link-material }/{ wa_l_link-werks }| v_i_type = v_l_type
     v_i_message = 'Material/centro no encontrado o no comprobable en MARA/MARC de ECC; destino pendiente' ).
   ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  LOOP AT it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet>.
   IF <fs_sheet>-number <> 3 AND <fs_sheet>-number <> 5. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
   LOOP AT <fs_sheet>-rows INTO wa_l_row.
    CLEAR v_l_position. IF <fs_sheet>-number = 5. v_l_position = wa_l_row-c02. ENDIF.
    IF <fs_sheet>-number = 3.
     IF wa_l_row-c06 IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
     v_l_result = origin_exists( v_i_table = 'TCURC' v_i_field = 'WAERS' v_i_value = wa_l_row-c06 ).
     IF v_l_result <> 'X'.
      v_l_type = 'E'. IF v_l_result = 'U'. v_l_type = 'W'. ENDIF.
      plantilla_issue( v_i_order = CONV aufnr( wa_l_row-c01 ) v_i_sheet = 'Cab.' v_i_position = ''
       v_i_field = 'TRANSACTIONCURRENCY' v_i_value = wa_l_row-c06 v_i_type = v_l_type
       v_i_message = 'Moneda no encontrada o no comprobable en TCURC origen' ).
     ENDIF.
    ELSE.
     DO 2 TIMES.
      IF sy-index = 1. v_l_idx = 8. ELSE. v_l_idx = 10. ENDIF.
      v_l_col = v_l_idx. v_l_field = 'C' && v_l_col.
      ASSIGN COMPONENT v_l_field OF STRUCTURE wa_l_row TO <fs_cell>.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR <fs_cell> IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
      v_l_value = <fs_cell>.
      v_l_result = origin_exists( v_i_table = 'T006' v_i_field = 'MSEHI' v_i_value = v_l_value ).
      IF v_l_result <> 'X'.
       v_l_other = origin_exists( v_i_table = 'T006I' v_i_field = 'ISOCODE' v_i_value = v_l_value ).
       IF v_l_other = 'X'. v_l_result = 'X'.
       ELSEIF v_l_other = 'U'. v_l_result = 'U'. ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      IF v_l_result <> 'X'.
       v_l_type = 'E'. IF v_l_result = 'U'. v_l_type = 'W'. ENDIF.
       plantilla_issue( v_i_order = CONV aufnr( wa_l_row-c01 ) v_i_sheet = 'Posicion' v_i_position = v_l_position
        v_i_field = v_l_field v_i_value = v_l_value v_i_type = v_l_type
        v_i_message = 'Unidad no encontrada o no comprobable en T006/T006I origen' ).
      ENDIF.
     ENDDO.
    ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
 ENDMETHOD.


* @019 Auditoria de confirmaciones; no cambia trabajo BAPI ni F9 literal del anexo.
 METHOD confirmations_audit.
  TYPES: BEGIN OF ty_reverse, rueck TYPE afru-rueck, rmzhl TYPE afru-rmzhl,
   cantidad TYPE i, END OF ty_reverse.
  DATA: it_l_reverse TYPE HASHED TABLE OF ty_reverse WITH UNIQUE KEY rueck rmzhl,
   wa_l_reverse TYPE ty_reverse, v_l_first TYPE sy-tabix, v_l_last TYPE sy-tabix,
   v_l_new TYPE abap_bool.
  DATA: it_l_orders TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF aufk, wa_l_order TYPE aufk,
   it_l_afko TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afko, wa_l_afko TYPE afko,
   it_l_afvc TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afvc, wa_l_afvc TYPE afvc,
   it_l_afvv TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afvv, wa_l_afvv TYPE afvv,
   it_l_afru TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afru, wa_l_afru TYPE afru,
   it_l_by_counter TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afru, wa_l_peer TYPE afru,
   wa_l_audit TYPE ty_conf_audit, wa_l_sum TYPE ty_conf_sum,
   wa_l_diag TYPE ty_diag, v_lr TYPE REF TO data,
   v_l_peers TYPE i, v_l_index TYPE sy-tabix, v_l_problem TYPE abap_bool,
   v_l_delta TYPE decfloat34, v_l_same_unit TYPE abap_bool,
   v_l_input TYPE f, v_l_output TYPE f, v_l_total TYPE decfloat34,
   v_l_converted TYPE decfloat34, v_l_convert_fail TYPE abap_bool,
   v_l_review TYPE abap_bool.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_t> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_sum> TYPE ty_conf_sum,
   <fs_reverse> TYPE ty_reverse.
  it_l_orders = it_orders.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'AFKO' it_parent = it_l_orders
   v_i_source = 'AUFNR' v_i_target = 'AUFNR' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_afko = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'AFVC' it_parent = it_l_afko
   v_i_source = 'AUFPL' v_i_target = 'AUFPL' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_afvc = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'AFVV' it_parent = it_l_afvc
   v_i_source = 'AUFPL' v_i_target = 'AUFPL' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_afvv = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'AFRU' it_parent = it_l_orders
   v_i_source = 'AUFNR' v_i_target = 'AUFNR' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_afru = <fs_t>.
  SORT it_l_afko BY aufpl. SORT it_l_afvc BY aufpl aplzl.
  SORT it_l_afvv BY aufpl aplzl. SORT it_l_afru BY aufpl aplzl rueck rmzhl.
  it_l_by_counter = it_l_afru. SORT it_l_by_counter BY rueck rmzhl.
  LOOP AT it_l_afru INTO wa_l_afru WHERE stzhl IS NOT INITIAL AND stokz = space.
   READ TABLE it_l_reverse ASSIGNING <fs_reverse> WITH TABLE KEY
    rueck = wa_l_afru-rueck rmzhl = wa_l_afru-stzhl.
   IF sy-subrc = 0. ADD 1 TO <fs_reverse>-cantidad.
   ELSE.
    CLEAR wa_l_reverse. wa_l_reverse-rueck = wa_l_afru-rueck.
    wa_l_reverse-rmzhl = wa_l_afru-stzhl. wa_l_reverse-cantidad = 1.
    INSERT wa_l_reverse INTO TABLE it_l_reverse.
   ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  LOOP AT it_l_afru INTO wa_l_afru.
   CLEAR wa_l_audit.
   wa_l_audit-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_audit-batch = v_g_batch.
   MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_l_afru TO wa_l_audit.
   wa_l_audit-trabajo = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_afru v_i_field = 'ISMNW' ).
   wa_l_audit-unidad = wa_l_afru-ismne.
   wa_l_audit-estado = 'VIGENTE'. wa_l_audit-incluido = abap_true.
   IF wa_l_afru-stokz IS NOT INITIAL AND wa_l_afru-stzhl IS NOT INITIAL.
    wa_l_audit-estado = 'CADENA_ANULACION_REVISAR'. CLEAR wa_l_audit-incluido.
   ELSEIF wa_l_afru-stokz IS NOT INITIAL.
    wa_l_audit-estado = 'ORIGINAL_ANULADA'. CLEAR wa_l_audit-incluido.
    CLEAR v_l_peers.
    READ TABLE it_l_reverse INTO wa_l_reverse WITH TABLE KEY
     rueck = wa_l_afru-rueck rmzhl = wa_l_afru-rmzhl.
    IF sy-subrc = 0. v_l_peers = wa_l_reverse-cantidad. ENDIF.
    IF v_l_peers <> 1. wa_l_audit-estado = 'ANULADA_PAREJA_NO_UNICA'. ENDIF.
   ELSEIF wa_l_afru-stzhl IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR wa_l_audit-incluido.
    READ TABLE it_l_by_counter INTO wa_l_peer WITH KEY
     rueck = wa_l_afru-rueck rmzhl = wa_l_afru-stzhl BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc = 0 AND wa_l_peer-stokz IS NOT INITIAL AND wa_l_peer-stzhl IS INITIAL.
     wa_l_audit-estado = 'REGISTRO_ANULACION'.
    ELSE. wa_l_audit-estado = 'ANULACION_SIN_ORIGINAL'. ENDIF.
   ENDIF.
   READ TABLE it_l_afvc INTO wa_l_afvc WITH KEY
    aufpl = wa_l_afru-aufpl aplzl = wa_l_afru-aplzl BINARY SEARCH.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    wa_l_audit-estado = 'SIN_OPERACION'. CLEAR wa_l_audit-incluido.
   ELSE.
    READ TABLE it_l_afko INTO wa_l_afko WITH KEY aufpl = wa_l_afru-aufpl BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR wa_l_afko-aufnr <> wa_l_afru-aufnr.
     wa_l_audit-estado = 'ORDEN_INCONSISTENTE'. CLEAR wa_l_audit-incluido.
    ENDIF.
   ENDIF.
   wa_l_audit-evidencia = |RUECK/RMZHL { wa_l_afru-rueck }/{ wa_l_afru-rmzhl }; | &&
    |STOKZ={ wa_l_afru-stokz }; STZHL={ wa_l_afru-stzhl }. Solo auditoria; no modifica datos SAP.|.
   APPEND wa_l_audit TO it_g_conf_audit.
  ENDLOOP.
  SORT it_g_conf_audit BY aufpl aplzl unidad rueck rmzhl.
  LOOP AT it_l_afvc INTO wa_l_afvc.
   v_l_first = lines( it_g_conf_sum ) + 1.
   UNASSIGN <fs_sum>.
   READ TABLE it_l_afko INTO wa_l_afko WITH KEY aufpl = wa_l_afvc-aufpl BINARY SEARCH.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
   READ TABLE it_l_afvv INTO wa_l_afvv WITH KEY
    aufpl = wa_l_afvc-aufpl aplzl = wa_l_afvc-aplzl BINARY SEARCH.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. CLEAR wa_l_afvv. ENDIF.
   CLEAR: v_l_problem, v_l_index.
   READ TABLE it_g_conf_audit TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY
    aufpl = wa_l_afvc-aufpl aplzl = wa_l_afvc-aplzl BINARY SEARCH.
   IF sy-subrc = 0. v_l_index = sy-tabix. ENDIF.
   IF v_l_index > 0.
    LOOP AT it_g_conf_audit INTO wa_l_audit FROM v_l_index.
     IF wa_l_audit-aufpl <> wa_l_afvc-aufpl OR wa_l_audit-aplzl <> wa_l_afvc-aplzl. EXIT. ENDIF.
     v_l_new = abap_false.
     IF <fs_sum> IS NOT ASSIGNED. v_l_new = abap_true.
     ELSEIF <fs_sum>-unidad <> wa_l_audit-unidad. v_l_new = abap_true. ENDIF.
     IF v_l_new = abap_true.
      CLEAR wa_l_sum. wa_l_sum-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_sum-batch = v_g_batch.
      wa_l_sum-aufnr = wa_l_afko-aufnr. wa_l_sum-aufpl = wa_l_afvc-aufpl.
      wa_l_sum-aplzl = wa_l_afvc-aplzl. wa_l_sum-vornr = wa_l_afvc-vornr.
      wa_l_sum-unidad = wa_l_audit-unidad. APPEND wa_l_sum TO it_g_conf_sum.
      READ TABLE it_g_conf_sum ASSIGNING <fs_sum> INDEX lines( it_g_conf_sum ).
     ENDIF.
     ADD 1 TO <fs_sum>-registros.
     CASE wa_l_audit-estado.
      WHEN 'VIGENTE'.
       ADD 1 TO <fs_sum>-vigentes.
       READ TABLE it_l_by_counter INTO wa_l_peer WITH KEY
        rueck = wa_l_audit-rueck rmzhl = wa_l_audit-rmzhl BINARY SEARCH.
       IF sy-subrc = 0. <fs_sum>-suma_vigentes = <fs_sum>-suma_vigentes + wa_l_peer-ismnw. ENDIF.
      WHEN 'ORIGINAL_ANULADA'. ADD 1 TO <fs_sum>-anuladas.
      WHEN 'REGISTRO_ANULACION'. ADD 1 TO <fs_sum>-anulaciones.
      WHEN OTHERS. ADD 1 TO <fs_sum>-revisar.
     ENDCASE.
    ENDLOOP.
   ELSE.
    CLEAR wa_l_sum. wa_l_sum-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_sum-batch = v_g_batch.
    wa_l_sum-aufnr = wa_l_afko-aufnr. wa_l_sum-aufpl = wa_l_afvc-aufpl.
    wa_l_sum-aplzl = wa_l_afvc-aplzl. wa_l_sum-vornr = wa_l_afvc-vornr.
    wa_l_sum-unidad = wa_l_afvv-arbeh. APPEND wa_l_sum TO it_g_conf_sum.
   ENDIF.
   v_l_last = lines( it_g_conf_sum ).
* @020 Normalizar cada subtotal a ARBEH antes de conciliar la operacion completa.
   CLEAR: v_l_total, v_l_convert_fail, v_l_review.
   LOOP AT it_g_conf_sum ASSIGNING <fs_sum> FROM v_l_first TO v_l_last.
    CLEAR: <fs_sum>-trabajo_afvv, <fs_sum>-diferencia,
     <fs_sum>-suma_convertida, <fs_sum>-total_operacion.
    <fs_sum>-unidad_afvv = wa_l_afvv-arbeh.
    IF <fs_sum>-revisar > 0. v_l_review = abap_true. ENDIF.
    IF wa_l_afvv-aufpl IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    <fs_sum>-trabajo_afvv = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_afvv v_i_field = 'ISMNW' ).
    CLEAR: v_l_input, v_l_output, v_l_converted.
    IF <fs_sum>-unidad IS INITIAL OR wa_l_afvv-arbeh IS INITIAL.
     v_l_convert_fail = abap_true. CONTINUE.
    ELSEIF <fs_sum>-unidad = wa_l_afvv-arbeh.
     v_l_converted = <fs_sum>-suma_vigentes.
    ELSE.
     v_l_input = <fs_sum>-suma_vigentes.
     CALL FUNCTION 'UNIT_CONVERSION_SIMPLE'
      EXPORTING input = v_l_input unit_in = <fs_sum>-unidad
       unit_out = wa_l_afvv-arbeh no_type_check = abap_true
       round_sign = space
      IMPORTING output = v_l_output
      EXCEPTIONS conversion_not_found = 1 division_by_zero = 2
       input_invalid = 3 output_invalid = 4 overflow = 5
       type_invalid = 6 units_missing = 7 unit_in_not_found = 8
       unit_out_not_found = 9 OTHERS = 10.
     IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      v_l_convert_fail = abap_true. CONTINUE.
     ENDIF.
     v_l_converted = CONV decfloat34( v_l_output ).
    ENDIF.
    <fs_sum>-suma_convertida = v_l_converted.
    v_l_total = v_l_total + v_l_converted.
   ENDLOOP.
* Misma conciliacion por operacion en sus filas de unidades originales; no sumar esos totales repetidos.
   LOOP AT it_g_conf_sum ASSIGNING <fs_sum> FROM v_l_first TO v_l_last.
    IF wa_l_afvv-aufpl IS INITIAL. <fs_sum>-estado = 'SIN_AFVV'.
    ELSEIF v_l_review = abap_true. <fs_sum>-estado = 'ANULACIONES_REVISAR'.
    ELSEIF v_l_convert_fail = abap_true. <fs_sum>-estado = 'UNIDADES_NO_COMPARABLES'.
    ELSE.
     <fs_sum>-total_operacion = v_l_total.
* ISMNW tiene tres decimales; redondear solo el total convertido, no cada confirmacion.
     v_l_delta = round( val = v_l_total dec = 3 ) - wa_l_afvv-ismnw.
     <fs_sum>-diferencia = |{ v_l_delta NUMBER = RAW }|.
     IF v_l_delta = 0. <fs_sum>-estado = 'CUADRA_AFVV'.
     ELSE. <fs_sum>-estado = 'DIFERENCIA_REVISAR'. ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
  LOOP AT it_l_orders INTO wa_l_order.
   CLEAR wa_l_diag. wa_l_diag-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_diag-batch = v_g_batch.
   wa_l_diag-aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr. wa_l_diag-entidad = 'CONFIRMACIONES'.
   READ TABLE it_g_conf_audit TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr.
   IF sy-subrc = 0. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'AUDITORIA_DISPONIBLE'.
   ELSE. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'SIN_AFRU'. ENDIF.
   wa_l_diag-detalle = 'Ver CONFIRMACIONES y CONTROL_TRABAJO; suma vigente AFRU no reemplaza AFVV/BAPI ni representa WIP'.
   APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_g_diag.
  ENDLOOP.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD confirmations_save.
  DATA: it_l_lines TYPE ty_strings, v_l_binary TYPE xstring.
  IF it_g_conf_audit IS NOT INITIAL.
   it_l_lines = serialize( it_g_conf_audit ).
   emit( v_i_entity = 'CONFIRMACIONES' v_i_kind = 'TXT'
    v_i_records = lines( it_g_conf_audit ) v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
  ENDIF.
  IF it_g_conf_sum IS NOT INITIAL.
   it_l_lines = serialize( it_g_conf_sum ).
   emit( v_i_entity = 'CONTROL_TRABAJO' v_i_kind = 'TXT'
    v_i_records = lines( it_g_conf_sum ) v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
  ENDIF.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD positions_pending.
  DATA: it_l_orders TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF aufk, wa_l_order TYPE aufk,
   it_l_afko TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afko, wa_l_afko TYPE afko,
   it_l_afvc TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afvc, wa_l_afvc TYPE afvc,
   it_l_afvv TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afvv, wa_l_afvv TYPE afvv,
   it_l_jest TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF jest, wa_l_jest TYPE jest,
   it_l_resb TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF resb, wa_l_resb TYPE resb,
   it_l_makt TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF makt, wa_l_makt TYPE makt,
   it_l_ops TYPE ty_bapi_operations, wa_l_op TYPE bapi_alm_order_operation_e,
   wa_l_match TYPE bapi_alm_order_operation_e,
   wa_l_wip TYPE ty_wip, wa_l_row TYPE ty_row, wa_l_link TYPE ty_poslink,
   wa_l_diag TYPE ty_diag, it_l_keys TYPE ty_textkeys, wa_l_key TYPE ty_textkey,
   v_lr TYPE REF TO data, v_l_no TYPE i, v_l_matches TYPE i, v_l_afmatches TYPE i,
   v_l_cnf TYPE abap_bool, v_l_del TYPE abap_bool, v_l_ok TYPE abap_bool,
   v_l_pending TYPE resb-bdmng, v_l_ops TYPE i, v_l_comp TYPE i,
   v_l_read TYPE i, v_l_final TYPE i, v_l_deleted TYPE i, v_l_unknown TYPE i.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_t> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_sheet> TYPE ty_sheet,
   <fs_diag> TYPE ty_diag.
  it_l_orders = it_orders.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'AFKO' it_parent = it_l_orders
   v_i_source = 'AUFNR' v_i_target = 'AUFNR' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_afko = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'AFVC' it_parent = it_l_afko
   v_i_source = 'AUFPL' v_i_target = 'AUFPL' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_afvc = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'AFVV' it_parent = it_l_afvc
   v_i_source = 'AUFPL' v_i_target = 'AUFPL' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_afvv = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'JEST' it_parent = it_l_afvc
   v_i_source = 'OBJNR' v_i_target = 'OBJNR' v_i_extra = |INACT = ' '| ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_jest = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'RESB' it_parent = it_l_afko
   v_i_source = 'RSNUM' v_i_target = 'RSNUM' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_resb = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'MAKT' it_parent = it_l_resb
   v_i_source = 'MATNR' v_i_target = 'MATNR'
   v_i_extra = |SPRAS = { literal( CONV string( sy-langu ) ) }| ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_makt = <fs_t>.
  SORT it_l_afvc BY aufpl vornr aplzl.
  SORT it_l_afvv BY aufpl aplzl.
  SORT it_l_resb BY rsnum rspos.
  diagnostics( it_orders = it_l_orders it_operations = it_l_afvc ).
  IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
  LOOP AT it_g_diag ASSIGNING <fs_diag> WHERE entidad = 'OPERACION'.
   READ TABLE it_l_afvc INTO wa_l_afvc WITH KEY objnr = <fs_diag>-clave.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
   READ TABLE it_l_afko INTO wa_l_afko WITH KEY aufpl = wa_l_afvc-aufpl.
   IF sy-subrc = 0. <fs_diag>-aufnr = wa_l_afko-aufnr. ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet> WITH KEY number = 5.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
  LOOP AT it_l_orders INTO wa_l_order.
   CLEAR: v_l_no, v_l_ops, v_l_comp, v_l_read, v_l_final, v_l_deleted, v_l_unknown.
   READ TABLE it_g_wip INTO wa_l_wip WITH KEY aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR wa_l_wip-escenario <> 'B'.
    log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'POSICION' v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
     v_i_message = 'A.5: posiciones solo para escenario B; escenario distinto o indeterminado, no se generan' ).
    CONTINUE.
   ENDIF.
   READ TABLE it_l_afko INTO wa_l_afko WITH KEY aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'POSICION' v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
     v_i_message = 'Sin AFKO: no se pueden relacionar operaciones y reserva para A.5' ).
    CONTINUE.
   ENDIF.
   bapi_operations( EXPORTING v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
    IMPORTING it_e_operations = it_l_ops v_e_ok = v_l_ok ).
   IF v_l_ok = abap_false. v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
   IF lines( it_l_ops ) > p_det.
    v_g_failed = abap_true.
    log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'POSICION' v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
     v_i_message = 'Operaciones BAPI superan P_DET; paquete no exportado' ). RETURN.
   ENDIF.
   LOOP AT it_l_afvc INTO wa_l_afvc WHERE aufpl = wa_l_afko-aufpl.
    ADD 1 TO v_l_read.
    CLEAR: v_l_cnf, v_l_del.
    CLEAR wa_l_diag. wa_l_diag-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_diag-batch = v_g_batch.
    wa_l_diag-aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr. wa_l_diag-entidad = 'SELECCION_OPERACION'.
    wa_l_diag-clave = |{ wa_l_afvc-aufpl }/{ wa_l_afvc-aplzl }|.
    IF wa_l_afvc-objnr IS INITIAL.
     ADD 1 TO v_l_unknown.
     wa_l_diag-resultado = 'OBJNR_AUSENTE'.
     wa_l_diag-detalle = |VORNR { wa_l_afvc-vornr }; no se presume pendiente|.
     APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_g_diag.
     log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'POSICION' v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
      v_i_message = 'Operacion sin OBJNR; no se presume pendiente' ). CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.
    LOOP AT it_l_jest INTO wa_l_jest WHERE objnr = wa_l_afvc-objnr.
     CASE wa_l_jest-stat.
      WHEN 'I0009'. v_l_cnf = abap_true.
      WHEN 'I0013' OR 'I0076'. v_l_del = abap_true.
     ENDCASE.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF v_l_del = abap_true.
     ADD 1 TO v_l_deleted. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'EXCLUIDA_BORRADO'.
    ELSEIF v_l_cnf = abap_true.
     ADD 1 TO v_l_final. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'EXCLUIDA_CNF'.
    ELSE. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'INCLUIDA_PENDIENTE'. ENDIF.
    wa_l_diag-detalle = |VORNR { wa_l_afvc-vornr }; CNF={ v_l_cnf }; DLT/DLFL={ v_l_del }|.
    APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_g_diag.
    IF v_l_cnf = abap_true OR v_l_del = abap_true. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    ADD 1 TO v_l_no. CLEAR: wa_l_row, wa_l_link.
    wa_l_row-c01 = wa_l_order-aufnr.
    wa_l_link-posicion = v_l_no. wa_l_row-c02 = wa_l_link-posicion.
    wa_l_row-c04 = wa_l_afvc-ltxa1. wa_l_row-c05 = iso( sy-langu ).
* Cantidad BAPI solo con correspondencia no ambigua; nunca usar horas como cantidad.
    CLEAR: wa_l_match, v_l_matches, v_l_afmatches.
    LOOP AT it_l_ops INTO wa_l_op WHERE activity = wa_l_afvc-vornr AND sub_activity = space.
     ADD 1 TO v_l_matches. wa_l_match = wa_l_op.
    ENDLOOP.
    LOOP AT it_l_afvc TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
     WHERE aufpl = wa_l_afko-aufpl AND vornr = wa_l_afvc-vornr.
     ADD 1 TO v_l_afmatches.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF v_l_matches = 1 AND v_l_afmatches = 1.
     wa_l_row-c07 = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_match v_i_field = 'QUANTITY' ).
     wa_l_row-c08 = unidad_iso( v_i_unit = CONV string( wa_l_match-base_uom )
      v_i_iso = CONV string( wa_l_match-base_uom_iso ) ).
    ENDIF.
    READ TABLE it_l_afvv INTO wa_l_afvv WITH KEY
     aufpl = wa_l_afvc-aufpl aplzl = wa_l_afvc-aplzl BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
     wa_l_row-c09 = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_afvv v_i_field = 'DAUNO' ).
     wa_l_row-c10 = unidad_iso( v_i_unit = CONV string( wa_l_afvv-daune ) v_i_iso = '' ).
    ELSE. CLEAR wa_l_afvv. ENDIF.
    IF lines( <fs_sheet>-rows ) >= p_det OR v_l_no > 999999.
     v_g_failed = abap_true.
     log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'POSICION'
      v_i_message = 'Limite de posiciones o numeracion excedido; paquete no exportado' ). RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    APPEND wa_l_row TO <fs_sheet>-rows. ADD 1 TO v_l_ops.
    wa_l_link-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_link-batch = v_g_batch.
    wa_l_link-aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr. wa_l_link-clase = 'OPERACION'.
    wa_l_link-werks = wa_l_order-werks.
    wa_l_link-aufpl = wa_l_afvc-aufpl. wa_l_link-aplzl = wa_l_afvc-aplzl.
    wa_l_link-vornr = wa_l_afvc-vornr. wa_l_link-steus = wa_l_afvc-steus.
    wa_l_link-cantidad = wa_l_row-c07. wa_l_link-unidad = wa_l_row-c08.
    wa_l_link-referencia_origen = |AFVC { wa_l_afvc-aufpl }/{ wa_l_afvc-aplzl }; VORNR { wa_l_afvc-vornr }|.
    IF wa_l_afvv-aufpl = wa_l_afvc-aufpl AND wa_l_afvv-aplzl = wa_l_afvc-aplzl.
     wa_l_link-trabajo_previsto = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_afvv v_i_field = 'ARBEI' ).
     wa_l_link-unidad_trabajo = wa_l_afvv-arbeh.
     wa_l_link-duracion_origen = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_afvv v_i_field = 'DAUNO' ).
     wa_l_link-unidad_duracion = wa_l_afvv-daune.
    ENDIF.
    wa_l_link-resultado = 'DATOS_ECC_DIRECTOS'.
    APPEND wa_l_link TO it_g_poslink.
    CLEAR wa_l_key. wa_l_key-aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr. wa_l_key-position = v_l_no.
    wa_l_key-name = sy-mandt && wa_l_afvc-aufpl && wa_l_afvc-aplzl.
    APPEND wa_l_key TO it_l_keys.
    wa_l_key-name = wa_l_afvc-aufpl && wa_l_afvc-aplzl. APPEND wa_l_key TO it_l_keys.
   ENDLOOP.
   LOOP AT it_l_resb INTO wa_l_resb WHERE rsnum = wa_l_afko-rsnum.
    IF wa_l_resb-xloek IS NOT INITIAL OR wa_l_resb-kzear IS NOT INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    v_l_pending = wa_l_resb-bdmng - wa_l_resb-enmng.
    IF v_l_pending <= 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    ADD 1 TO v_l_no. CLEAR: wa_l_row, wa_l_link.
    wa_l_row-c01 = wa_l_order-aufnr. wa_l_link-posicion = v_l_no.
    wa_l_row-c02 = wa_l_link-posicion. wa_l_row-c05 = iso( sy-langu ).
    wa_l_row-c06 = wa_l_resb-matnr.
    wa_l_row-c07 = |{ v_l_pending NUMBER = RAW }|.
    wa_l_row-c08 = unidad_iso( v_i_unit = CONV string( wa_l_resb-meins ) v_i_iso = '' ).
    READ TABLE it_l_makt INTO wa_l_makt WITH KEY matnr = wa_l_resb-matnr.
    IF sy-subrc = 0. wa_l_row-c04 = wa_l_makt-maktx. ENDIF.
    IF lines( <fs_sheet>-rows ) >= p_det OR v_l_no > 999999.
     v_g_failed = abap_true.
     log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'POSICION'
      v_i_message = 'Limite de posiciones o numeracion excedido; paquete no exportado' ). RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    APPEND wa_l_row TO <fs_sheet>-rows. ADD 1 TO v_l_comp.
    wa_l_link-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_link-batch = v_g_batch.
    wa_l_link-aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr. wa_l_link-clase = 'COMPONENTE'.
    wa_l_link-werks = wa_l_resb-werks.
    wa_l_link-rsnum = wa_l_resb-rsnum. wa_l_link-rspos = wa_l_resb-rspos.
    wa_l_link-aufpl = wa_l_resb-aufpl. wa_l_link-aplzl = wa_l_resb-aplzl.
    wa_l_link-material = wa_l_resb-matnr.
    wa_l_link-cantidad = wa_l_row-c07. wa_l_link-unidad = wa_l_row-c08.
    wa_l_link-referencia_origen = |RESB { wa_l_resb-rsnum }/{ wa_l_resb-rspos }|.
    wa_l_link-almacen = wa_l_resb-lgort.
    wa_l_link-requerido = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_resb v_i_field = 'BDMNG' ).
    wa_l_link-retirado = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_resb v_i_field = 'ENMNG' ).
    wa_l_link-resultado = 'MATERIAL_ECC_DIRECTO'.
    READ TABLE it_l_afvc INTO wa_l_afvc WITH KEY
     aufpl = wa_l_resb-aufpl aplzl = wa_l_resb-aplzl.
    IF sy-subrc = 0. wa_l_link-vornr = wa_l_afvc-vornr. ENDIF.
    APPEND wa_l_link TO it_g_poslink.
   ENDLOOP.
   CLEAR wa_l_diag. wa_l_diag-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_diag-batch = v_g_batch.
   wa_l_diag-aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr. wa_l_diag-entidad = 'CONCILIACION_OP'.
   IF v_l_read = v_l_ops + v_l_final + v_l_deleted + v_l_unknown.
    wa_l_diag-resultado = 'CUADRA'.
   ELSE. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'NO_CUADRA'. v_g_failed = abap_true. ENDIF.
   wa_l_diag-detalle = |Leidas { v_l_read } = pendientes { v_l_ops } + CNF { v_l_final } | &&
    |+ borradas { v_l_deleted } + sin OBJNR { v_l_unknown }; componentes generados { v_l_comp }|.
   APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_g_diag.
   log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'POSICION' v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
    v_i_message = |A.5 pendientes: operaciones { v_l_ops }; componentes { v_l_comp }; consecutivo 1..{ v_l_no }| ).
   IF v_l_no > 0.
    log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'MAPEO_POS' v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
     v_i_message = 'A.5 aplicado con valores ECC; campos sin fuente directa permanecen vacios' ).
   ENDIF.
   IF lines( <fs_sheet>-rows ) > p_det.
    log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'POSICION'
     v_i_message = 'Posiciones superan P_DET; paquete no exportado' ).
    v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN.
   ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  notes( it_keys = it_l_keys v_i_id = 'AVOT' v_i_sheet = 7 ).
 ENDMETHOD.


* @022 Datos directos de las operaciones persistidas; sin tablas de homologacion.
 METHOD direct_items.
  DATA: it_l_links TYPE ty_poslinks, wa_l_link TYPE ty_poslink,
   it_l_afvc TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afvc, wa_l_afvc TYPE afvc,
   it_l_part TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ihpa, wa_l_part TYPE ihpa,
   it_l_ops TYPE ty_bapi_operations, wa_l_op TYPE bapi_alm_order_operation_e,
   wa_l_match TYPE bapi_alm_order_operation_e,
   wa_l_row TYPE ty_row, wa_l_diag TYPE ty_diag,
   v_lr TYPE REF TO data, v_l_count TYPE i, v_l_matches TYPE i,
   v_l_ok TYPE abap_bool, v_l_current TYPE aufnr, v_l_n TYPE i.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_t> TYPE STANDARD TABLE,
   <fs_sheet> TYPE ty_sheet, <fs_pos> TYPE ty_sheet, <fs_row> TYPE ty_row.
  it_l_links = it_g_poslink.
  DELETE it_l_links WHERE clase <> 'OPERACION'.
  IF it_l_links IS INITIAL. RETURN. ENDIF.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'AFVC' it_parent = it_l_links
   v_i_source = 'AUFPL' v_i_target = 'AUFPL' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>.
  IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_afvc = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'IHPA' it_parent = it_l_afvc
   v_i_source = 'OBJNR' v_i_target = 'OBJNR' v_i_extra = 'KZLOESCH = '' ''' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>.
  IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_part = <fs_t>.
  DELETE it_l_part WHERE parnr IS INITIAL.
  SORT it_l_part BY objnr parvw parnr.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it_l_part COMPARING objnr parvw parnr.
  READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet> WITH KEY number = 6.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
  READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_pos> WITH KEY number = 5.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
  SORT it_l_links BY aufnr posicion.
  LOOP AT it_l_links INTO wa_l_link.
   READ TABLE it_l_afvc INTO wa_l_afvc WITH KEY
    aufpl = wa_l_link-aufpl aplzl = wa_l_link-aplzl.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
   CLEAR v_l_count.
   LOOP AT it_l_part INTO wa_l_part WHERE objnr = wa_l_afvc-objnr.
    IF wa_l_afvc-objnr IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    CLEAR wa_l_row. wa_l_row-c01 = wa_l_link-aufnr.
    wa_l_row-c02 = wa_l_link-posicion. wa_l_row-c03 = wa_l_part-parvw.
    wa_l_row-c04 = wa_l_part-parnr.
    READ TABLE <fs_sheet>-rows TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY
     c01 = wa_l_row-c01 c02 = wa_l_row-c02 c03 = wa_l_row-c03 c04 = wa_l_row-c04.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
     IF lines( <fs_sheet>-rows ) >= p_det.
      v_g_failed = abap_true.
      log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'IHPA'
       v_i_message = 'Interlocutores de posicion superan P_DET; no exportar paquete' ). RETURN.
     ENDIF.
     APPEND wa_l_row TO <fs_sheet>-rows.
    ENDIF.
    ADD 1 TO v_l_count.
   ENDLOOP.
   CLEAR wa_l_diag. wa_l_diag-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_diag-batch = v_g_batch.
   wa_l_diag-aufnr = wa_l_link-aufnr. wa_l_diag-entidad = 'SOCIOS_POSICION'.
   wa_l_diag-clave = |{ wa_l_link-posicion }; AFVC { wa_l_link-aufpl }/{ wa_l_link-aplzl }|.
   IF wa_l_afvc-objnr IS INITIAL. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'CLAVE_ORIGEN_AUSENTE'.
   ELSEIF v_l_count = 0. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'SIN_REGISTROS_ORIGEN'.
   ELSE. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'DATOS_ECC_COPIADOS'. ENDIF.
   wa_l_diag-detalle = |IHPA OBJNR={ wa_l_afvc-objnr }; socios { v_l_count }; sin conversion a BP|.
   APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_g_diag.
* Personal de ejecucion es un campo de la operacion; no inferir persona responsable de cabecera.
   IF v_l_current <> wa_l_link-aufnr.
    v_l_current = wa_l_link-aufnr.
    bapi_operations( EXPORTING v_i_order = wa_l_link-aufnr
     IMPORTING it_e_operations = it_l_ops v_e_ok = v_l_ok ).
    IF v_l_ok = abap_false. v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
   ENDIF.
   CLEAR: v_l_matches, v_l_n, wa_l_match.
   LOOP AT it_l_afvc TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
    WHERE aufpl = wa_l_link-aufpl AND vornr = wa_l_link-vornr.
    ADD 1 TO v_l_n.
   ENDLOOP.
   LOOP AT it_l_ops INTO wa_l_op WHERE activity = wa_l_link-vornr AND sub_activity = space.
    ADD 1 TO v_l_matches. wa_l_match = wa_l_op.
   ENDLOOP.
   wa_l_diag-entidad = 'PERSONAL_OPERACION'.
   wa_l_diag-resultado = 'RELACION_NO_UNICA'.
   wa_l_diag-detalle = 'No se copia PERS_NO sin correspondencia unica AFVC/BAPI por operacion'.
   IF v_l_n = 1 AND v_l_matches = 1.
    IF wa_l_match-pers_no IS INITIAL.
     wa_l_diag-resultado = 'CAMPO_ORIGEN_VACIO'.
     wa_l_diag-detalle = 'ET_OPERATIONS-PERS_NO no informado; no asignar persona por defecto'.
    ELSE.
     READ TABLE <fs_pos>-rows ASSIGNING <fs_row>
      WITH KEY c01 = wa_l_link-aufnr c02 = wa_l_link-posicion.
     IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF <fs_row>-c18 IS INITIAL. <fs_row>-c18 = wa_l_match-pers_no. ENDIF.
      wa_l_diag-resultado = 'DATOS_ECC_COPIADOS'.
      wa_l_diag-detalle = |ET_OPERATIONS-PERS_NO={ wa_l_match-pers_no }; ID personal ECC sin conversion|.
     ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
   ENDIF.
   APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_g_diag.
  ENDLOOP.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD enrich_bapi.
  DATA: wa_l_order TYPE aufk, wa_l_srv TYPE bapi_alm_order_srvdat_e,
   wa_l_header TYPE bapi_alm_order_header_e,
   it_l_objects TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapi_alm_order_objectlist,
   wa_l_object TYPE bapi_alm_order_objectlist,
   it_l_ops TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapi_alm_order_operation_e,
   wa_l_op TYPE bapi_alm_order_operation_e,
   it_l_texts TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapi_alm_text,
   wa_l_text TYPE bapi_alm_text,
   it_l_lines TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapi_alm_text_lines,
   wa_l_line TYPE bapi_alm_text_lines,
   it_l_return TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapiret2, wa_l_return TYPE bapiret2,
   it_l_part TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ihpa, wa_l_part TYPE ihpa,
   wa_l_row TYPE ty_row, wa_l_note TYPE ty_note,
   v_l_failed TYPE abap_bool, v_l_equipment TYPE string,
   v_l_location TYPE string, v_l_text TYPE string,
   v_l_start TYPE i, v_l_end TYPE i, v_l_pos TYPE n LENGTH 6,
   v_l_sheet TYPE i, v_l_id TYPE thead-tdid, v_l_exists TYPE abap_bool,
   v_l_count TYPE i, wa_l_diag TYPE ty_diag.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_headsheet> TYPE ty_sheet, <fs_sheet> TYPE ty_sheet,
   <fs_row> TYPE ty_row, <fs_head> TYPE ty_head.
  LOOP AT it_orders INTO wa_l_order.
   CLEAR: wa_l_srv, wa_l_header, it_l_objects, it_l_ops,
    it_l_texts, it_l_lines, it_l_return, v_l_failed.
   CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_ALM_ORDER_GET_DETAIL'
    EXPORTING number = wa_l_order-aufnr
    IMPORTING es_header = wa_l_header es_srvdata = wa_l_srv
    TABLES et_operations = it_l_ops et_olist = it_l_objects
     et_texts = it_l_texts et_text_lines = it_l_lines return = it_l_return
    EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. v_l_failed = abap_true. ENDIF.
   LOOP AT it_l_return INTO wa_l_return.
    IF wa_l_return-type CA 'EAX' AND wa_l_return-type IS NOT INITIAL.
     v_l_failed = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
    log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'BAPI_AMPL'
     v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
     v_i_message = |BAPI { wa_l_return-type } { wa_l_return-id }/{ wa_l_return-number }: { wa_l_return-message }| ).
   ENDLOOP.
   IF v_l_failed = abap_true.
    log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'BAPI_AMPL'
     v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr v_i_message = 'Fallo BAPI ampliada; paquete no exportado' ).
    v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN.
   ENDIF.
   IF lines( it_l_ops ) > p_det OR lines( it_l_objects ) > p_det OR
      lines( it_l_texts ) > p_det OR lines( it_l_lines ) > p_det.
    log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'BAPI_AMPL'
     v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr v_i_message = 'BAPI ampliada supera P_DET; paquete no exportado' ).
    v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN.
   ENDIF.
* @016 Evidencia por orden; no confundir opcion desactivada con ausencia de datos.
   CLEAR wa_l_diag. wa_l_diag-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_diag-batch = v_g_batch.
   wa_l_diag-aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr. wa_l_diag-entidad = 'TEXTOS_BAPI'.
   IF it_l_texts IS INITIAL.
    wa_l_diag-resultado = 'SIN_DATOS_BAPI'.
   ELSE.
    wa_l_diag-resultado = 'REVISAR_IDIOMA_TIPO'.
   ENDIF.
   wa_l_diag-detalle = |Textos { lines( it_l_texts ) }; lineas { lines( it_l_lines ) }; | &&
    |idioma { sy-langu }; IDs destino { p_htid }/{ p_itid }. No prueba ausencia en STXH.|.
   APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_g_diag.
   wa_l_diag-entidad = 'OBJETOS_BAPI'.
   IF p_obj IS INITIAL. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'OPCION_DESACTIVADA'.
   ELSEIF it_l_objects IS INITIAL AND wa_l_header-equipment IS INITIAL
    AND wa_l_header-funct_loc IS INITIAL. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'SIN_DATOS_BAPI'.
   ELSE. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'FUENTE_DISPONIBLE'. ENDIF.
   wa_l_diag-detalle = |Lista { lines( it_l_objects ) }; equipo { wa_l_header-equipment }; | &&
    |ubicacion { wa_l_header-funct_loc }; P_OBJ={ p_obj }. No asignar a posiciones sin vinculo.|.
   APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_g_diag.
* Complementar solo datos comerciales ausentes; conservar fuentes existentes.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_headsheet> WITH KEY number = 3.
   READ TABLE <fs_headsheet>-rows ASSIGNING <fs_row> WITH KEY c01 = wa_l_order-aufnr.
   IF sy-subrc = 0.
    IF <fs_row>-c15 IS INITIAL. <fs_row>-c15 = wa_l_srv-purch_no_c. ENDIF.
    IF <fs_row>-c16 IS INITIAL AND wa_l_srv-purch_date IS NOT INITIAL.
     <fs_row>-c16 = wa_l_srv-purch_date.
    ENDIF.
    IF <fs_row>-c17 IS INITIAL. <fs_row>-c17 = wa_l_srv-salesorg. ENDIF.
    IF <fs_row>-c18 IS INITIAL. <fs_row>-c18 = wa_l_srv-distr_chan. ENDIF.
    IF <fs_row>-c19 IS INITIAL. <fs_row>-c19 = wa_l_srv-division. ENDIF.
    IF <fs_row>-c20 IS INITIAL. <fs_row>-c20 = wa_l_srv-sales_off. ENDIF.
    IF <fs_row>-c21 IS INITIAL. <fs_row>-c21 = wa_l_srv-sales_grp. ENDIF.
    READ TABLE it_g_head ASSIGNING <fs_head> WITH KEY aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
     <fs_head>-mc_purch_no = <fs_row>-c15.
     IF <fs_row>-c16 IS NOT INITIAL. <fs_head>-mc_purch_date = <fs_row>-c16. ENDIF.
     <fs_head>-mc_sales_org = <fs_row>-c17.
     <fs_head>-mc_dist_channel = <fs_row>-c18.
     <fs_head>-mc_division = <fs_row>-c19.
     <fs_head>-mc_sales_office = <fs_row>-c20.
     <fs_head>-mc_sales_group = <fs_row>-c21.
    ENDIF.
   ENDIF.
* Producto de cabecera no se reparte entre operaciones sin regla aprobada.
   IF wa_l_srv-material IS NOT INITIAL OR wa_l_srv-material_long IS NOT INITIAL.
    log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'MAPEO_PRODUCTO' v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
     v_i_message = |Producto servicio { wa_l_srv-material_long }/{ wa_l_srv-material }; cantidad { wa_l_srv-quantity } | &&
      |{ wa_l_srv-base_uom_iso }. Dato de cabecera no se reparte entre operaciones sin relacion directa| ).
   ENDIF.
* Objetos optativos: habilitar P_OBJ solo si aplican a este proceso.
   IF p_obj = abap_true.
    CLEAR wa_l_object.
    wa_l_object-equipment = wa_l_header-equipment.
    wa_l_object-funct_loc = wa_l_header-funct_loc.
    INSERT wa_l_object INTO it_l_objects INDEX 1.
    READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet> WITH KEY number = 12.
    LOOP AT it_l_objects INTO wa_l_object.
     IF wa_l_object-equipment IS INITIAL AND wa_l_object-funct_loc IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
     IF wa_l_object-equipment IS NOT INITIAL AND wa_l_object-funct_loc IS NOT INITIAL.
      log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'OBJETO_CAB' v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
       v_i_message = 'Equipo y ubicacion ECC presentes: se conservan en filas separadas sin duplicados' ).
     ENDIF.
     CLEAR wa_l_row. wa_l_row-c01 = wa_l_order-aufnr.
     IF wa_l_object-equipment IS NOT INITIAL. wa_l_row-c02 = wa_l_object-equipment.
     ELSE. wa_l_row-c03 = wa_l_object-funct_loc. ENDIF.
     READ TABLE <fs_sheet>-rows TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY
      c01 = wa_l_row-c01 c02 = wa_l_row-c02 c03 = wa_l_row-c03.
     IF sy-subrc <> 0. APPEND wa_l_row TO <fs_sheet>-rows. ENDIF.
     IF wa_l_object-equipment IS NOT INITIAL AND wa_l_object-funct_loc IS NOT INITIAL.
      CLEAR wa_l_row. wa_l_row-c01 = wa_l_order-aufnr. wa_l_row-c03 = wa_l_object-funct_loc.
      READ TABLE <fs_sheet>-rows TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY
       c01 = wa_l_row-c01 c02 = wa_l_row-c02 c03 = wa_l_row-c03.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0. APPEND wa_l_row TO <fs_sheet>-rows. ENDIF.
     ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet> WITH KEY number = 8.
    LOOP AT it_l_ops INTO wa_l_op.
     IF wa_l_op-sub_activity IS NOT INITIAL OR wa_l_op-activity CN '0123456789'. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
     v_l_equipment = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_op v_i_field = 'EQUIPMENT' ).
     IF v_l_equipment IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
     v_l_pos = position_for_operation(
      v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr v_i_activity = wa_l_op-activity ).
     IF v_l_pos IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
     READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_headsheet> WITH KEY number = 5.
     READ TABLE <fs_headsheet>-rows TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY
      c01 = wa_l_order-aufnr c02 = v_l_pos.
     IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
     CLEAR wa_l_row. wa_l_row-c01 = wa_l_order-aufnr.
     wa_l_row-c02 = v_l_pos. wa_l_row-c03 = v_l_equipment.
     APPEND wa_l_row TO <fs_sheet>-rows.
    ENDLOOP.
   ENDIF.
* Responsable: ZR contrastado en ordenes HN11 y CO11; no elegir otras funciones.
   CLEAR it_l_part.
   SELECT * FROM ihpa INTO TABLE it_l_part WHERE objnr = wa_l_order-objnr
    AND parvw = 'ZR' AND kzloesch = space.
   SORT it_l_part BY parnr. DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it_l_part COMPARING parnr.
   DELETE it_l_part WHERE parnr IS INITIAL.
   CLEAR wa_l_diag. wa_l_diag-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_diag-batch = v_g_batch.
   wa_l_diag-aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr. wa_l_diag-entidad = 'RESPONSABLE'.
   wa_l_diag-clave = 'IHPA-PARVW=ZR'.
   IF lines( it_l_part ) = 1.
    READ TABLE it_l_part INTO wa_l_part INDEX 1.
    READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet> WITH KEY number = 11.
    CLEAR wa_l_row. wa_l_row-c01 = wa_l_order-aufnr. wa_l_row-c02 = wa_l_part-parnr.
    APPEND wa_l_row TO <fs_sheet>-rows.
    wa_l_diag-resultado = 'RESPONSABLE_RECUPERADO'.
   ELSEIF it_l_part IS INITIAL.
    wa_l_diag-resultado = 'SIN_RESPONSABLE_ZR'.
   ELSE.
    wa_l_diag-resultado = 'VARIOS_RESPONSABLES_ZR'.
   ENDIF.
   wa_l_diag-detalle = |Funcion ZR; { lines( it_l_part ) } interlocutores distintos vigentes; sin conversion a empleado/BP|.
   APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_g_diag.
   IF lines( it_l_part ) <> 1.
    log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'RESPONSABLE' v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
     v_i_message = CONV string( wa_l_diag-detalle ) ).
   ENDIF.
* BAPI no informa TDID. Solo habilitar notas con ID destino explicitamente configurado.
   LOOP AT it_l_texts INTO wa_l_text.
    IF wa_l_text-orderid IS NOT INITIAL AND wa_l_text-orderid <> wa_l_order-aufnr. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    IF wa_l_text-langu <> sy-langu. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    IF wa_l_text-sub_activity IS NOT INITIAL OR wa_l_text-reserv_no IS NOT INITIAL
       OR wa_l_text-item_no_of_production_resource IS NOT INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    CLEAR: v_l_pos, v_l_id.
    IF wa_l_text-activity IS INITIAL.
     v_l_sheet = 10. v_l_id = p_htid.
    ELSE.
     IF wa_l_text-activity CN '0123456789'. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
     v_l_sheet = 7. v_l_id = p_itid.
     v_l_pos = position_for_operation(
      v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr v_i_activity = wa_l_text-activity ).
     IF v_l_pos IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
     READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_headsheet> WITH KEY number = 5.
     READ TABLE <fs_headsheet>-rows TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY
      c01 = wa_l_order-aufnr c02 = v_l_pos.
     IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF v_l_id IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
* Evitar mezclar clases de texto existentes o duplicar notas READ_TEXT.
    READ TABLE it_g_notes TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY
     aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr position = v_l_pos language = sy-langu.
    IF sy-subrc = 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    v_l_start = wa_l_text-textstart. v_l_end = wa_l_text-textend.
    IF v_l_start < 1 OR v_l_end < v_l_start OR v_l_end > lines( it_l_lines ).
     log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'TEXTOS_BAPI' v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
      v_i_message = 'Punteros de texto BAPI invalidos; nota no generada' ). CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR v_l_text.
    LOOP AT it_l_lines INTO wa_l_line FROM v_l_start TO v_l_end.
     IF v_l_text IS INITIAL OR wa_l_line-tdformat = '='.
      v_l_text = v_l_text && wa_l_line-tdline.
     ELSE.
      v_l_text = v_l_text && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline && wa_l_line-tdline.
     ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF v_l_text IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet> WITH KEY number = v_l_sheet.
    CLEAR wa_l_row. wa_l_row-c01 = wa_l_order-aufnr.
    IF v_l_sheet = 10.
     wa_l_row-c02 = iso( sy-langu ). wa_l_row-c03 = v_l_id. wa_l_row-c04 = v_l_text.
    ELSE.
     wa_l_row-c02 = v_l_pos. wa_l_row-c03 = iso( sy-langu ).
     wa_l_row-c04 = v_l_id. wa_l_row-c05 = v_l_text.
    ENDIF.
    APPEND wa_l_row TO <fs_sheet>-rows.
    CLEAR wa_l_note. wa_l_note-aufnr = wa_l_order-aufnr.
    wa_l_note-position = v_l_pos. wa_l_note-language = sy-langu.
    wa_l_note-iso = iso( sy-langu ). wa_l_note-tdid = v_l_id.
    wa_l_note-content = v_l_text. APPEND wa_l_note TO it_g_notes.
   ENDLOOP.
   log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'BAPI_AMPL' v_i_order = wa_l_order-aufnr
    v_i_message = |Objetos BAPI { lines( it_l_objects ) }; textos { lines( it_l_texts ) }; P_OBJ={ p_obj }, funcion respo| &&
     |nsable=ZR, IDs destino={ p_htid }/{ p_itid }| ).
  ENDLOOP.
* Cobertura explicita de las diez hojas: no presentar vacios como implementacion completa.
  LOOP AT it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet>.
   IF lines( <fs_sheet>-rows ) > p_det.
    log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'COBERTURA' v_i_message = 'Hoja supera P_DET; no exportar paquete' ).
    v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN.
   ENDIF.
   log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'COBERTURA'
    v_i_message = |Hoja { <fs_sheet>-number } { <fs_sheet>-title }: { lines( <fs_sheet>-rows ) } registros| ).
  ENDLOOP.
  log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'MAPEO_PEND'
   v_i_message = 'Precios: sin fuente directa verificada; no se utilizan costos como condiciones de precio' ).
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD sheets_init.
  DATA wa_l_sheet TYPE ty_sheet.
  CLEAR it_g_sheets.
* @007 plantilla 2025 verificada: filas 1-8 conservadas; datos desde fila 9.
  CLEAR wa_l_sheet.
  wa_l_sheet-number = 3.
  wa_l_sheet-title = 'Cab.'.
  wa_l_sheet-columns = 26.
  wa_l_sheet-startrow = 9. APPEND wa_l_sheet TO it_g_sheets.
  CLEAR wa_l_sheet.
  wa_l_sheet-number = 4.
  wa_l_sheet-title = 'Participante a nivel cabecera'.
  wa_l_sheet-columns = 4.
  wa_l_sheet-startrow = 9. APPEND wa_l_sheet TO it_g_sheets.
  CLEAR wa_l_sheet.
  wa_l_sheet-number = 5.
  wa_l_sheet-title = 'Posición'.
  wa_l_sheet-columns = 23.
  wa_l_sheet-startrow = 9. APPEND wa_l_sheet TO it_g_sheets.
  CLEAR wa_l_sheet.
  wa_l_sheet-number = 6.
  wa_l_sheet-title = 'Participante a nivel posición'.
  wa_l_sheet-columns = 5.
  wa_l_sheet-startrow = 9. APPEND wa_l_sheet TO it_g_sheets.
  CLEAR wa_l_sheet.
  wa_l_sheet-number = 7.
  wa_l_sheet-title = 'Notas de posición'.
  wa_l_sheet-columns = 5.
  wa_l_sheet-startrow = 9. APPEND wa_l_sheet TO it_g_sheets.
  CLEAR wa_l_sheet.
  wa_l_sheet-number = 8.
  wa_l_sheet-title = 'Objetos referencia de posición'.
  wa_l_sheet-columns = 5.
  wa_l_sheet-startrow = 9. APPEND wa_l_sheet TO it_g_sheets.
  CLEAR wa_l_sheet.
  wa_l_sheet-number = 9.
  wa_l_sheet-title = 'Elementos de precio posición'.
  wa_l_sheet-columns = 9.
  wa_l_sheet-startrow = 9. APPEND wa_l_sheet TO it_g_sheets.
  CLEAR wa_l_sheet.
  wa_l_sheet-number = 10.
  wa_l_sheet-title = 'Notas de cabecera'.
  wa_l_sheet-columns = 4.
  wa_l_sheet-startrow = 9. APPEND wa_l_sheet TO it_g_sheets.
  CLEAR wa_l_sheet.
  wa_l_sheet-number = 11.
  wa_l_sheet-title = 'Persona responsable'.
  wa_l_sheet-columns = 2.
  wa_l_sheet-startrow = 9. APPEND wa_l_sheet TO it_g_sheets.
  CLEAR wa_l_sheet.
  wa_l_sheet-number = 12.
  wa_l_sheet-title = 'Objeto de referencia cabecera'.
  wa_l_sheet-columns = 4.
  wa_l_sheet-startrow = 9. APPEND wa_l_sheet TO it_g_sheets.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo workbook: completar los datos conservando la estructura y estilos de la plantilla.
*
 METHOD workbook.
  DATA: v_lo_zip TYPE REF TO cl_abap_zip, v_l_content TYPE xstring,
   v_l_xml TYPE string, v_l_path TYPE string, v_l_rows TYPE string,
   v_l_add TYPE string, v_l_rownum TYPE i, v_l_col TYPE i,
   v_l_field TYPE string, v_l_letter TYPE c LENGTH 1,
   v_l_idx TYPE n LENGTH 2, v_l_value TYPE string,
   wa_l_row TYPE ty_row, v_l_prefix TYPE string, v_l_suffix TYPE string,
   v_l_offset TYPE i, v_l_length TYPE i, v_l_end TYPE i,
   v_l_from TYPE i, v_l_to TYPE i, v_l_style TYPE string,
   v_l_type TYPE char1, v_l_number TYPE string, v_l_regex TYPE string.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_sheet> TYPE ty_sheet, <fs_cell> TYPE any.
  CREATE OBJECT v_lo_zip.
  v_lo_zip->load( EXPORTING zip = v_g_template EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1 ).
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. MESSAGE e007(zsm_os). ENDIF.
  LOOP AT it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet>.
   IF <fs_sheet>-rows IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
   v_l_path = |xl/worksheets/sheet{ <fs_sheet>-number }.xml|.
   v_lo_zip->get( EXPORTING name = v_l_path IMPORTING content = v_l_content
    EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1 ).
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. MESSAGE e008(zsm_os). ENDIF.
   v_l_xml = cl_abap_codepage=>convert_from( source = v_l_content ).
   FIND FIRST OCCURRENCE OF '<sheetData>' IN v_l_xml MATCH OFFSET v_l_offset.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. MESSAGE e008(zsm_os). ENDIF.
   v_l_from = v_l_offset + strlen( '<sheetData>' ).
   FIND FIRST OCCURRENCE OF '</sheetData>' IN v_l_xml MATCH OFFSET v_l_to.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. MESSAGE e008(zsm_os). ENDIF.
   v_l_prefix = v_l_xml(v_l_from). v_l_suffix = v_l_xml+v_l_to.
   v_l_length = v_l_to - v_l_from. v_l_rows = v_l_xml+v_l_from(v_l_length).
*
* conservar documentacion; sustituir solo filas de datos de la copia.
*
   v_l_rownum = <fs_sheet>-startrow.
   FIND FIRST OCCURRENCE OF REGEX '<row r="([0-9]+)"' IN v_l_rows.
   v_l_end = strlen( v_l_rows ).
   DATA it_l_matches TYPE match_result_tab.
   DATA wa_l_match LIKE LINE OF it_l_matches.
   FIND ALL OCCURRENCES OF REGEX '<row r="[0-9]+"' IN v_l_rows RESULTS it_l_matches.
   LOOP AT it_l_matches INTO wa_l_match.
    v_l_add = v_l_rows+wa_l_match-offset(wa_l_match-length).
    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF REGEX '[^0-9]' IN v_l_add WITH ''.
    IF CONV i( v_l_add ) >= <fs_sheet>-startrow.
     v_l_end = wa_l_match-offset. EXIT.
    ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
   v_l_rows = v_l_rows(v_l_end).
   LOOP AT <fs_sheet>-rows INTO wa_l_row.
    v_l_add = |<row r="{ v_l_rownum }">|.
    DO <fs_sheet>-columns TIMES.
     v_l_col = sy-index. v_l_idx = v_l_col.
     v_l_field = 'C' && v_l_idx.
     ASSIGN COMPONENT v_l_field OF STRUCTURE wa_l_row TO <fs_cell>.
     v_l_value = <fs_cell>.
     IF v_l_value IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
     v_l_offset = v_l_col - 1.
     v_l_letter = sy-abcde+v_l_offset(1).
     CLEAR v_l_style.
     v_l_regex = |<c r="{ v_l_letter }{ <fs_sheet>-startrow }"[^>]* s="([0-9]+)"|.
     FIND FIRST OCCURRENCE OF REGEX v_l_regex IN v_l_xml SUBMATCHES v_l_style.
     IF v_l_style IS NOT INITIAL. v_l_style = | s="{ v_l_style }"|. ENDIF.
     v_l_type = 's'.
     IF <fs_sheet>-number = 5 AND ( v_l_col = 2 OR v_l_col = 3 OR
        v_l_col = 7 OR v_l_col = 9 OR v_l_col = 20 OR v_l_col = 22 ). v_l_type = 'n'. ENDIF.
     IF ( <fs_sheet>-number = 6 OR <fs_sheet>-number = 7 OR <fs_sheet>-number = 8
        OR <fs_sheet>-number = 9 ) AND v_l_col = 2. v_l_type = 'n'. ENDIF.
     IF <fs_sheet>-number = 9 AND ( v_l_col = 3 OR v_l_col = 4 OR v_l_col = 6 OR v_l_col = 8 ). v_l_type = 'n'. ENDIF.
     IF <fs_sheet>-number = 3 AND ( v_l_col = 9 OR v_l_col = 12 OR v_l_col = 16 ). v_l_type = 'd'. ENDIF.
     CASE v_l_type.
      WHEN 'n'.
       v_l_add = v_l_add && |<c r="{ v_l_letter }{ v_l_rownum }"{ v_l_style }><v>{ v_l_value }</v></c>|.
      WHEN 'd'.
       v_l_number = v_l_value(4) && '-' && v_l_value+4(2) && '-' && v_l_value+6(2).
       v_l_add = v_l_add && |<c r="{ v_l_letter }{ v_l_rownum }"{ v_l_style } t="d"><v>{ v_l_number }</v></c>|.
      WHEN OTHERS.
       IF strlen( v_l_value ) > 32767.
        log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'XLSX'
         v_i_message = 'Texto supera 32767 caracteres de Excel; no se trunca' ).
        v_g_failed = abap_true. CLEAR v_r_binary. RETURN.
       ENDIF.
       v_l_value = xml( v_l_value ).
       v_l_add = v_l_add && |<c r="{ v_l_letter }{ v_l_rownum }"{ v_l_style } t="inlineStr"><is>| &&
        '<t xml:space="preserve">' && v_l_value && '</t></is></c>'.
     ENDCASE.
    ENDDO.
    v_l_rows = v_l_rows && v_l_add && '</row>'. ADD 1 TO v_l_rownum.
   ENDLOOP.
   v_l_xml = v_l_prefix && v_l_rows && v_l_suffix.
   v_l_end = v_l_rownum - 1.
   v_l_offset = <fs_sheet>-columns - 1.
   IF v_l_offset < 14. v_l_offset = 14. ENDIF.
   v_l_letter = sy-abcde+v_l_offset(1).
   v_l_add = |<dimension ref="A1:{ v_l_letter }{ v_l_end }"/>|.
   REPLACE FIRST OCCURRENCE OF REGEX '<dimension[^>]*/>' IN v_l_xml WITH v_l_add.
   v_l_content = cl_abap_codepage=>convert_to( source = v_l_xml ).
   v_lo_zip->delete( EXPORTING name = v_l_path EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1 ).
   v_lo_zip->add( name = v_l_path content = v_l_content ).
  ENDLOOP.
  v_r_binary = v_lo_zip->save( ).
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo notes: comprobar las claves de texto y recuperar las notas con la funcion estandar.
*
 METHOD notes.
  DATA: v_lr TYPE REF TO data, it_l_stxh TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF stxh,
   wa_l_stxh TYPE stxh, wa_l_key TYPE ty_textkey,
   wa_l_note TYPE ty_note, it_l_text TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF tline,
   wa_l_text TYPE tline, wa_l_row TYPE ty_row,
   v_l_extra TYPE string, v_l_limit TYPE i.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_t> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_sheet> TYPE ty_sheet.
  v_l_extra = |TDOBJECT = 'AUFK' AND TDID = { literal( CONV string( v_i_id ) ) }| &&
   | AND TDSPRAS = { literal( CONV string( sy-langu ) ) }|.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'STXH' it_parent = it_keys v_i_source = 'NAME'
   v_i_target = 'TDNAME' v_i_extra = v_l_extra ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_stxh = <fs_t>.
  READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet> WITH KEY number = v_i_sheet.
  LOOP AT it_l_stxh INTO wa_l_stxh.
   READ TABLE it_keys INTO wa_l_key WITH KEY name = wa_l_stxh-tdname.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
   CLEAR: it_l_text, wa_l_note.
   CALL FUNCTION 'READ_TEXT'
    EXPORTING object = wa_l_stxh-tdobject id = wa_l_stxh-tdid
      name = wa_l_stxh-tdname language = wa_l_stxh-tdspras
    TABLES lines = it_l_text EXCEPTIONS not_found = 4 OTHERS = 8.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'TEXTOS' v_i_order = wa_l_key-aufnr
     v_i_message = 'No se pudo leer texto encontrado en STXH' ). CONTINUE.
   ENDIF.
   wa_l_note-aufnr = wa_l_key-aufnr. wa_l_note-position = wa_l_key-position.
   wa_l_note-tdobject = wa_l_stxh-tdobject. wa_l_note-tdname = wa_l_stxh-tdname.
   wa_l_note-tdid = wa_l_stxh-tdid. wa_l_note-language = wa_l_stxh-tdspras.
   wa_l_note-iso = iso( wa_l_stxh-tdspras ).
   LOOP AT it_l_text INTO wa_l_text.
    IF wa_l_text-tdformat = '='.
     wa_l_note-content = wa_l_note-content && wa_l_text-tdline.
    ELSEIF wa_l_note-content IS INITIAL.
     wa_l_note-content = wa_l_text-tdline.
    ELSE.
     wa_l_note-content = wa_l_note-content && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline && wa_l_text-tdline.
    ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
   IF wa_l_note-iso IS INITIAL.
    log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'TEXTOS' v_i_order = wa_l_note-aufnr
     v_i_message = 'Idioma sin codigo ISO; no se entrega una nota sin idioma' ).
    v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN.
   ENDIF.
   IF wa_l_note-content IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
   CLEAR wa_l_row.
   wa_l_row-c01 = wa_l_note-aufnr.
   IF v_i_sheet = 10.
    wa_l_row-c02 = wa_l_note-iso. wa_l_row-c03 = wa_l_note-tdid. wa_l_row-c04 = wa_l_note-content.
   ELSE.
    wa_l_row-c02 = wa_l_note-position. wa_l_row-c03 = wa_l_note-iso.
    wa_l_row-c04 = wa_l_note-tdid. wa_l_row-c05 = wa_l_note-content.
   ENDIF.
   APPEND wa_l_row TO <fs_sheet>-rows.
   READ TABLE it_g_head TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY aufnr = wa_l_note-aufnr.
   IF sy-subrc = 0. APPEND wa_l_note TO it_g_notes. ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF it_l_stxh IS INITIAL AND it_keys IS NOT INITIAL.
   log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'TEXTOS'
    v_i_message = |Sin STXH AUFK/{ v_i_id } para claves candidatas; validar cabecera real del texto| ).
  ENDIF.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo exports: generar las salidas de las hojas con datos del paquete.
*

* @016 Normalizacion tecnica de unidad; nunca inventar unidad si origen esta vacio.
 METHOD unidad_iso.
  DATA: v_l_unit TYPE t006-msehi, v_l_iso TYPE t006-isocode.
  v_r_unit = v_i_iso.
  IF v_r_unit IS NOT INITIAL OR v_i_unit IS INITIAL. RETURN. ENDIF.
  v_l_unit = v_i_unit.
  SELECT SINGLE isocode INTO v_l_iso FROM t006 WHERE msehi = v_l_unit.
  IF sy-subrc = 0 AND v_l_iso IS NOT INITIAL. v_r_unit = v_l_iso.
  ELSE. v_r_unit = v_i_unit. ENDIF.
 ENDMETHOD.

  wa_l_cov-campo = 'SERVICEORDER'.
  wa_l_cov-origen = 'AUFK-AUFNR'.
  wa_l_cov-regla = 'Relacion con cabecera existente'.
  APPEND wa_l_cov TO it_l_cov.
  CLEAR wa_l_cov. wa_l_cov-numero = 12. wa_l_cov-columna = 2.
  wa_l_cov-campo = 'SERVICEREFERENCEEQUIPMENT'.
  wa_l_cov-origen = 'ES_HEADER / ET_OLIST-EQUIPMENT'.
  wa_l_cov-regla = 'Fuente ECC directa; equipo y ubicacion en filas separadas sin duplicados'.
  APPEND wa_l_cov TO it_l_cov.
  CLEAR wa_l_cov. wa_l_cov-numero = 12. wa_l_cov-columna = 3.
  wa_l_cov-campo = 'SERVICEREFFUNCTIONALLOCATION'.
  wa_l_cov-origen = 'ES_HEADER / ET_OLIST-FUNCT_LOC'.
  wa_l_cov-regla = 'Ubicacion ECC directa; fila separada cuando tambien existe equipo'.
  APPEND wa_l_cov TO it_l_cov.
  CLEAR wa_l_cov. wa_l_cov-numero = 12. wa_l_cov-columna = 4.
  wa_l_cov-campo = 'SRVCREFOBJISMAINOBJECT'.
  wa_l_cov-origen = 'PENDIENTE'.
  wa_l_cov-regla = 'Sin origen directo identificado en las fuentes actuales; conservar vacio'.
  APPEND wa_l_cov TO it_l_cov.
  LOOP AT it_l_cov ASSIGNING <fs_cov>.
   <fs_cov>-runid = v_g_run. <fs_cov>-batch = v_g_batch.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet> WITH KEY number = <fs_cov>-numero.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. <fs_cov>-estado = 'HOJA_NO_PREPARADA'. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
   <fs_cov>-hoja = <fs_sheet>-title. <fs_cov>-filas = lines( <fs_sheet>-rows ).
   v_l_idx = <fs_cov>-columna. v_l_field = 'C' && v_l_idx.
   LOOP AT <fs_sheet>-rows INTO wa_l_row.
    ASSIGN COMPONENT v_l_field OF STRUCTURE wa_l_row TO <fs_cell>.
    IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <fs_cell> IS NOT INITIAL. ADD 1 TO <fs_cov>-con_valor.
    ELSE. ADD 1 TO <fs_cov>-sin_valor. ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
   IF <fs_cov>-origen = 'PENDIENTE'. <fs_cov>-estado = 'ORIGEN_PENDIENTE'.
   ELSEIF <fs_cov>-filas = 0. <fs_cov>-estado = 'SIN_FILAS_VER_DIAGNOSTICO'.
   ELSEIF <fs_cov>-sin_valor = 0. <fs_cov>-estado = 'POBLADO_DESDE_ORIGEN'.
   ELSEIF <fs_cov>-con_valor = 0. <fs_cov>-estado = 'SIN_VALOR_EN_SALIDA'.
   ELSE. <fs_cov>-estado = 'POBLADO_PARCIAL'. ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  it_l_lines = serialize( it_l_cov ).
  emit( v_i_entity = 'COBERTURA_CAMPOS' v_i_kind = 'TXT'
   v_i_records = lines( it_l_cov ) v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
* La ausencia de filas no demuestra NO_APLICA. Se informa por orden y hoja.
  READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_headsheet> WITH KEY number = 3.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. RETURN. ENDIF.
  LOOP AT <fs_headsheet>-rows INTO wa_l_head.
   LOOP AT it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet>.
    CLEAR: wa_l_diag, v_l_rows.
    LOOP AT <fs_sheet>-rows INTO wa_l_row WHERE c01 = wa_l_head-c01.
     ADD 1 TO v_l_rows.
    ENDLOOP.
    wa_l_diag-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_diag-batch = v_g_batch.
    wa_l_diag-aufnr = wa_l_head-c01. wa_l_diag-entidad = 'COBERTURA_HOJA'.
    wa_l_diag-clave = <fs_sheet>-title.
    IF v_l_rows > 0. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'CON_REGISTROS'.
    ELSE. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'SIN_FILAS_NO_CONCLUYENTE'. ENDIF.
    IF <fs_sheet>-number = 9.
     wa_l_diag-resultado = 'ORIGEN_PENDIENTE'.
    ELSEIF ( <fs_sheet>-number = 8 OR <fs_sheet>-number = 12 ) AND p_obj IS INITIAL.
     wa_l_diag-resultado = 'OPCION_DESACTIVADA'.

    ENDIF.
    wa_l_diag-detalle = |Filas { v_l_rows }; ver COBERTURA_CAMPOS y evidencia BAPI/textos. | &&
     |No inferir NO_APLICA ni ausencia en ECC solo por una hoja vacia.|.
    APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_g_diag.
   ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
 ENDMETHOD.


* @024 Libro tecnico de apoyo. No es una plantilla de carga directa en Cockpit.
 METHOD technical_sheet.
  DATA: lo_ws TYPE REF TO zcl_excel_worksheet,
   it_l_cat TYPE zexcel_t_fieldcatalog, it_l_parts TYPE ty_strings,
   it_l_sources TYPE ty_strings, it_l_comp TYPE cl_abap_structdescr=>component_table,
   wa_l_comp LIKE LINE OF it_l_comp, lo_struct TYPE REF TO cl_abap_structdescr,
   lo_table TYPE REF TO cl_abap_tabledescr, v_lr_table TYPE REF TO data,
   v_lr_row TYPE REF TO data, wa_l_row TYPE ty_row,
   v_l_part TYPE string, v_l_name TYPE string, v_l_source TYPE string,
   v_l_group TYPE string, v_l_field TYPE string, v_l_n TYPE i,
   wa_l_order TYPE aufk, wa_l_afih TYPE afih, wa_l_afko TYPE afko,
   wa_l_iloa TYPE iloa, wa_l_link TYPE ty_poslink,
   wa_l_note TYPE ty_note,
   v_l_current TYPE aufnr, v_l_context TYPE string.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_data> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_row> TYPE any,
   <fs_cell> TYPE any, <fs_cat> TYPE zexcel_s_fieldcatalog.
  SPLIT v_i_map AT '|' INTO TABLE it_l_parts.
  LOOP AT it_l_parts INTO v_l_part.
   SPLIT v_l_part AT '=' INTO v_l_name v_l_source.
   CLEAR wa_l_comp. wa_l_comp-name = v_l_name.
   wa_l_comp-type = cl_abap_elemdescr=>get_string( ).
   APPEND wa_l_comp TO it_l_comp. APPEND v_l_source TO it_l_sources.
  ENDLOOP.
  lo_struct = cl_abap_structdescr=>create( it_l_comp ).
  lo_table = cl_abap_tabledescr=>create( lo_struct ).
  CREATE DATA v_lr_table TYPE HANDLE lo_table.
  CREATE DATA v_lr_row TYPE HANDLE lo_struct.
  ASSIGN v_lr_table->* TO <fs_data>. ASSIGN v_lr_row->* TO <fs_row>.
  LOOP AT it_rows INTO wa_l_row.
   CLEAR <fs_row>.
   IF v_i_first = abap_false AND v_l_current <> wa_l_row-c01.
    v_l_current = wa_l_row-c01.
    CLEAR: wa_l_order, wa_l_afih, wa_l_afko, wa_l_iloa.
    READ TABLE it_g_origin INTO wa_l_order WITH KEY aufnr = v_l_current.
* Lecturas por claves completas de orden/objeto, nunca seleccion sin filtro.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM afih INTO wa_l_afih WHERE aufnr = v_l_current.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM afko INTO wa_l_afko WHERE aufnr = v_l_current.
    IF wa_l_afih-iloan IS NOT INITIAL.
     SELECT SINGLE * FROM iloa INTO wa_l_iloa WHERE iloan = wa_l_afih-iloan.
    ENDIF.
   ENDIF.
   CLEAR wa_l_link.
   IF v_i_title = 'A5_Posiciones' OR v_i_title = 'A4_Socios_posicion'
    OR v_i_title = 'A6_Notas_posicion' OR v_i_title = 'A7_Objetos_posicion'.
    READ TABLE it_g_poslink INTO wa_l_link
     WITH KEY aufnr = v_l_current posicion = wa_l_row-c02.
   ENDIF.
   CLEAR wa_l_note.
   IF v_i_title = 'A6_Notas_posicion'.
    READ TABLE it_g_notes INTO wa_l_note WITH KEY aufnr = v_l_current
     position = wa_l_row-c02 iso = wa_l_row-c03 tdid = wa_l_row-c04.
   ELSEIF v_i_title = 'A8_Notas_cabecera'.
    READ TABLE it_g_notes INTO wa_l_note WITH KEY aufnr = v_l_current
     position = '000000' iso = wa_l_row-c02 tdid = wa_l_row-c03.
   ENDIF.
   LOOP AT it_l_sources INTO v_l_source.
    v_l_n = sy-tabix. ASSIGN COMPONENT v_l_n OF STRUCTURE <fs_row> TO <fs_cell>.
    CLEAR: v_l_group, v_l_field.
    SPLIT v_l_source AT '.' INTO v_l_group v_l_field.
    CASE v_l_group.
     WHEN 'AUFK'. <fs_cell> = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_order v_i_field = v_l_field ).
     WHEN 'AFIH'. <fs_cell> = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_afih v_i_field = v_l_field ).
     WHEN 'AFKO'. <fs_cell> = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_afko v_i_field = v_l_field ).
     WHEN 'ILOA'. <fs_cell> = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_iloa v_i_field = v_l_field ).
     WHEN 'LINK'. <fs_cell> = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_link v_i_field = v_l_field ).
     WHEN 'NOTE'. <fs_cell> = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_note v_i_field = v_l_field ).
     WHEN OTHERS. <fs_cell> = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_row v_i_field = v_l_source ).
    ENDCASE.
   ENDLOOP.
   APPEND <fs_row> TO <fs_data>.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF v_i_first = abap_true.
   lo_ws = io_excel->get_active_worksheet( ). lo_ws->set_title( ip_title = v_i_title ).
  ELSE. lo_ws = io_excel->add_new_worksheet( ip_title = v_i_title ). ENDIF.
  lo_ws->set_cell( ip_column = 'A' ip_row = 1 ip_value = v_i_summary ).
  lo_ws->set_cell( ip_column = 'A' ip_row = 2
   ip_value = 'Referencia ECC para llenado manual; sin homologacion; vacio no equivale a no aplica.' ).
  v_l_context = |RUN={ v_g_run }; PAQUETE={ v_g_batch }; SID={ sy-sysid }; MANDANTE={ sy-mandt }|.
  lo_ws->set_cell( ip_column = 'A' ip_row = 3 ip_value = v_l_context ).
  it_l_cat = zcl_excel_common=>get_fieldcatalog( ip_table = <fs_data> ).
  LOOP AT it_l_cat ASSIGNING <fs_cat>.
   <fs_cat>-scrtext_s = <fs_cat>-fieldname.
   <fs_cat>-scrtext_m = <fs_cat>-fieldname.
   <fs_cat>-scrtext_l = <fs_cat>-fieldname.
  ENDLOOP.
  lo_ws->bind_table( ip_table = <fs_data> it_field_catalog = it_l_cat
   is_table_settings = VALUE #( top_left_column = 'A' top_left_row = 5
    table_style = zcl_excel_table=>builtinstyle_medium2 show_row_stripes = abap_true ) ).
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD technical_workbook.
  DATA: lo_excel TYPE REF TO zcl_excel, lo_writer TYPE REF TO zif_excel_writer,
   wa_l_sheet TYPE ty_sheet, it_l_guide TYPE ty_rows, wa_l_guide TYPE ty_row,
   v_l_title TYPE zexcel_sheet_title, v_l_summary TYPE string,
   v_l_map TYPE string, v_lx TYPE REF TO cx_root,
   it_l_rows TYPE ty_rows, wa_l_row TYPE ty_row, it_l_heads TYPE ty_rows,
   it_l_a9 TYPE ty_rows, it_l_a10 TYPE ty_rows, wa_l_head TYPE ty_row,
   wa_l_afih TYPE afih, wa_l_iloa TYPE iloa, wa_l_resp_diag TYPE ty_diag.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_object> TYPE ty_row.
  TRY.
   CREATE OBJECT lo_excel. CREATE OBJECT lo_writer TYPE zcl_excel_writer_2007.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 3.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR wa_l_sheet-rows IS INITIAL. RETURN. ENDIF.
   it_l_heads = wa_l_sheet-rows.

* A9 muestra el resultado de consultar ZR por orden; sin depender de parametro oculto.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 11.
   LOOP AT it_l_heads INTO wa_l_head.
    CLEAR wa_l_row.
    READ TABLE wa_l_sheet-rows INTO wa_l_row WITH KEY c01 = wa_l_head-c01.
    wa_l_row-c01 = wa_l_head-c01. wa_l_row-c04 = 'ZR'.
    IF wa_l_row-c02 IS INITIAL.
     wa_l_row-c03 = 'SIN_RESP_UNICO_RECUPERADO'.
     CLEAR wa_l_resp_diag.
     READ TABLE it_g_diag INTO wa_l_resp_diag WITH KEY
      aufnr = wa_l_head-c01 entidad = 'RESPONSABLE' clave = 'IHPA-PARVW=ZR'.
     IF sy-subrc = 0. wa_l_row-c03 = wa_l_resp_diag-resultado. ENDIF.
    ELSE. wa_l_row-c03 = 'RESPONSABLE_RECUPERADO'. ENDIF.
    APPEND wa_l_row TO it_l_a9.
   ENDLOOP.
* A10 une evidencia BAPI con datos directos, conservando solo ordenes validadas.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 12.
   LOOP AT wa_l_sheet-rows INTO wa_l_row.
    READ TABLE it_l_heads TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY c01 = wa_l_row-c01.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    wa_l_row-c04 = 'BAPI'. APPEND wa_l_row TO it_l_a10.
   ENDLOOP.
   LOOP AT it_l_heads INTO wa_l_head.
    CLEAR: wa_l_afih, wa_l_iloa, wa_l_row.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM afih INTO wa_l_afih WHERE aufnr = wa_l_head-c01.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    IF wa_l_afih-iloan IS NOT INITIAL.
     SELECT SINGLE * FROM iloa INTO wa_l_iloa WHERE iloan = wa_l_afih-iloan.
    ENDIF.
    IF wa_l_afih-iloan IS INITIAL AND wa_l_afih-equnr IS INITIAL
     AND wa_l_iloa-tplnr IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
    wa_l_row-c01 = wa_l_head-c01. wa_l_row-c02 = wa_l_afih-equnr.
    wa_l_row-c03 = wa_l_iloa-tplnr. wa_l_row-c04 = 'AFIH_ILOA'.
    READ TABLE it_l_a10 ASSIGNING <fs_object> WITH KEY
     c01 = wa_l_row-c01 c02 = wa_l_row-c02 c03 = wa_l_row-c03.
    IF sy-subrc = 0. <fs_object>-c04 = 'BAPI_Y_AFIH_ILOA'.
    ELSE. APPEND wa_l_row TO it_l_a10. ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.

   CLEAR wa_l_guide. wa_l_guide-c01 = 'A2_Cabecera'.
   wa_l_guide-c02 = 'AUFK + AFIH + AFKO; datos comerciales ya recuperados de ECC/BAPI.'.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 3.
   wa_l_guide-c03 = lines( wa_l_sheet-rows ).
   wa_l_guide-c04 = `AUFNR <- AUFK-AUFNR; AUART <- AUFK-AUART; KTEXT <- AUFK-KTEXT; IDIOMA_ISO <- T002-LAIS` &&
    `O desde SY-LANGU; WAERS <- AUFK-WAERS; PRIOK <- AFIH-PRIOK; FECHA_INICIO_ECC <- AFKO-G` &&
    `STRP; FECHA_FIN_ECC <- AFKO-GLTRP; PURCH_NO_C <- ES_SRVDATA-PURCH_NO_C; PURCH_DATE <- ` &&
    `ES_SRVDATA-PURCH_DATE; SALESORG <- ILOA-VKORG o ES_SRVDATA-SALESORG; DISTR_CHAN <- ILO` &&
    `A-VTWEG o ES_SRVDATA-DISTR_CHAN; DIVISION <- ILOA-SPART o ES_SRVDATA-DIVISION; SALES_O` &&
    `FF <- ILOA-VKBUR o ES_SRVDATA-SALES_OFF; SALES_GRP <- ILOA-VKGRP o ES_SRVDATA-SALES_GR` &&
    `P; SOLD_TO <- AFIH-KUNUM o IHPA segun funcion; SHIP_TO <- IHPA-PARNR funcion WE; BILL_` &&
    `TO <- IHPA-PARNR funcion RE; PAYER <- IHPA-PARNR funcion RG; CONTACT <- IHPA-PARNR fun` &&
    `cion AP; AUTYP <- AUFK.AUTYP; OBJNR <- AUFK.OBJNR; BUKRS <- AUFK.BUKRS; WERKS <- AUFK.` &&
    `WERKS; KOKRS <- AUFK.KOKRS; KOSTV <- AUFK.KOSTV; ERDAT <- AUFK.ERDAT; IWERK <- AFIH.IW` &&
    `ERK; INGRP <- AFIH.INGRP; GEWRK <- AFIH.GEWRK; KUNUM <- AFIH.KUNUM; ILART <- AFIH.ILAR` &&
    `T; AUFPL <- AFKO.AUFPL; RSNUM <- AFKO.RSNUM; GSTRP <- AFKO.GSTRP; GLTRP <- AFKO.GLTRP` .
   APPEND wa_l_guide TO it_l_guide.
   CLEAR wa_l_guide. wa_l_guide-c01 = 'A3_Socios_cabecera'.
   wa_l_guide-c02 = 'IHPA: OBJNR de AUFK; sin convertir PARNR a BP.'.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 4.
   wa_l_guide-c03 = lines( wa_l_sheet-rows ).
