   wa_l_guide-c04 = 'AUFNR <- AUFK-AUFNR; OBJNR <- AUFK.OBJNR; PARVW <- IHPA-PARVW; PARNR <- IHPA-PARNR'.
   APPEND wa_l_guide TO it_l_guide.
   CLEAR wa_l_guide. wa_l_guide-c01 = 'A4_Socios_posicion'.
   wa_l_guide-c02 = 'IHPA por OBJNR de operacion; AUFPL/APLZL/VORNR conservan la relacion ECC.'.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 6.
   wa_l_guide-c03 = lines( wa_l_sheet-rows ).
   wa_l_guide-c04 = `AUFNR <- AUFK-AUFNR; POSICION <- Consecutivo generado por orden; no equivale a VORNR; ` &&
    `AUFPL <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.AUFPL; APLZL <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.APLZL; VORNR <- TRAZA_POSIC` &&
    `IONES.VORNR; PARVW <- IHPA-PARVW; PARNR <- IHPA-PARNR` .
   APPEND wa_l_guide TO it_l_guide.
   CLEAR wa_l_guide. wa_l_guide-c01 = 'A5_Posiciones'.
   wa_l_guide-c02 = 'Operaciones AFVC/AFVV y componentes RESB pendientes; POSICION es consecutivo generado.'.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 5.
   wa_l_guide-c03 = lines( wa_l_sheet-rows ).
   wa_l_guide-c04 = `AUFNR <- AUFK-AUFNR; POSICION <- Consecutivo generado por orden; no equivale a VORNR; ` &&
    `CLASE_ORIGEN <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.CLASE; AUFPL <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.AUFPL; APLZL <- TRAZ` &&
    `A_POSICIONES.APLZL; VORNR <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.VORNR; STEUS <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.STEUS; ` &&
    `WERKS <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.WERKS; DESCRIPCION_ECC <- AFVC-LTXA1 o MAKT-MAKTX; IDIOMA_IS` &&
    `O <- T002-LAISO desde SY-LANGU; MATNR <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.MATERIAL; CANTIDAD_EXTRAIDA ` &&
    `<- ET_OPERATIONS-QUANTITY o RESB-BDMNG menos ENMNG; UNIDAD_EXTRAIDA <- BAPI unidad exi` &&
    `stente o RESB-MEINS; revisar ISO; DAUNO <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.DURACION_ORIGEN; DAUNE <- ` &&
    `TRAZA_POSICIONES.UNIDAD_DURACION; ARBEI <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.TRABAJO_PREVISTO; ARBEH <-` &&
    ` TRAZA_POSICIONES.UNIDAD_TRABAJO; RSNUM <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.RSNUM; RSPOS <- TRAZA_POSI` &&
    `CIONES.RSPOS; LGORT <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.ALMACEN; BDMNG <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.REQUERIDO; ` &&
    `ENMNG <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.RETIRADO; PERS_NO <- ET_OPERATIONS-PERS_NO; REFERENCIA_ECC <` &&
    `- TRAZA_POSICIONES.REFERENCIA_ORIGEN` .
   APPEND wa_l_guide TO it_l_guide.
   CLEAR wa_l_guide. wa_l_guide-c01 = 'A6_Notas_posicion'.
   wa_l_guide-c02 = 'READ_TEXT y textos BAPI identificados: contenido de operacion vinculado a POSICION.'.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 7.
   wa_l_guide-c03 = lines( wa_l_sheet-rows ).
   wa_l_guide-c04 = `AUFNR <- AUFK-AUFNR; POSICION <- Consecutivo generado por orden; no equivale a VORNR; ` &&
    `AUFPL <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.AUFPL; APLZL <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.APLZL; VORNR <- TRAZA_POSIC` &&
    `IONES.VORNR; IDIOMA_ISO <- T002-LAISO desde SY-LANGU; TDID <- READ_TEXT: THEAD-TDID o ` &&
    `ID configurado para BAPI; LONGTEXT <- READ_TEXT: TLINE-TDLINE concatenado o ET_TEXT_LI` &&
    `NES; TDOBJECT <- TEXTO_RECUPERADO.TDOBJECT; TDNAME <- TEXTO_RECUPERADO.TDNAME; TDSPRAS` && ` <- TEXTO_RECUPERADO.LANGUAGE` .
   APPEND wa_l_guide TO it_l_guide.
   CLEAR wa_l_guide. wa_l_guide-c01 = 'A7_Objetos_posicion'.
   wa_l_guide-c02 = 'BAPI_ALM_ORDER_GET_DETAIL: equipo de operacion; solo relaciones explicitas.'.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 8.
   wa_l_guide-c03 = lines( wa_l_sheet-rows ).
   wa_l_guide-c04 = `AUFNR <- AUFK-AUFNR; POSICION <- Consecutivo generado por orden; no equivale a VORNR; ` &&
    `AUFPL <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.AUFPL; APLZL <- TRAZA_POSICIONES.APLZL; VORNR <- TRAZA_POSIC` &&
    `IONES.VORNR; EQUNR <- BAPI-EQUIPMENT o AFIH-EQUNR; TPLNR <- BAPI FUNCT_LOC sin conve` && `rsion` .
   APPEND wa_l_guide TO it_l_guide.
   CLEAR wa_l_guide. wa_l_guide-c01 = 'A8_Notas_cabecera'.
   wa_l_guide-c02 = 'READ_TEXT: AUFK/KOPF y variantes existentes; contenido con saltos de linea.'.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 10.
   wa_l_guide-c03 = lines( wa_l_sheet-rows ).
   wa_l_guide-c04 = `AUFNR <- AUFK-AUFNR; IDIOMA_ISO <- T002-LAISO desde SY-LANGU; TDID <- READ_TEXT: THEAD` &&
    `-TDID o ID configurado para BAPI; LONGTEXT <- READ_TEXT: TLINE-TDLINE concatenado o ET` &&
    `_TEXT_LINES; TDOBJECT <- TEXTO_RECUPERADO.TDOBJECT; TDNAME <- TEXTO_RECUPERADO.TDNAME;` &&
    ` TDSPRAS <- TEXTO_RECUPERADO.LANGUAGE` .
   APPEND wa_l_guide TO it_l_guide.
   CLEAR wa_l_guide. wa_l_guide-c01 = 'A9_Responsable'.
   wa_l_guide-c02 = 'IHPA: PARVW = ZR; solo interlocutor vigente unico, sin conversion a empleado/BP.'.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 11.
   wa_l_guide-c03 = lines( it_l_a9 ).
   wa_l_guide-c04 = 'AUFNR <- AUFK-AUFNR; OBJNR <- AUFK.OBJNR; PARNR <- IHPA-PARNR; INGRP <- AFIH.INGRP; GEWRK <- AFIH.GEWRK'.
   wa_l_guide-c04 = wa_l_guide-c04 && `; PARVW <- regla de extraccion ZR, contrastada en HN11/CO11; ESTADO_LECTURA <- control` &&
    ` tecnico, no estado SAP` .
   APPEND wa_l_guide TO it_l_guide.
   CLEAR wa_l_guide. wa_l_guide-c01 = 'A10_Objetos_cabecera'.
   wa_l_guide-c02 = 'BAPI y AFIH/ILOA independientes; FUENTE_OBJETO identifica procedencia y se conserva ILOAN.'.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 12.
   wa_l_guide-c03 = lines( it_l_a10 ).
   wa_l_guide-c04 = `AUFNR <- AUFK-AUFNR; EQUNR <- BAPI-EQUIPMENT o AFIH-EQUNR; TPLNR <- BAPI FUNCT_LOC s` &&
    `in conversion; ILOAN <- AFIH.ILOAN; EQUNR_CAB_AFIH <- AFIH.EQUNR; TPLNR_CAB_ILOA <- IL` && `OA.TPLNR` .
   APPEND wa_l_guide TO it_l_guide.
   READ TABLE it_l_guide ASSIGNING <fs_object> WITH KEY c01 = 'A10_Objetos_cabecera'.
   IF sy-subrc = 0.
    <fs_object>-c04 = <fs_object>-c04 && '; FUENTE_OBJETO <- BAPI, AFIH_ILOA o BAPI_Y_AFIH_ILOA'.
   ENDIF.
   technical_sheet( io_excel = lo_excel v_i_title = 'A1_Resumen' v_i_first = abap_true
    v_i_summary = 'A1: guia de apartados A2-A10; WIP de consulta, sin decision final de migracion.'
    v_i_map = 'PESTANA=C01|FUENTES_Y_CONTENIDO=C02|FILAS=C03|DICCIONARIO_ORIGEN=C04'
    it_rows = it_l_guide ).
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 3.
   CLEAR it_l_rows.
   LOOP AT wa_l_sheet-rows INTO wa_l_row.
    READ TABLE it_l_heads TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY c01 = wa_l_row-c01.
    IF sy-subrc = 0. APPEND wa_l_row TO it_l_rows. ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
   technical_sheet( io_excel = lo_excel v_i_title = 'A2_Cabecera'
    v_i_summary = 'AUFK + AFIH + AFKO; datos comerciales ya recuperados de ECC/BAPI.'
    v_i_map = `AUFNR=C01|AUART=C02|KTEXT=C04|IDIOMA_ISO=C05|WAERS=C06|PRIOK=C08|FECHA_INICIO_ECC=C09|` &&
     `FECHA_FIN_ECC=C12|PURCH_NO_C=C15|PURCH_DATE=C16|SALESORG=C17|DISTR_CHAN=C18|DIVISION=C` &&
     `19|SALES_OFF=C20|SALES_GRP=C21|SOLD_TO=C22|SHIP_TO=C23|BILL_TO=C24|PAYER=C25|CONTACT=C` &&
     `26|AUTYP=AUFK.AUTYP|OBJNR=AUFK.OBJNR|BUKRS=AUFK.BUKRS|WERKS=AUFK.WERKS|KOKRS=AUFK.KOKR` &&
     `S|KOSTV=AUFK.KOSTV|ERDAT=AUFK.ERDAT|IWERK=AFIH.IWERK|INGRP=AFIH.INGRP|GEWRK=AFIH.GEWRK` &&
     `|KUNUM=AFIH.KUNUM|ILART=AFIH.ILART|AUFPL=AFKO.AUFPL|RSNUM=AFKO.RSNUM|GSTRP=AFKO.GSTRP|` && `GLTRP=AFKO.GLTRP` it_rows =
     it_l_rows ).
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 4.
   CLEAR it_l_rows.
   LOOP AT wa_l_sheet-rows INTO wa_l_row.
    READ TABLE it_l_heads TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY c01 = wa_l_row-c01.
    IF sy-subrc = 0. APPEND wa_l_row TO it_l_rows. ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
   technical_sheet( io_excel = lo_excel v_i_title = 'A3_Socios_cabecera'
    v_i_summary = 'IHPA: OBJNR de AUFK; sin convertir PARNR a BP.'
    v_i_map = 'AUFNR=C01|OBJNR=AUFK.OBJNR|PARVW=C02|PARNR=C03' it_rows = it_l_rows ).
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 6.
   CLEAR it_l_rows.
   LOOP AT wa_l_sheet-rows INTO wa_l_row.
    READ TABLE it_l_heads TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY c01 = wa_l_row-c01.
    IF sy-subrc = 0. APPEND wa_l_row TO it_l_rows. ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
   technical_sheet( io_excel = lo_excel v_i_title = 'A4_Socios_posicion'
    v_i_summary = 'IHPA por OBJNR de operacion; AUFPL/APLZL/VORNR conservan la relacion ECC.'
    v_i_map = 'AUFNR=C01|POSICION=C02|AUFPL=LINK.AUFPL|APLZL=LINK.APLZL|VORNR=LINK.VORNR|PARVW=C03|PARNR=C04' it_rows = it_l_rows ).
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 5.
   CLEAR it_l_rows.
   LOOP AT wa_l_sheet-rows INTO wa_l_row.
    READ TABLE it_l_heads TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY c01 = wa_l_row-c01.
    IF sy-subrc = 0. APPEND wa_l_row TO it_l_rows. ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
   technical_sheet( io_excel = lo_excel v_i_title = 'A5_Posiciones'
    v_i_summary = 'Operaciones AFVC/AFVV y componentes RESB pendientes; POSICION es consecutivo generado.'
    v_i_map = `AUFNR=C01|POSICION=C02|CLASE_ORIGEN=LINK.CLASE|AUFPL=LINK.AUFPL|APLZL=LINK.APLZL|VORNR` &&
     `=LINK.VORNR|STEUS=LINK.STEUS|WERKS=LINK.WERKS|DESCRIPCION_ECC=C04|IDIOMA_ISO=C05|MATNR` &&
     `=LINK.MATERIAL|CANTIDAD_EXTRAIDA=C07|UNIDAD_EXTRAIDA=C08|DAUNO=LINK.DURACION_ORIGEN|DA` &&
     `UNE=LINK.UNIDAD_DURACION|ARBEI=LINK.TRABAJO_PREVISTO|ARBEH=LINK.UNIDAD_TRABAJO|RSNUM=L` &&
     `INK.RSNUM|RSPOS=LINK.RSPOS|LGORT=LINK.ALMACEN|BDMNG=LINK.REQUERIDO|ENMNG=LINK.RETIRADO` &&
     `|PERS_NO=C18|REFERENCIA_ECC=LINK.REFERENCIA_ORIGEN` it_rows = it_l_rows ).
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 7.
   CLEAR it_l_rows.
   LOOP AT wa_l_sheet-rows INTO wa_l_row.
    READ TABLE it_l_heads TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY c01 = wa_l_row-c01.
    IF sy-subrc = 0. APPEND wa_l_row TO it_l_rows. ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
   technical_sheet( io_excel = lo_excel v_i_title = 'A6_Notas_posicion'
    v_i_summary = 'READ_TEXT y textos BAPI identificados: contenido de operacion vinculado a POSICION.'
    v_i_map = `AUFNR=C01|POSICION=C02|AUFPL=LINK.AUFPL|APLZL=LINK.APLZL|VORNR=LINK.VORNR|IDIOMA_ISO=C` &&
     `03|TDID=C04|LONGTEXT=C05|TDOBJECT=NOTE.TDOBJECT|TDNAME=NOTE.TDNAME|TDSPRAS=NOTE.LANGUA` && `GE` it_rows = it_l_rows ).
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 8.
   CLEAR it_l_rows.
   LOOP AT wa_l_sheet-rows INTO wa_l_row.
    READ TABLE it_l_heads TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY c01 = wa_l_row-c01.
    IF sy-subrc = 0. APPEND wa_l_row TO it_l_rows. ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
   technical_sheet( io_excel = lo_excel v_i_title = 'A7_Objetos_posicion'
    v_i_summary = 'BAPI_ALM_ORDER_GET_DETAIL: equipo de operacion; solo relaciones explicitas.'
    v_i_map = 'AUFNR=C01|POSICION=C02|AUFPL=LINK.AUFPL|APLZL=LINK.APLZL|VORNR=LINK.VORNR|EQUNR=C03|TPLNR=C04' it_rows = it_l_rows ).
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_sheet WITH KEY number = 10.
   CLEAR it_l_rows.
   LOOP AT wa_l_sheet-rows INTO wa_l_row.
    READ TABLE it_l_heads TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY c01 = wa_l_row-c01.
    IF sy-subrc = 0. APPEND wa_l_row TO it_l_rows. ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
   technical_sheet( io_excel = lo_excel v_i_title = 'A8_Notas_cabecera'
    v_i_summary = 'READ_TEXT: AUFK/KOPF y variantes existentes; contenido con saltos de linea.'
    v_i_map = `AUFNR=C01|IDIOMA_ISO=C02|TDID=C03|LONGTEXT=C04|TDOBJECT=NOTE.TDOBJECT|TDNAME=NOTE.TDNA` && `ME|TDSPRAS=NOTE.LANGUAGE`
     it_rows = it_l_rows ).
   it_l_rows = it_l_a9.
   technical_sheet( io_excel = lo_excel v_i_title = 'A9_Responsable'
    v_i_summary = 'IHPA: PARVW = ZR; solo interlocutor vigente unico, sin conversion a empleado/BP.'
    v_i_map = 'AUFNR=C01|OBJNR=AUFK.OBJNR|PARVW=C04|PARNR=C02|ESTADO_LECTURA=C03|INGRP=AFIH.INGRP|GEWRK=AFIH.GEWRK' it_rows =
     it_l_rows ).
   it_l_rows = it_l_a10.
   technical_sheet( io_excel = lo_excel v_i_title = 'A10_Objetos_cabecera'
    v_i_summary = 'BAPI y AFIH/ILOA independientes; FUENTE_OBJETO identifica procedencia y se conserva ILOAN.'
    v_i_map = `AUFNR=C01|EQUNR=C02|TPLNR=C03|FUENTE_OBJETO=C04|ILOAN=AFIH.ILOAN|EQUNR_CAB_AFIH=AFIH.E` &&
     `QUNR|TPLNR_CAB_ILOA=ILOA.TPLNR` it_rows =
     it_l_rows ).
   v_r_binary = lo_writer->write_file( lo_excel ).
  CATCH cx_root INTO v_lx.
   CLEAR v_r_binary.
   log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'ANEXO_TECNICO' v_i_message = v_lx->get_text( ) ).
  ENDTRY.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD exports.
  DATA: v_l_binary TYPE xstring, it_l_lines TYPE ty_strings,
   wa_l_row TYPE ty_row, v_l_line TYPE string, v_l_cell TYPE string,
   v_l_field TYPE string, v_l_idx TYPE n LENGTH 2,
   v_l_headers TYPE string, it_l_headers TYPE ty_strings, v_l_orders TYPE i.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_sheet> TYPE ty_sheet, <fs_cell> TYPE any.
  v_l_binary = technical_workbook( ).
  IF v_l_binary IS INITIAL. v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
  READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet> WITH KEY number = 3.
  v_l_orders = lines( <fs_sheet>-rows ).
  emit( v_i_entity = 'ANEXO_TECNICO' v_i_kind = 'XLSX' v_i_records = v_l_orders
   v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
  CLEAR v_l_binary.
  LOOP AT it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet>.
   IF <fs_sheet>-rows IS INITIAL. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
   CLEAR it_l_lines.
   CASE <fs_sheet>-number.
    WHEN 3. v_l_headers = `Orden de servicio*|Clase|Indicador de control p.rango números|Descripción|Idioma|Moned` &&
     `a|Contrato de servicio de referencia|Prioridad|Fecha de inicio deseada|Inicio desead` &&
      `o (hora)|Inicio deseado (huso horario)|Fecha de cierre deseada|Final deseado (hora)|Fi` &&
       `n deseado (huso horario)|Pedido de compras|Fecha de pedido|Organización de ventas|Ca` &&
      `nal de distribución|Sector|Oficina de ventas|Grupo de vendedores|Solicitante|Destinata` &&
       `rio de mercancías|Destinatario de la factura|Pagador|Persona de contacto` .
    WHEN 4. v_l_headers = 'Orden de servicio*|Función de interlocutor*|Número de interlocutor*|SC - ID de dirección externa'.
    WHEN 5. v_l_headers = `Orden de servicio*|Posición*|Posición superior|Descripción de la posición|Clave de idi` &&
     `oma|Producto|Ctd.|Unidad de medida|Duración|Unidad de duración|Tipo posición|Factura` &&
      `ble - Control|Solicitante|Destinatario de mercancías|Destinatario de la factura|Pagado` &&
       `r|Empleado responsable|Responsable de prestación de servicios|Contrato de servicio|P` &&
      `osición de contrato de servicio|Solicitud de servicio|Posición de solicitud de servici` &&
       `os|Hoja de horas de trabajo horas extras` .
    WHEN 6. v_l_headers = `Orden de servicio*|Posición de orden de servicio*|Función de interlocutor*|Número de i` &&
     `nterlocutor*|SC - ID de dirección externa` .
    WHEN 7. v_l_headers = 'Orden de servicio*|Posición de orden de servicio*|Clave de idioma|ID del texto|Texto'.
    WHEN 8. v_l_headers = `Orden de servicio*|Posición de orden de servicio*|Número de equipo|Ubicación técnica|O` &&
     `bjeto de referencia principal` .
    WHEN 9. v_l_headers = `Orden de servicio*|Posición de orden de servicio*|Número de paso|Contador para condici` &&
     `ones|Clase de condición|Importe (importe de precio o porcentaje)|Clave de moneda|Can` &&
      'tidad determinación precios condición|Cantidad base para la condición'.
    WHEN 10. v_l_headers = 'ID orden de servicio*|Clave de idioma*|ID del texto*|Texto'.
    WHEN 11. v_l_headers = 'Orden de servicio*|Empleado responsable*'.
    WHEN 12. v_l_headers = 'Orden de servicio*|Número de equipo|Ubicación técnica|Objeto de referencia principal'.
   ENDCASE.
   SPLIT v_l_headers AT '|' INTO TABLE it_l_headers.
   CONCATENATE LINES OF it_l_headers INTO v_l_line SEPARATED BY cl_abap_char_utilities=>horizontal_tab.
   APPEND v_l_line TO it_l_lines.
   LOOP AT <fs_sheet>-rows INTO wa_l_row.
    CLEAR v_l_line.
    DO <fs_sheet>-columns TIMES.
     v_l_idx = sy-index. v_l_field = 'C' && v_l_idx.
     ASSIGN COMPONENT v_l_field OF STRUCTURE wa_l_row TO <fs_cell>.
     v_l_cell = <fs_cell>. REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '"' IN v_l_cell WITH '""'.
     v_l_cell = '"' && v_l_cell && '"'.
     IF sy-index = 1. v_l_line = v_l_cell.
     ELSE. v_l_line = v_l_line && cl_abap_char_utilities=>horizontal_tab && v_l_cell. ENDIF.
    ENDDO.
    APPEND v_l_line TO it_l_lines.
   ENDLOOP.
   emit( v_i_entity = |HOJA_{ <fs_sheet>-number }| v_i_kind = 'TXT'
    v_i_records = lines( <fs_sheet>-rows ) v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
  ENDLOOP.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo process: seleccionar ordenes abiertas autorizadas y reunir los datos de cabecera y detalle.
*
 METHOD eval_set.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_eval> TYPE ty_eval.
  READ TABLE it_g_eval ASSIGNING <fs_eval> WITH KEY aufnr = v_i_order.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. RETURN. ENDIF.
  <fs_eval>-estado = v_i_state.
  <fs_eval>-motivo = v_i_reason.
  <fs_eval>-detalle = v_i_detail.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD eval_save.
  TYPES: BEGIN OF ty_reason_count, estado TYPE c LENGTH 1,
   motivo TYPE c LENGTH 30, cantidad TYPE i, END OF ty_reason_count.
  DATA: it_l_counts TYPE SORTED TABLE OF ty_reason_count WITH UNIQUE KEY estado motivo,
   wa_l_count TYPE ty_reason_count.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_reason> TYPE ty_reason_count.
  DATA: wa_l_metric_sheet TYPE ty_sheet, wa_l_metric_head TYPE ty_row,
   wa_l_metric_row TYPE ty_row, it_l_metric_heads TYPE ty_rows,
   v_l_partners TYPE i, v_l_positions TYPE i, v_l_ppart TYPE i, v_l_obj TYPE i,
   v_l_metric_count TYPE i.
  DATA: wa_l_eval TYPE ty_eval, it_l_lines TYPE ty_strings,
   v_l_binary TYPE xstring, v_l_a TYPE i, v_l_n TYPE i, v_l_r TYPE i.
  IF v_g_eval_saved = abap_true OR it_g_eval IS INITIAL. RETURN. ENDIF.
  confirmations_save( ).

* Cantidades corresponden a filas preparadas despues de las validaciones tecnicas.
  READ TABLE it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_metric_sheet WITH KEY number = 3.
  IF sy-subrc = 0. it_l_metric_heads = wa_l_metric_sheet-rows. ENDIF.
  LOOP AT it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_metric_sheet.
   DESCRIBE TABLE wa_l_metric_sheet-rows LINES v_l_metric_count.
   CASE wa_l_metric_sheet-number.
    WHEN 4. ADD v_l_metric_count TO v_g_stat_part.
    WHEN 5. ADD v_l_metric_count TO v_g_stat_pos.
    WHEN 6. ADD v_l_metric_count TO v_g_stat_ppart.
    WHEN 7. ADD v_l_metric_count TO v_g_stat_npos.
    WHEN 10. ADD v_l_metric_count TO v_g_stat_nhead.
    WHEN 11. ADD v_l_metric_count TO v_g_stat_resp.
   ENDCASE.
  ENDLOOP.
  LOOP AT it_l_metric_heads INTO wa_l_metric_head.
   IF v_g_stat_order_details >= p_prev. EXIT. ENDIF.
   ADD 1 TO v_g_stat_order_details.
   CLEAR: v_l_partners, v_l_positions, v_l_ppart, v_l_obj.
   LOOP AT it_g_sheets INTO wa_l_metric_sheet.
    LOOP AT wa_l_metric_sheet-rows INTO wa_l_metric_row WHERE c01 = wa_l_metric_head-c01.
     CASE wa_l_metric_sheet-number.
      WHEN 4. ADD 1 TO v_l_partners.
      WHEN 5. ADD 1 TO v_l_positions.
      WHEN 6. ADD 1 TO v_l_ppart.
      WHEN 8. ADD 1 TO v_l_obj.
      WHEN 11.
       log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'RESPONSABLE'
        v_i_order = CONV aufnr( wa_l_metric_head-c01 )
        v_i_message = |Responsable ZR recuperado; interlocutor { wa_l_metric_row-c02 }| ).
     ENDCASE.
    ENDLOOP.
   ENDLOOP.
   log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'RESULTADO_ORDEN'
    v_i_order = CONV aufnr( wa_l_metric_head-c01 )
    v_i_message = |Filas preparadas: posiciones { v_l_positions }; interlocutores de cabecera { v_l_partners }| ).
   IF v_l_ppart = 0.
    log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'RESULTADO_ORDEN'
     v_i_order = CONV aufnr( wa_l_metric_head-c01 )
     v_i_message = 'No se recuperaron interlocutores propios de las posiciones; A4 sin registros' ).
   ELSE.
    log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'RESULTADO_ORDEN'
     v_i_order = CONV aufnr( wa_l_metric_head-c01 )
     v_i_message = |Interlocutores propios de posiciones recuperados: { v_l_ppart }| ).
   ENDIF.
   IF v_l_obj = 0.
    log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'RESULTADO_ORDEN'
     v_i_order = CONV aufnr( wa_l_metric_head-c01 )
     v_i_message = 'No se recuperaron equipos o ubicaciones asociados a las posiciones; A7 sin registros' ).
   ELSE.
    log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'RESULTADO_ORDEN'
     v_i_order = CONV aufnr( wa_l_metric_head-c01 )
     v_i_message = |Objetos asociados a las posiciones recuperados: { v_l_obj }| ).
   ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT it_g_eval INTO wa_l_eval.
   READ TABLE it_l_counts ASSIGNING <fs_reason>
    WITH TABLE KEY estado = wa_l_eval-estado motivo = wa_l_eval-motivo.
   IF sy-subrc = 0. ADD 1 TO <fs_reason>-cantidad.
   ELSE.
    CLEAR wa_l_count. wa_l_count-estado = wa_l_eval-estado.
    wa_l_count-motivo = wa_l_eval-motivo. wa_l_count-cantidad = 1.
    INSERT wa_l_count INTO TABLE it_l_counts.
   ENDIF.
   CASE wa_l_eval-estado.
    WHEN 'A'. ADD 1 TO v_l_a.
    WHEN 'N'. ADD 1 TO v_l_n.
    WHEN OTHERS. ADD 1 TO v_l_r.
   ENDCASE.
  ENDLOOP.
  v_g_candidates = v_g_candidates + lines( it_g_eval ).
  ADD v_l_a TO v_g_accepted. ADD v_l_n TO v_g_excluded.
  ADD v_l_r TO v_g_unevaluated.
  LOOP AT it_l_counts INTO wa_l_count.
   log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'RESUMEN'
    v_i_message = |Paquete { v_g_batch }: filtro { wa_l_count-estado }, motivo { wa_l_count-motivo }, ordenes | &&
     |{ wa_l_count-cantidad }| ).
  ENDLOOP.
  it_l_lines = serialize( it_g_eval ).
  emit( v_i_entity = 'EVALUACION_ORDENES' v_i_kind = 'TXT'
   v_i_records = lines( it_g_eval ) v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
  IF it_g_poslink IS NOT INITIAL.
   it_l_lines = serialize( it_g_poslink ).
   emit( v_i_entity = 'TRAZA_POSICIONES' v_i_kind = 'TXT'
    v_i_records = lines( it_g_poslink ) v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
  ENDIF.
  IF it_g_diag IS NOT INITIAL.
   it_l_lines = serialize( it_g_diag ).
   emit( v_i_entity = 'DIAGNOSTICO_DATOS' v_i_kind = 'TXT'
    v_i_records = lines( it_g_diag ) v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
  ENDIF.
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'CONCILIACION'
   v_i_message = |Paquete: candidatas { lines( it_g_eval ) } = aceptadas { v_l_a } + excluidas { v_l_n } + no evaluadas | &&
    |{ v_l_r }| ).
  v_g_eval_saved = abap_true.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD diagnostics.
  DATA: v_lr TYPE REF TO data, it_l_status TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF jest,
   wa_l_status TYPE jest, it_l_resb TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF resb,
   wa_l_resb TYPE resb, wa_l_diag TYPE ty_diag,
   v_l_cnf TYPE abap_bool, v_l_pcnf TYPE abap_bool,
   v_l_deleted TYPE abap_bool, v_l_pending TYPE resb-bdmng,
   v_l_objnr TYPE jest-objnr, v_l_order TYPE aufnr,
   v_l_vornr TYPE afvc-vornr.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_t> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_op> TYPE any,
   <fs_order> TYPE any.
  IF it_operations IS NOT INITIAL.
   v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'JEST' it_parent = it_operations
    v_i_source = 'OBJNR' v_i_target = 'OBJNR' v_i_extra = |INACT = ' '| ).
   ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. it_l_status = <fs_t>.
   IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
   LOOP AT it_operations ASSIGNING <fs_op>.
    CLEAR: v_l_cnf, v_l_pcnf, v_l_deleted, wa_l_diag.
    v_l_objnr = value( wa_i_row = <fs_op> v_i_field = 'OBJNR' ).
    v_l_vornr = value( wa_i_row = <fs_op> v_i_field = 'VORNR' ).
    LOOP AT it_l_status INTO wa_l_status WHERE objnr = v_l_objnr.
     CASE wa_l_status-stat.
      WHEN 'I0009'. v_l_cnf = abap_true.
      WHEN 'I0010'. v_l_pcnf = abap_true.
      WHEN 'I0013' OR 'I0076'. v_l_deleted = abap_true.
     ENDCASE.
    ENDLOOP.
    wa_l_diag-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_diag-batch = v_g_batch.
    wa_l_diag-entidad = 'OPERACION'. wa_l_diag-clave = v_l_objnr.
    wa_l_diag-detalle = |VORNR { v_l_vornr }; CNF={ v_l_cnf }; PCNF={ v_l_pcnf }; borrado={ v_l_deleted }. Evidencia tecnica; v| &&
     |alidar clave de control y regla funcional.| .
    IF v_l_deleted = abap_true. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'BORRADO_ACTIVO'.
    ELSEIF v_l_cnf = abap_true. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'CNF_ACTIVO'.
    ELSEIF v_l_pcnf = abap_true. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'PCNF_ACTIVO'.
    ELSE. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'SIN_CNF_ACTIVO'. ENDIF.
* AUFNR no pertenece a AFVC: se resuelve fuera con los datos de AFKO en PROCESS.
    APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_g_diag.
   ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.
  IF it_orders IS INITIAL. RETURN. ENDIF.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'RESB' it_parent = it_orders
   v_i_source = 'AUFNR' v_i_target = 'AUFNR' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. it_l_resb = <fs_t>.
  IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
  LOOP AT it_orders ASSIGNING <fs_order>.
   v_l_order = value( wa_i_row = <fs_order> v_i_field = 'AUFNR' ).
   READ TABLE it_l_resb TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY aufnr = v_l_order.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    CLEAR wa_l_diag. wa_l_diag-runid = v_g_run.
    wa_l_diag-batch = v_g_batch. wa_l_diag-aufnr = v_l_order.
    wa_l_diag-entidad = 'COMPONENTES'. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'SIN_REGISTROS'.
    wa_l_diag-detalle = 'No hay RESB para esta orden; no impide posiciones de servicio'.
    APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_g_diag.
   ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  LOOP AT it_l_resb INTO wa_l_resb.
   CLEAR wa_l_diag. wa_l_diag-runid = v_g_run.
   wa_l_diag-batch = v_g_batch. wa_l_diag-aufnr = wa_l_resb-aufnr.
   wa_l_diag-entidad = 'COMPONENTE'.
   wa_l_diag-clave = |{ wa_l_resb-rsnum }/{ wa_l_resb-rspos }|.
   v_l_pending = wa_l_resb-bdmng - wa_l_resb-enmng.
   IF wa_l_resb-xloek IS NOT INITIAL. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'BORRADO'.
   ELSEIF wa_l_resb-kzear IS NOT INITIAL. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'SALIDA_FINAL'.
   ELSEIF v_l_pending > 0. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'CANTIDAD_PENDIENTE'.
   ELSE. wa_l_diag-resultado = 'SIN_CANTIDAD_PENDIENTE'. ENDIF.
   wa_l_diag-detalle = |MATNR { wa_l_resb-matnr }; requerida { wa_l_resb-bdmng }; retirada { wa_l_resb-enmng }; diferencia | &&
    |{ v_l_pending }; unidad { wa_l_resb-meins }. Solo se genera posicion si cumple A.5 y escenario B.| .
   APPEND wa_l_diag TO it_g_diag.
  ENDLOOP.
 ENDMETHOD.

 METHOD process.
  DATA: it_l_afih TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afih, wa_l_afih TYPE afih,
   it_l_afko TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afko, wa_l_afko TYPE afko,
   it_l_iloa TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF iloa, wa_l_iloa TYPE iloa,
   it_l_jest TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF jest, wa_l_jest TYPE jest,
   it_l_tj02t TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF tj02t, wa_l_tj02t TYPE tj02t,
   it_l_aufk TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF aufk, wa_l_aufk TYPE aufk,
   it_l_ihpa TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ihpa, wa_l_ihpa TYPE ihpa,
   it_l_afvc TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afvc, wa_l_afvc TYPE afvc,
   it_l_afvv TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afvv, wa_l_afvv TYPE afvv,
   it_l_keys TYPE ty_textkeys, wa_l_key TYPE ty_textkey,
   it_l_pkeys TYPE ty_textkeys, wa_l_head TYPE ty_head,
   wa_l_row TYPE ty_row, v_lr TYPE REF TO data,
   v_l_open TYPE abap_bool, v_l_ok TYPE abap_bool,
   v_l_status TYPE string, v_l_iso TYPE string, v_l_pos TYPE  n LENGTH 6,
   v_l_duplicate TYPE abap_bool, v_l_field TYPE string,
   v_l_prio TYPE string, v_l_idx TYPE i.
  TYPES: BEGIN OF ty_seen, aufnr TYPE aufnr, position TYPE n LENGTH 6, END OF ty_seen.
  DATA it_l_seen TYPE HASHED TABLE OF ty_seen WITH UNIQUE KEY aufnr position.
  DATA wa_l_seen TYPE ty_seen.
  DATA: wa_l_eval TYPE ty_eval, v_l_exclusion TYPE string,
   wa_l_diag TYPE ty_diag, it_l_diag_afvc TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afvc.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_diag> TYPE ty_diag.
  DATA: it_l_bapi_ops TYPE ty_bapi_operations,
   wa_l_bapi_op TYPE bapi_alm_order_operation_e,
   v_l_bapi_ok TYPE abap_bool, v_l_poscount TYPE i,
   v_l_skipped TYPE i, v_l_opno TYPE afvc-vornr,
   v_l_afvc_matches TYPE i, wa_l_afvc_text TYPE afvc.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_t> TYPE STANDARD TABLE, <fs_sheet> TYPE ty_sheet,
   <fs_cell> TYPE any.
* @013 todos los candidatos tienen resultado, incluso si una lectura aborta.
  CLEAR: it_g_eval, it_g_diag, it_g_poslink, it_g_origin, v_g_eval_saved.
  LOOP AT it_candidates INTO wa_l_aufk.
   CLEAR wa_l_eval.
   MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_l_aufk TO wa_l_eval.
   wa_l_eval-runid = v_g_run. wa_l_eval-batch = v_g_batch.
   wa_l_eval-estado = 'R'. wa_l_eval-motivo = 'NO_EVALUADA'.
   wa_l_eval-detalle = 'Evaluacion no completada; revisar error tecnico del paquete'.
   APPEND wa_l_eval TO it_g_eval.
  ENDLOOP.
  sheets_init( ).
  CLEAR: it_g_conf_audit, it_g_conf_sum.
  confirmations_audit( it_orders = it_candidates ).
  IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'AFIH' it_parent = it_candidates
   v_i_source = 'AUFNR' v_i_target = 'AUFNR' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_afih = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'AFKO' it_parent = it_candidates
   v_i_source = 'AUFNR' v_i_target = 'AUFNR' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_afko = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'ILOA' it_parent = it_l_afih
   v_i_source = 'ILOAN' v_i_target = 'ILOAN' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_iloa = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'JEST' it_parent = it_candidates
   v_i_source = 'OBJNR' v_i_target = 'OBJNR' v_i_extra = 'INACT = '' ''' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_jest = <fs_t>.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'TJ02T' it_parent = it_l_jest
   v_i_source = 'STAT' v_i_target = 'ISTAT'
   v_i_extra = |SPRAS = { literal( CONV string( p_lang ) ) }| ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_tj02t = <fs_t>.
  SORT it_l_afih BY aufnr. SORT it_l_afko BY aufnr. SORT it_l_iloa BY iloan.
  LOOP AT it_candidates INTO wa_l_aufk.
   READ TABLE it_l_afih INTO wa_l_afih WITH KEY aufnr = wa_l_aufk-aufnr BINARY SEARCH.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    eval_set( v_i_order = wa_l_aufk-aufnr v_i_state = 'N'
     v_i_reason = 'SIN_AFIH' v_i_detail = 'AUFK sin cabecera AFIH; excluida por la regla tecnica existente' ).
    CONTINUE.
   ENDIF.
   IF wa_l_afih-iwerk NOT IN s_iwerk.
    eval_set( v_i_order = wa_l_aufk-aufnr v_i_state = 'N'
     v_i_reason = 'FILTRO_IWERK' v_i_detail = |AFIH-IWERK { wa_l_afih-iwerk } fuera de seleccion| ).
    CONTINUE.
   ENDIF.
   CLEAR: wa_l_afko, wa_l_iloa.
   READ TABLE it_l_afko INTO wa_l_afko WITH KEY aufnr = wa_l_aufk-aufnr BINARY SEARCH.
   IF wa_l_afko-gstrp NOT IN s_gstrp.
    eval_set( v_i_order = wa_l_aufk-aufnr v_i_state = 'N'
     v_i_reason = 'FILTRO_GSTRP' v_i_detail = |AFKO-GSTRP { wa_l_afko-gstrp } fuera de seleccion; comprobar existencia AFKO| ).
    CONTINUE.
   ENDIF.
   READ TABLE it_l_iloa INTO wa_l_iloa WITH KEY iloan = wa_l_afih-iloan BINARY SEARCH.
   v_l_open = abap_true. v_l_ok = xsdbool( p_stat IS INITIAL ).
   CLEAR: v_l_status, v_l_exclusion.
   LOOP AT it_l_jest INTO wa_l_jest WHERE objnr = wa_l_aufk-objnr.
    IF wa_l_jest-stat = 'I0045' OR wa_l_jest-stat = 'I0046' OR
       wa_l_jest-stat = 'I0076' OR wa_l_jest-stat = 'I0013'.
     v_l_open = abap_false.
     v_l_exclusion = v_l_exclusion && wa_l_jest-stat && ` `.
    ENDIF.
    IF wa_l_jest-stat = p_stat. v_l_ok = abap_true. ENDIF.
    READ TABLE it_l_tj02t INTO wa_l_tj02t WITH KEY istat = wa_l_jest-stat.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
     IF v_l_status IS INITIAL. v_l_status = wa_l_tj02t-txt04.
     ELSE. v_l_status = v_l_status && ' ' && wa_l_tj02t-txt04. ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
   ENDLOOP.
   IF v_l_open = abap_false.
    eval_set( v_i_order = wa_l_aufk-aufnr v_i_state = 'N'
     v_i_reason = 'ESTADO_EXCLUIDO' v_i_detail = |JEST activo: { v_l_exclusion }; regla actual provisional| ).
    CONTINUE.
   ENDIF.
   IF v_l_ok = abap_false.
    eval_set( v_i_order = wa_l_aufk-aufnr v_i_state = 'N'
     v_i_reason = 'FILTRO_P_STAT' v_i_detail = |No presenta estado activo solicitado { p_stat }| ).
    CONTINUE.
   ENDIF.
   eval_set( v_i_order = wa_l_aufk-aufnr v_i_state = 'A'
    v_i_reason = 'ACEPTADA_REGLA_ACTUAL' v_i_detail =
     'Seleccionada por filtro existente; no implica validacion de carga ni decision WIP' ).
***   IF authorized( wa_i_aufk = wa_l_aufk wa_i_afih = wa_l_afih wa_i_iloa = wa_l_iloa ) = abap_false.
***    log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'AUTH' v_i_order = wa_l_aufk-aufnr
***     v_i_message = 'Orden excluida por autorizacion' ). CONTINUE.
***   ENDIF.
   APPEND wa_l_aufk TO it_l_aufk. ADD 1 TO v_g_total.
   CLEAR wa_l_head.
   MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_l_aufk TO wa_l_head.
   wa_l_head-gstrp = wa_l_afko-gstrp. wa_l_head-gltrp = wa_l_afko-gltrp.
   wa_l_head-status = v_l_status.
* @008 anexar cabecera despues de copiar sus valores de plantilla.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet> WITH KEY number = 3.
   CLEAR wa_l_row.
   wa_l_row-c01 = wa_l_aufk-aufnr. wa_l_row-c02 = wa_l_aufk-auart.
   IF p_num = 'I'. wa_l_row-c03 = 'X'. ENDIF.
   wa_l_row-c04 = wa_l_aufk-ktext. wa_l_row-c05 = iso( p_lang ).
   wa_l_row-c06 = wa_l_aufk-waers. wa_l_row-c08 = wa_l_afih-priok.
   IF wa_l_afko-gstrp IS NOT INITIAL. wa_l_row-c09 = wa_l_afko-gstrp. ENDIF.
   IF wa_l_afko-gltrp IS NOT INITIAL. wa_l_row-c12 = wa_l_afko-gltrp. ENDIF.
   wa_l_row-c17 = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_iloa v_i_field = 'VKORG' ).
   wa_l_row-c18 = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_iloa v_i_field = 'VTWEG' ).
   wa_l_row-c19 = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_iloa v_i_field = 'SPART' ).
   wa_l_row-c20 = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_iloa v_i_field = 'VKBUR' ).
   wa_l_row-c21 = value( wa_i_row = wa_l_iloa v_i_field = 'VKGRP' ).
   wa_l_row-c22 = wa_l_afih-kunum.
* @008 reflejar el llenado existente; no calcular nuevos valores de negocio.
   wa_l_head-mc_nrange = wa_l_row-c03.
   wa_l_head-mc_language = wa_l_row-c05.
   wa_l_head-mc_currency = wa_l_row-c06.
   wa_l_head-mc_contract = wa_l_row-c07.
   wa_l_head-mc_priority = wa_l_row-c08.
   IF wa_l_row-c09 IS NOT INITIAL.
    wa_l_head-mc_start_date = wa_l_row-c09.
   ENDIF.
   IF wa_l_row-c10 IS NOT INITIAL.
    wa_l_head-mc_start_time = wa_l_row-c10.
   ENDIF.
   wa_l_head-mc_start_tzone = wa_l_row-c11.
   IF wa_l_row-c12 IS NOT INITIAL.
    wa_l_head-mc_end_date = wa_l_row-c12.
   ENDIF.
   IF wa_l_row-c13 IS NOT INITIAL.
    wa_l_head-mc_end_time = wa_l_row-c13.
   ENDIF.
   wa_l_head-mc_end_tzone = wa_l_row-c14.
   wa_l_head-mc_purch_no = wa_l_row-c15.
   IF wa_l_row-c16 IS NOT INITIAL.
    wa_l_head-mc_purch_date = wa_l_row-c16.
   ENDIF.
   wa_l_head-mc_sales_org = wa_l_row-c17.
   wa_l_head-mc_dist_channel = wa_l_row-c18.
   wa_l_head-mc_division = wa_l_row-c19.
   wa_l_head-mc_sales_office = wa_l_row-c20.
   wa_l_head-mc_sales_group = wa_l_row-c21.
   wa_l_head-mc_sold_to = wa_l_row-c22.
   wa_l_head-mc_ship_to = wa_l_row-c23.
   wa_l_head-mc_bill_to = wa_l_row-c24.
   wa_l_head-mc_payer = wa_l_row-c25.
   wa_l_head-mc_contact = wa_l_row-c26.
   IF lines( it_g_head ) < p_prev. APPEND wa_l_head TO it_g_head. ENDIF.
   APPEND wa_l_row TO <fs_sheet>-rows.
   CLEAR wa_l_key. wa_l_key-aufnr = wa_l_aufk-aufnr.
   wa_l_key-name = sy-mandt && wa_l_aufk-aufnr. APPEND wa_l_key TO it_l_keys.
   wa_l_key-name = wa_l_aufk-aufnr. APPEND wa_l_key TO it_l_keys.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF it_l_aufk IS INITIAL. RETURN. ENDIF.
  v_lr = linked( v_i_table = 'IHPA' it_parent = it_l_aufk
   v_i_source = 'OBJNR' v_i_target = 'OBJNR' v_i_extra = 'KZLOESCH = '' ''' ).
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF. it_l_ihpa = <fs_t>.
  READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet> WITH KEY number = 4.
  LOOP AT it_l_ihpa INTO wa_l_ihpa.
   READ TABLE it_l_aufk INTO wa_l_aufk WITH KEY objnr = wa_l_ihpa-objnr.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
   CLEAR wa_l_row. wa_l_row-c01 = wa_l_aufk-aufnr.
   wa_l_row-c02 = wa_l_ihpa-parvw. wa_l_row-c03 = wa_l_ihpa-parnr.
   APPEND wa_l_row TO <fs_sheet>-rows.
  ENDLOOP.
  notes( it_keys = it_l_keys v_i_id = 'KOPF' v_i_sheet = 10 ).
  notes( it_keys = it_l_keys v_i_id = 'LTXT' v_i_sheet = 10 ).
  IF p_pos = 'X'.
   positions_pending( it_orders = it_l_aufk ).
   IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
  log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'MAPEO'
   v_i_message = 'Datos ECC directos; campos sin dato o fuente identificada quedan vacios. Ver COBERTURA_CAMPOS' ).
  cabecera_socios( it_partners = it_l_ihpa ).
  enrich_bapi( it_orders = it_l_aufk ).
  IF v_g_failed = abap_false. direct_items( ). ENDIF.
  IF v_g_failed = abap_false.
   it_g_origin = it_l_aufk.
   origin_reports( it_orders = it_l_aufk ).
  ENDIF.
  IF v_g_failed = abap_false. plantilla_audit( ). ENDIF.
  IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
  positions_final_diag( ).
  cobertura_save( ).
  IF v_g_failed = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
  IF p_simul = abap_true.
   log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'SIMULACION'
    v_i_message = 'Revision tecnica: se prepararon datos; no se genera Cockpit ni TXT de hojas' ).
  ELSE.
   READ TABLE it_g_sheets ASSIGNING <fs_sheet> WITH KEY number = 3.
   IF <fs_sheet>-rows IS NOT INITIAL. exports( ). ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo catalog: preparar el catalogo de columnas para la consulta de resultados.
*
 METHOD catalog.
  DATA: it_l_comp TYPE abap_component_tab,
   wa_l_comp LIKE LINE OF it_l_comp, wa_l_cat TYPE lvc_s_fcat,
   v_lo_elem TYPE REF TO cl_abap_elemdescr, wa_l_dfies TYPE dfies.
  it_l_comp = fields( it_data ).
  CLEAR it_r_cat.
  LOOP AT it_l_comp INTO wa_l_comp.
* @007 solo componentes elementales; los campos ocultos siguen en el layout.
   IF wa_l_comp-type->kind <> cl_abap_typedescr=>kind_elem. CONTINUE. ENDIF.
   CLEAR: wa_l_cat, wa_l_dfies.
   wa_l_cat-fieldname = wa_l_comp-name.
   v_lo_elem ?= wa_l_comp-type.
   wa_l_cat-inttype = v_lo_elem->type_kind.
   wa_l_cat-intlen = v_lo_elem->length.
   wa_l_cat-decimals_o = v_lo_elem->decimals.
   wa_l_cat-outputlen = 20.
   wa_l_cat-coltext = wa_l_comp-name.
   CLEAR: wa_l_cat-no_out, wa_l_cat-tech.
   IF v_lo_elem->is_ddic_type( ) = abap_true.
    v_lo_elem->get_ddic_field( EXPORTING p_langu = sy-langu
     RECEIVING p_flddescr = wa_l_dfies EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1 ).
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
     wa_l_cat-rollname = wa_l_dfies-rollname.
     wa_l_cat-datatype = wa_l_dfies-datatype.
     wa_l_cat-convexit = wa_l_dfies-convexit.
     wa_l_cat-scrtext_s = wa_l_dfies-scrtext_s.
     wa_l_cat-scrtext_m = wa_l_dfies-scrtext_m.
     wa_l_cat-scrtext_l = wa_l_dfies-scrtext_l.
     IF wa_l_dfies-scrtext_l IS NOT INITIAL.
      wa_l_cat-coltext = wa_l_dfies-scrtext_l.
     ELSEIF wa_l_dfies-fieldtext IS NOT INITIAL.
      wa_l_cat-coltext = wa_l_dfies-fieldtext.
     ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
   ENDIF.
   CASE wa_l_comp-name.
    WHEN 'MC_NRANGE'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Numeracion interna'.
    WHEN 'MC_LANGUAGE'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Idioma ISO'.
    WHEN 'MC_CURRENCY'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Moneda'.
    WHEN 'MC_CONTRACT'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Contrato de referencia'.
    WHEN 'MC_PRIORITY'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Prioridad'.
    WHEN 'MC_START_DATE'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Fecha de inicio deseada'.
    WHEN 'MC_START_TIME'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Hora de inicio deseada'.
    WHEN 'MC_START_TZONE'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Huso horario de inicio'.
    WHEN 'MC_END_DATE'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Fecha de fin deseada'.
    WHEN 'MC_END_TIME'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Hora de fin deseada'.
    WHEN 'MC_END_TZONE'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Huso horario de fin'.
    WHEN 'MC_PURCH_NO'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Pedido del cliente'.
    WHEN 'MC_PURCH_DATE'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Fecha de pedido del cliente'.
    WHEN 'MC_SALES_ORG'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Organizacion de ventas'.
    WHEN 'MC_DIST_CHANNEL'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Canal de distribucion'.
    WHEN 'MC_DIVISION'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Sector'.
    WHEN 'MC_SALES_OFFICE'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Oficina de ventas'.
    WHEN 'MC_SALES_GROUP'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Grupo de vendedores'.
    WHEN 'MC_SOLD_TO'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Solicitante'.
    WHEN 'MC_SHIP_TO'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Destinatario de mercancias'.
    WHEN 'MC_BILL_TO'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Destinatario de factura'.
    WHEN 'MC_PAYER'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Pagador'.
    WHEN 'MC_CONTACT'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Persona de contacto'.
    WHEN 'AUFNR'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Orden'. wa_l_cat-key = 'X'.
    WHEN 'AUART'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Tipo de orden'.
    WHEN 'KTEXT'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Descripcion de la orden'. wa_l_cat-outputlen = 40.
    WHEN 'WERKS'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Centro'.
    WHEN 'ERDAT'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Fecha de creacion'.
    WHEN 'GSTRP'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Inicio basico'.
    WHEN 'GLTRP'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Fin basico'.
    WHEN 'STATUS'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Estados activos'. wa_l_cat-outputlen = 35.
    WHEN 'VORNR'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Operacion'.
    WHEN 'LTXA1'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Descripcion de operacion'. wa_l_cat-outputlen = 40.
    WHEN 'ARBPL'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Puesto de trabajo'.
    WHEN 'PLANNED'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Trabajo previsto'. wa_l_cat-qfieldname = 'UNIT'.
    WHEN 'UNIT'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Unidad de trabajo'.
    WHEN 'ACTUAL'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Trabajo real'. wa_l_cat-qfieldname = 'UNIT'.
    WHEN 'PARVW'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Funcion de interlocutor'.
    WHEN 'PARNR'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Numero de interlocutor'.
    WHEN 'ERNAM'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Creado por'.
    WHEN 'AEDAT'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Fecha de modificacion'.
    WHEN 'AENAM'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Modificado por'.
    WHEN 'STAT'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Codigo de estado'.
    WHEN 'TIPO'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Tipo de estado'.
    WHEN 'TXT04'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Estado abreviado'.
    WHEN 'TXT30'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Descripcion del estado'.
    WHEN 'STSMA'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Perfil de estados'.
    WHEN 'EVIDENCIA'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Resultado de lectura'.
    WHEN 'ATNAM'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Caracteristica'.
    WHEN 'ATBEZ'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Descripcion de caracteristica'.
    WHEN 'ATWRT'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Valor de caracteristica'.
    WHEN 'ATFLV'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Valor numerico origen'.
    WHEN 'ATFLB'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Limite numerico origen'.
    WHEN 'TIPO_VALOR'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Relacion de valores origen'.
    WHEN 'UNIDAD'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Unidad origen'.
    WHEN 'INACT'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Estado inactivo'.
    WHEN 'MATNR'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Material'.
    WHEN 'BDMNG'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Cantidad necesaria'. wa_l_cat-qfieldname = 'MEINS'.
    WHEN 'ENMNG'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Cantidad retirada'. wa_l_cat-qfieldname = 'MEINS'.
    WHEN 'MEINS'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Unidad de medida'.
    WHEN 'LGORT'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Almacen'.
    WHEN 'KOSTL'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Centro de coste receptor'.
    WHEN 'KONTY'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Tipo de receptor'.
    WHEN 'PROZS'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Porcentaje de liquidacion'.
    WHEN 'PERBZ'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Tipo de liquidacion'.
    WHEN 'TDID'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'ID de texto'.
    WHEN 'TDSPRAS'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Idioma SAP'.
    WHEN 'ISO'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Idioma ISO'.
    WHEN 'POSITION'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Posicion'.
    WHEN 'CONTENT'. wa_l_cat-coltext = 'Texto completo'. wa_l_cat-outputlen = 80.
   ENDCASE.
   CASE wa_l_comp-name.
    WHEN 'AUFNR' OR 'AUART' OR 'KTEXT' OR 'WERKS' OR 'ERDAT'
      OR 'GSTRP' OR 'GLTRP' OR 'STATUS' OR 'VORNR' OR 'LTXA1'
      OR 'ARBPL' OR 'PLANNED' OR 'UNIT' OR 'ACTUAL' OR 'PARVW'
      OR 'PARNR' OR 'STAT' OR 'MATNR' OR 'BDMNG' OR 'ENMNG'
      OR 'MEINS' OR 'LGORT' OR 'KOSTL' OR 'KONTY' OR 'PROZS'
      OR 'PERBZ' OR 'TDID' OR 'TDSPRAS' OR 'ISO' OR 'POSITION' OR 'CONTENT'.
     CLEAR wa_l_cat-no_out.
   ENDCASE.
   IF sy-langu <> 'S' AND wa_l_dfies-scrtext_l IS NOT INITIAL.
    wa_l_cat-coltext = wa_l_dfies-scrtext_l.
   ENDIF.
   IF wa_l_comp-name = 'MC_LANGUAGE' OR wa_l_comp-name = 'MC_CURRENCY'
     OR wa_l_comp-name = 'MC_PRIORITY' OR wa_l_comp-name = 'MC_SOLD_TO'.
    CLEAR wa_l_cat-no_out.
   ENDIF.
   wa_l_cat-seltext = wa_l_cat-coltext.
   wa_l_cat-tooltip = wa_l_cat-coltext.
   IF wa_l_cat-scrtext_s IS INITIAL. wa_l_cat-scrtext_s = wa_l_cat-coltext. ENDIF.
   IF wa_l_cat-scrtext_m IS INITIAL. wa_l_cat-scrtext_m = wa_l_cat-coltext. ENDIF.
   IF wa_l_cat-scrtext_l IS INITIAL. wa_l_cat-scrtext_l = wa_l_cat-coltext. ENDIF.
   APPEND wa_l_cat TO it_r_cat.
  ENDLOOP.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo toolbar: incorporar los botones aprobados a cada panel del resultado.
*
 METHOD toolbar.
  DATA wa_l_button TYPE stb_button.
  IF v_i_bottom = abap_false.
   wa_l_button-function = 'XLSX'. wa_l_button-text = 'Excel (.xlsx)'. APPEND wa_l_button TO v_io_toolbar->mt_toolbar.
   wa_l_button-function = 'PDF'. wa_l_button-text = 'PDF consulta'. APPEND wa_l_button TO v_io_toolbar->mt_toolbar.
   wa_l_button-function = 'LOG'. wa_l_button-text = 'Log de ejecucion'.
   APPEND wa_l_button TO v_io_toolbar->mt_toolbar.
**   wa_l_button-function = 'LOGDET'. wa_l_button-text = 'Detalle por orden'.
   APPEND wa_l_button TO v_io_toolbar->mt_toolbar.
   wa_l_button-function = 'WIP'. wa_l_button-text = 'Consulta WIP'. APPEND wa_l_button TO v_io_toolbar->mt_toolbar.
**   wa_l_button-function = 'VALPL'. wa_l_button-text = 'Validar plantilla'. APPEND wa_l_button TO v_io_toolbar->mt_toolbar.
  ELSE.
   wa_l_button-function = 'OPS'. wa_l_button-text = 'Operaciones'. APPEND wa_l_button TO v_io_toolbar->mt_toolbar.
   wa_l_button-function = 'COMP'. wa_l_button-text = 'Componentes'. APPEND wa_l_button TO v_io_toolbar->mt_toolbar.
   wa_l_button-function = 'STAT'. wa_l_button-text = 'Estados'. APPEND wa_l_button TO v_io_toolbar->mt_toolbar.
   wa_l_button-function = 'PART'. wa_l_button-text = 'Interlocutores'. APPEND wa_l_button TO v_io_toolbar->mt_toolbar.
   wa_l_button-function = 'SETT'. wa_l_button-text = 'Liquidacion'. APPEND wa_l_button TO v_io_toolbar->mt_toolbar.
   wa_l_button-function = 'TEXT'. wa_l_button-text = 'Textos largos'. APPEND wa_l_button TO v_io_toolbar->mt_toolbar.
   wa_l_button-function = 'CLASS'. wa_l_button-text = 'Clasificacion'. APPEND wa_l_button TO v_io_toolbar->mt_toolbar.
  ENDIF.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo screen: crear los contenedores y presentar las cabeceras y el detalle inicial.
*
 METHOD screen.
  DATA: v_lo_up TYPE REF TO cl_gui_container, v_lo_down TYPE REF TO cl_gui_container,
   it_l_cat TYPE lvc_t_fcat, wa_l_layout TYPE lvc_s_layo,
   wa_l_variant TYPE disvariant, wa_l_head TYPE ty_head.
  IF v_go_container IS BOUND. RETURN. ENDIF.
  CREATE OBJECT v_go_events.
  CREATE OBJECT v_go_container EXPORTING container_name = 'CC_MAIN'.
  CREATE OBJECT v_go_split EXPORTING parent = v_go_container rows = 2 columns = 1.
  v_lo_up = v_go_split->get_container( row = 1 column = 1 ).
  v_lo_down = v_go_split->get_container( row = 2 column = 1 ).
  v_go_split->set_row_height( id = 1 height = 55 ).
  CREATE OBJECT v_go_top EXPORTING i_parent = v_lo_up.
  CREATE OBJECT v_go_bottom EXPORTING i_parent = v_lo_down.
  SET HANDLER v_go_events->top_toolbar FOR v_go_top.
  SET HANDLER v_go_events->bottom_toolbar FOR v_go_bottom.
  SET HANDLER v_go_events->command FOR v_go_top.
  SET HANDLER v_go_events->command FOR v_go_bottom.
  SET HANDLER v_go_events->double FOR v_go_top.
  SET HANDLER v_go_events->note FOR v_go_bottom.
  it_l_cat = catalog( it_g_head ).
* @009 titulos exactos de Cabecera 2025, solo en el ALV superior.
  DATA wa_l_headcat TYPE lvc_s_fcat.
  LOOP AT it_l_cat INTO wa_l_headcat.
   CASE wa_l_headcat-fieldname.
    WHEN 'AUFNR'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 1.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Orden de servicio*'.
    WHEN 'AUART'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 2.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Clase'.
    WHEN 'MC_NRANGE'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 3.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Indicador de control p.rango números'.
    WHEN 'KTEXT'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 4.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Descripción'.
    WHEN 'MC_LANGUAGE'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 5.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Idioma'.
    WHEN 'MC_CURRENCY'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 6.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Moneda'.
    WHEN 'MC_CONTRACT'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 7.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Contrato de servicio de referencia'.
    WHEN 'MC_PRIORITY'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 8.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Prioridad'.
    WHEN 'MC_START_DATE'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 9.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Fecha de inicio deseada'.
    WHEN 'MC_START_TIME'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 10.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Inicio deseado (hora)'.
    WHEN 'MC_START_TZONE'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 11.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Inicio deseado (huso horario)'.
    WHEN 'MC_END_DATE'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 12.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Fecha de cierre deseada'.
    WHEN 'MC_END_TIME'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 13.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Final deseado (hora)'.
    WHEN 'MC_END_TZONE'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 14.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Fin deseado (huso horario)'.
    WHEN 'MC_PURCH_NO'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 15.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Pedido de compras'.
    WHEN 'MC_PURCH_DATE'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 16.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Fecha de pedido'.
    WHEN 'MC_SALES_ORG'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 17.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Organización de ventas'.
    WHEN 'MC_DIST_CHANNEL'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 18.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Canal de distribución'.
    WHEN 'MC_DIVISION'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 19.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Sector'.
    WHEN 'MC_SALES_OFFICE'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 20.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Oficina de ventas'.
    WHEN 'MC_SALES_GROUP'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 21.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Grupo de vendedores'.
    WHEN 'MC_SOLD_TO'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 22.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Solicitante'.
    WHEN 'MC_SHIP_TO'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 23.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Destinatario de mercancías'.
    WHEN 'MC_BILL_TO'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 24.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Destinatario de la factura'.
    WHEN 'MC_PAYER'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 25.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Pagador'.
    WHEN 'MC_CONTACT'. wa_l_headcat-col_pos = 26.
     wa_l_headcat-coltext = 'Persona de contacto'.
* @010 cabecera: solo las 26 columnas de la plantilla, incluso en layout.
    WHEN OTHERS.
     DELETE it_l_cat INDEX sy-tabix.
     CONTINUE.
   ENDCASE.
   wa_l_headcat-seltext = wa_l_headcat-coltext.
   wa_l_headcat-tooltip = wa_l_headcat-coltext.
   wa_l_headcat-scrtext_s = wa_l_headcat-coltext.
   wa_l_headcat-scrtext_m = wa_l_headcat-coltext.
   wa_l_headcat-scrtext_l = wa_l_headcat-coltext.
   IF wa_l_headcat-outputlen < strlen( wa_l_headcat-coltext ).
    wa_l_headcat-outputlen = strlen( wa_l_headcat-coltext ).
   ENDIF.
   MODIFY it_l_cat FROM wa_l_headcat INDEX sy-tabix.
  ENDLOOP.
  wa_l_layout-zebra = 'X'. wa_l_layout-sel_mode = 'A'. wa_l_layout-cwidth_opt = 'X'.
  wa_l_layout-grid_title = |Ordenes extraidas - muestra { lines( it_g_head ) } de { v_g_total }|.
  wa_l_variant-report = sy-repid. wa_l_variant-handle = 'CAB4'.
  v_go_top->set_table_for_first_display(
   EXPORTING is_layout = wa_l_layout is_variant = wa_l_variant i_save = 'A' i_default = space
   CHANGING it_outtab = it_g_head it_fieldcatalog = it_l_cat ).
  READ TABLE it_g_head INDEX 1 INTO wa_l_head.
  IF sy-subrc = 0. v_g_selected = wa_l_head-aufnr. ENDIF.
  display_detail( 'OPS' ).
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo selected: establecer la orden elegida y refrescar su detalle.
*
 METHOD selected.
  DATA wa_l_head TYPE ty_head.
  READ TABLE it_g_head INDEX v_i_row INTO wa_l_head.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. RETURN. ENDIF.
  v_g_selected = wa_l_head-aufnr.
  IF v_g_handle IS INITIAL. ADD 1 TO v_g_batch. bal_start( ). ENDIF.
  display_detail( v_g_kind ). bal_save( ).
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo display_detail: leer y mostrar la entidad solicitada para la orden seleccionada.
*
 METHOD display_detail.
  DATA: it_l_aufk TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF aufk, wa_l_aufk TYPE aufk,
   it_l_afko TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afko, it_l_afvc TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afvc,
   wa_l_afvc TYPE afvc, it_l_afvv TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF afvv, wa_l_afvv TYPE afvv,
   it_l_crhd TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF crhd, wa_l_crhd TYPE crhd,
   it_l_ops TYPE ty_operations, wa_l_op TYPE ty_operation,
   it_l_notes TYPE ty_notes, wa_l_note TYPE ty_note,
   v_lr TYPE REF TO data, it_l_cat TYPE lvc_t_fcat,
   wa_l_layout TYPE lvc_s_layo, wa_l_variant TYPE disvariant,
   v_l_table TYPE tabname, v_l_extra TYPE string,
   v_l_count TYPE i.
  DATA: it_l_bapi_ops TYPE ty_bapi_operations,
   wa_l_bapi_op TYPE bapi_alm_order_operation_e,
   v_l_bapi_ok TYPE abap_bool.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_t> TYPE STANDARD TABLE.
  IF v_g_selected IS INITIAL. RETURN. ENDIF.
*
* revalidar existencia y autorizacion antes de leer detalles al navegar.
*
  SELECT SINGLE * INTO wa_l_aufk FROM aufk WHERE aufnr = v_g_selected.
  DATA wa_l_afih TYPE afih. DATA wa_l_iloa TYPE iloa.
  SELECT SINGLE * INTO wa_l_afih FROM afih WHERE aufnr = v_g_selected.
  SELECT SINGLE * INTO wa_l_iloa FROM iloa WHERE iloan = wa_l_afih-iloan.
**  IF authorized( wa_i_aufk = wa_l_aufk wa_i_afih = wa_l_afih wa_i_iloa = wa_l_iloa ) = abap_false.
**   MESSAGE 'Sin autorizacion para consultar detalle' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. RETURN.
**  ENDIF.
  APPEND wa_l_aufk TO it_l_aufk.
  v_g_kind = v_i_kind. IF v_g_kind IS INITIAL. v_g_kind = 'OPS'. ENDIF.
  CASE v_g_kind.
   WHEN 'OPS'.
    bapi_operations(
     EXPORTING v_i_order = v_g_selected
     IMPORTING it_e_operations = it_l_bapi_ops v_e_ok = v_l_bapi_ok ).
    IF v_l_bapi_ok = abap_false.
     bal_save( ).
     MESSAGE 'No se pudieron leer operaciones BAPI; revisar SLG1' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
     RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    IF lines( it_l_bapi_ops ) > p_det.
     MESSAGE 'Operaciones superan P_DET; reduzca la seleccion' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
     RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    LOOP AT it_l_bapi_ops INTO wa_l_bapi_op.
     CLEAR wa_l_op.
     wa_l_op-aufnr = v_g_selected.
     wa_l_op-vornr = wa_l_bapi_op-activity.
     wa_l_op-ltxa1 = wa_l_bapi_op-description.
     wa_l_op-arbpl = wa_l_bapi_op-work_cntr.
     wa_l_op-planned = wa_l_bapi_op-work_activity.
     wa_l_op-unit = wa_l_bapi_op-un_work.
     wa_l_op-actual = wa_l_bapi_op-work_actual.
     APPEND wa_l_op TO it_l_ops.
    ENDLOOP.
    CREATE DATA v_lr LIKE it_l_ops. ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. <fs_t> = it_l_ops.
   WHEN 'STAT'. v_lr = states_read( it_orders = it_l_aufk ).
   WHEN 'CLASS'. v_lr = classes_read( it_orders = it_l_aufk ).
   WHEN 'TEXT'.
    LOOP AT it_g_notes INTO wa_l_note WHERE aufnr = v_g_selected. APPEND wa_l_note TO it_l_notes. ENDLOOP.
    CREATE DATA v_lr LIKE it_l_notes. ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>. <fs_t> = it_l_notes.
   WHEN OTHERS.
    CASE v_g_kind.
     WHEN 'COMP'. v_l_table = 'RESB'. v_l_extra = 'XLOEK = '' '''.
     WHEN 'STAT'. v_l_table = 'JEST'. v_l_extra = 'INACT = '' '''.
     WHEN 'PART'. v_l_table = 'IHPA'. v_l_extra = 'KZLOESCH = '' '''.
     WHEN 'SETT'. v_l_table = 'COBRB'.
    ENDCASE.
    IF v_g_kind = 'COMP'.
     v_lr = linked( v_i_table = v_l_table it_parent = it_l_aufk v_i_source = 'AUFNR' v_i_target = 'AUFNR' v_i_extra = v_l_extra ).
    ELSE.
     v_lr = linked( v_i_table = v_l_table it_parent = it_l_aufk v_i_source = 'OBJNR' v_i_target = 'OBJNR' v_i_extra = v_l_extra ).
    ENDIF.
  ENDCASE.
  ASSIGN v_lr->* TO <fs_t>.
  v_gr_detail = v_lr. it_l_cat = catalog( <fs_t> ).
  wa_l_layout-zebra = 'X'. wa_l_layout-cwidth_opt = 'X'.
  CASE v_g_kind.
   WHEN 'OPS'. wa_l_layout-grid_title = |Operaciones de orden { v_g_selected }|.
   WHEN 'COMP'. wa_l_layout-grid_title = |Componentes de orden { v_g_selected }|.
   WHEN 'STAT'. wa_l_layout-grid_title = |Estados de orden { v_g_selected }|.
   WHEN 'PART'. wa_l_layout-grid_title = |Interlocutores de orden { v_g_selected }|.
   WHEN 'SETT'. wa_l_layout-grid_title = |Reglas de liquidacion de orden { v_g_selected }|.
   WHEN 'CLASS'. wa_l_layout-grid_title = |Caracteristicas de orden { v_g_selected }; clase { p_klart }|.
   WHEN 'TEXT'. wa_l_layout-grid_title = |Textos largos de orden { v_g_selected }|.
  ENDCASE.
  wa_l_variant-report = sy-repid. wa_l_variant-handle = v_g_kind.
  v_go_bottom->set_table_for_first_display(
   EXPORTING is_layout = wa_l_layout is_variant = wa_l_variant i_save = 'A' i_default = space
   CHANGING it_outtab = <fs_t> it_fieldcatalog = it_l_cat ).
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo note_popup: mostrar el contenido completo del texto elegido en la consulta.
*
 METHOD note_popup.
  TYPES ty_char132 TYPE c LENGTH 132.
  DATA: wa_l_note TYPE ty_note, it_l_lines TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_char132,
   it_l_strings TYPE ty_strings, v_l_string TYPE string, v_l_line TYPE ty_char132.
  IF v_g_kind <> 'TEXT'. RETURN. ENDIF.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_t> TYPE STANDARD TABLE.
  ASSIGN v_gr_detail->* TO <fs_t>.
  READ TABLE <fs_t> INDEX v_i_row INTO wa_l_note.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. RETURN. ENDIF.
  SPLIT wa_l_note-content AT cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline INTO TABLE it_l_strings.
  LOOP AT it_l_strings INTO v_l_string.
   WHILE strlen( v_l_string ) > 132.
    v_l_line = v_l_string(132). APPEND v_l_line TO it_l_lines. v_l_string = v_l_string+132.
   ENDWHILE.
   v_l_line = v_l_string. APPEND v_l_line TO it_l_lines.
  ENDLOOP.
  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_WITH_TABLE_DISPLAY'
   EXPORTING startpos_col = 5 startpos_row = 5 endpos_col = 120
    endpos_row = 25 titletext = 'Texto largo de cabecera o posicion'
   TABLES valuetab = it_l_lines EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo action: procesar los comandos de descarga y navegacion del resultado.
*
 METHOD action.
  DATA: it_l_rows TYPE lvc_t_row, wa_l_row TYPE lvc_s_row.
  IF v_i_code = 'LOG'." OR v_i_code = 'LOGDET'.
   bal_save( ).
   display_log( v_i_detail = xsdbool( v_i_code = 'LOGDET' ) ).
   RETURN.
  ENDIF.
  IF v_g_handle IS INITIAL. ADD 1 TO v_g_batch. bal_start( ). ENDIF.
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'CONSULTA' v_i_message = CONV string( v_i_code ) ).
  CASE v_i_code.
   WHEN 'XLSX' OR 'TXT'. download( CONV char10( v_i_code ) ).
   WHEN 'PDF'. pdf( ).
   WHEN 'WIP'. wip_show( ).
   WHEN 'VALPL'. plantilla_show( ).
   WHEN 'OPS' OR 'COMP' OR 'STAT' OR 'PART' OR 'SETT' OR 'TEXT' OR 'CLASS'.
    v_go_top->get_selected_rows( IMPORTING et_index_rows = it_l_rows ).
    READ TABLE it_l_rows INDEX 1 INTO wa_l_row.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
     DATA wa_l_head TYPE ty_head.
     READ TABLE it_g_head INDEX wa_l_row-index INTO wa_l_head.
     IF sy-subrc = 0. v_g_selected = wa_l_head-aufnr. ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    display_detail( CONV char10( v_i_code ) ).
  ENDCASE.
  bal_save( ).
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo display_log: consultar los registros persistidos de la ejecucion con autorizacion.
*
 METHOD display_log.
  DATA: it_l_ext TYPE bal_r_extn, wa_l_ext LIKE LINE OF it_l_ext,
   it_l_obj TYPE bal_r_obj, wa_l_obj LIKE LINE OF it_l_obj,
   it_l_sub TYPE bal_r_sub, wa_l_sub LIKE LINE OF it_l_sub,
   wa_l_filter TYPE bal_s_lfil, it_l_headers TYPE balhdr_t,
   it_l_handles TYPE bal_t_logh, it_l_memory TYPE bal_t_logh,
   v_l_handle TYPE balloghndl, v_l_display_rc TYPE sysubrc,
   wa_l_profile TYPE bal_s_prof,
   wa_l_msg_filter TYPE bal_s_mfil,
   wa_l_level LIKE LINE OF wa_l_msg_filter-detlevel.
  AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT 'S_APPL_LOG' ID 'ALG_OBJECT' FIELD 'ZSM_OS'
   ID 'ALG_SUBOBJ' FIELD 'EXTRACCION' ID 'ACTVT' FIELD '03'.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. MESSAGE 'Sin autorizacion de consulta SLG1' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. RETURN. ENDIF.
  wa_l_ext-sign = 'I'. wa_l_ext-option = 'CP'. wa_l_ext-low = v_g_run && '*'. APPEND wa_l_ext TO it_l_ext.
  wa_l_obj-sign = 'I'. wa_l_obj-option = 'EQ'. wa_l_obj-low = 'ZSM_OS'. APPEND wa_l_obj TO it_l_obj.
  wa_l_sub-sign = 'I'. wa_l_sub-option = 'EQ'. wa_l_sub-low = 'EXTRACCION'. APPEND wa_l_sub TO it_l_sub.
  wa_l_filter-extnumber = it_l_ext. wa_l_filter-object = it_l_obj. wa_l_filter-subobject = it_l_sub.
  CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_DB_SEARCH' EXPORTING i_s_log_filter = wa_l_filter
   IMPORTING e_t_log_header = it_l_headers EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR it_l_headers IS INITIAL.
   MESSAGE 'No se encontraron logs guardados para esta ejecucion' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. RETURN.
  ENDIF.
* Una consulta anterior puede haber dejado el mismo log cargado en memoria.
* Liberar solo copias del RUN actual; los registros guardados en SLG1 permanecen.
  CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_GLB_SEARCH_LOG'
   EXPORTING i_s_log_filter = wa_l_filter
   IMPORTING e_t_log_handle = it_l_memory EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  LOOP AT it_l_memory INTO v_l_handle.
   CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_LOG_REFRESH'
    EXPORTING i_log_handle = v_l_handle EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE 'No se pudo liberar la consulta previa del log; volver al ALV e intentar de nuevo' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
    RETURN.
   ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  CLEAR it_l_handles.
  CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_DB_LOAD' EXPORTING i_t_log_header = it_l_headers
   IMPORTING e_t_log_handle = it_l_handles EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR it_l_handles IS INITIAL.
   MESSAGE 'No fue posible cargar el log de aplicacion' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. RETURN.
  ENDIF.
* Perfil estandar explicito: cabeceras de logs y mensajes, como en SLG1.
  CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_DSP_PROFILE_STANDARD_GET'
   IMPORTING e_s_display_profile = wa_l_profile.
  IF v_i_detail = abap_false.
   wa_l_level-sign = 'I'. wa_l_level-option = 'EQ'. wa_l_level-low = '0'.
   APPEND wa_l_level TO wa_l_msg_filter-detlevel.
  ENDIF.
  CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_DSP_LOG_DISPLAY'
   EXPORTING i_t_log_handle = it_l_handles i_s_display_profile = wa_l_profile
    i_s_msg_filter = wa_l_msg_filter
   EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  v_l_display_rc = sy-subrc.
* Salir del visor no debe impedir abrir otra vez Log de ejecucion o Detalle.
  LOOP AT it_l_handles INTO v_l_handle.
   CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_LOG_REFRESH'
    EXPORTING i_log_handle = v_l_handle EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF v_l_display_rc <> 0.
   MESSAGE 'No fue posible mostrar el log de aplicacion' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
  ENDIF.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @006 metodo download: mostrar los archivos ya guardados en el pc para el formato elegido.
*

*
* @006 implementacion anterior en servidor, conservada solo como referencia.
*
* METHOD download.
*   DATA: v_l_folder TYPE string, wa_l_file TYPE ty_file, v_l_binary TYPE xstring,
*    it_l_binary TYPE solix_tab, v_l_size TYPE i, v_l_local TYPE string,
*    v_l_leaf TYPE string, it_l_parts TYPE ty_strings, v_l_part TYPE string,
*    v_l_rc TYPE sysubrc.
*   cl_gui_frontend_services=>directory_browse(
*    EXPORTING window_title = 'Directorio para archivos de la ejecucion'
*    CHANGING selected_folder = v_l_folder EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1 ).
*   IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR v_l_folder IS INITIAL. RETURN. ENDIF.
*   LOOP AT it_g_files INTO wa_l_file WHERE kind = v_i_kind AND state = 'OK'.
*    CALL FUNCTION 'AUTHORITY_CHECK_DATASET'
*     EXPORTING program = sy-repid activity = 'READ' filename = wa_l_file-path
*     EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
*    IF sy-subrc <> 0. MESSAGE 'Sin permiso de lectura del archivo servidor' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. RETURN. ENDIF.
*    OPEN DATASET wa_l_file-path FOR INPUT IN BINARY MODE.
*    IF sy-subrc <> 0. MESSAGE 'Archivo servidor no disponible' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. RETURN. ENDIF.
*    CLEAR v_l_binary. READ DATASET wa_l_file-path INTO v_l_binary.
*    CLOSE DATASET wa_l_file-path.
*    CALL FUNCTION 'SCMS_XSTRING_TO_BINARY'
*     EXPORTING buffer = v_l_binary IMPORTING output_length = v_l_size TABLES binary_tab = it_l_binary.
*    v_l_leaf = wa_l_file-path. REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '\' IN v_l_leaf WITH '/'.
*    SPLIT v_l_leaf AT '/' INTO TABLE it_l_parts.
*    READ TABLE it_l_parts INDEX lines( it_l_parts ) INTO v_l_leaf.
*    v_l_local = v_l_folder && '/' && v_l_leaf.
*    cl_gui_frontend_services=>gui_download(
*     EXPORTING filename = v_l_local filetype = 'BIN' bin_filesize = v_l_size
*     CHANGING data_tab = it_l_binary EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1 ).
*    IF sy-subrc <> 0. MESSAGE 'Fallo descarga local' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. RETURN. ENDIF.
*   ENDLOOP.
*   MESSAGE 'Archivos descargados; no dependen del layout ni de la muestra ALV' TYPE 'S'.
*  ENDMETHOD.
*
* @006 implementacion local para pc.
*
 METHOD download.
  TYPES: BEGIN OF ty_choice,
   numero TYPE i, paquete TYPE i, formato TYPE c LENGTH 10,
   contenido TYPE c LENGTH 30, registros TYPE i, ruta TYPE c LENGTH 1024,
   END OF ty_choice.
  DATA: it_l_files TYPE ty_files, wa_l_file TYPE ty_file,
   it_l_choices TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_choice, wa_l_choice TYPE ty_choice,
   it_l_fcat TYPE slis_t_fieldcat_alv, wa_l_fcat TYPE slis_fieldcat_alv,
   wa_l_pick TYPE slis_selfield, v_l_exit TYPE c LENGTH 1,
   v_l_index TYPE sy-tabix, v_l_no TYPE i,
   v_l_name TYPE string, v_l_path TYPE string, v_l_fullpath TYPE string,
   v_l_extension TYPE string, v_l_filter TYPE string,
   it_l_parts TYPE ty_strings, v_l_part TYPE string,
   v_l_source TYPE string, v_l_action TYPE i,
   it_l_binary TYPE solix_tab, v_l_bytes TYPE i,
   v_lx TYPE REF TO cx_root.
  IF sy-batch = abap_true.
   MESSAGE 'Guardar como requiere SAP GUI en dialogo' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. RETURN.
  ENDIF.
  LOOP AT it_g_files INTO wa_l_file WHERE kind = v_i_kind AND state = 'OK'.
   APPEND wa_l_file TO it_l_files.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF it_l_files IS INITIAL.
   MESSAGE 'No hay archivos generados de ese formato; revisar modo simulado y log' TYPE 'S'. RETURN.
  ENDIF.
  TRY.
   v_l_index = 1.
   IF lines( it_l_files ) > 1.
    LOOP AT it_l_files INTO wa_l_file.
     ADD 1 TO v_l_no. CLEAR wa_l_choice.
     wa_l_choice-numero = v_l_no. wa_l_choice-paquete = wa_l_file-batch.
     wa_l_choice-formato = wa_l_file-kind. wa_l_choice-contenido = wa_l_file-entity.
     wa_l_choice-registros = wa_l_file-records. wa_l_choice-ruta = wa_l_file-path.
     APPEND wa_l_choice TO it_l_choices.
    ENDLOOP.
    CLEAR wa_l_fcat. wa_l_fcat-fieldname = 'NUMERO'. wa_l_fcat-seltext_l = 'Archivo'.
    wa_l_fcat-outputlen = 7. APPEND wa_l_fcat TO it_l_fcat.
    CLEAR wa_l_fcat. wa_l_fcat-fieldname = 'PAQUETE'. wa_l_fcat-seltext_l = 'Paquete'.
    wa_l_fcat-outputlen = 8. APPEND wa_l_fcat TO it_l_fcat.
    CLEAR wa_l_fcat. wa_l_fcat-fieldname = 'FORMATO'. wa_l_fcat-seltext_l = 'Formato'.
    wa_l_fcat-outputlen = 8. APPEND wa_l_fcat TO it_l_fcat.
    CLEAR wa_l_fcat. wa_l_fcat-fieldname = 'CONTENIDO'. wa_l_fcat-seltext_l = 'Contenido'.
    wa_l_fcat-outputlen = 22. APPEND wa_l_fcat TO it_l_fcat.
    CLEAR wa_l_fcat. wa_l_fcat-fieldname = 'REGISTROS'. wa_l_fcat-seltext_l = 'Ordenes / registros'.
    wa_l_fcat-outputlen = 18. APPEND wa_l_fcat TO it_l_fcat.
    CLEAR wa_l_fcat. wa_l_fcat-fieldname = 'RUTA'. wa_l_fcat-seltext_l = 'Archivo generado'.
    wa_l_fcat-outputlen = 70. APPEND wa_l_fcat TO it_l_fcat.
    CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_POPUP_TO_SELECT'
     EXPORTING i_title = 'Seleccione el archivo que desea guardar'
      i_selection = abap_true i_zebra = abap_true i_tabname = 'IT_L_CHOICES'
      it_fieldcat = it_l_fcat
     IMPORTING es_selfield = wa_l_pick e_exit = v_l_exit
     TABLES t_outtab = it_l_choices EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
     MESSAGE 'No fue posible mostrar la seleccion de archivos' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    IF v_l_exit = abap_true. RETURN. ENDIF.
    v_l_index = wa_l_pick-tabindex.
    IF v_l_index IS INITIAL.
     MESSAGE 'Seleccione un archivo para guardarlo' TYPE 'S'. RETURN.
    ENDIF.
   ENDIF.
   READ TABLE it_l_files INTO wa_l_file INDEX v_l_index.
   IF sy-subrc <> 0. RETURN. ENDIF.
   v_l_source = wa_l_file-path.
   REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '\' IN v_l_source WITH '/'.
   SPLIT v_l_source AT '/' INTO TABLE it_l_parts.
   LOOP AT it_l_parts INTO v_l_part. v_l_name = v_l_part. ENDLOOP.
   v_l_extension = v_i_kind. TRANSLATE v_l_extension TO LOWER CASE.
   IF v_i_kind = 'XLSX'. v_l_filter = 'Excel (*.xlsx)|*.xlsx|'.
   ELSEIF v_i_kind = 'PDF'. v_l_filter = 'PDF (*.pdf)|*.pdf|'.
   ELSE. v_l_filter = 'Todos los archivos (*.*)|*.*|'. ENDIF.
   cl_gui_frontend_services=>file_save_dialog(
    EXPORTING window_title = 'Guardar archivo generado'
     default_extension = v_l_extension default_file_name = v_l_name
     file_filter = v_l_filter initial_directory = v_g_pc_dir
    CHANGING filename = v_l_name path = v_l_path fullpath = v_l_fullpath user_action = v_l_action
    EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1 ).
   IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE 'No fue posible abrir Guardar como' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. RETURN.
   ENDIF.
   IF v_l_action = cl_gui_frontend_services=>action_cancel OR v_l_fullpath IS INITIAL. RETURN. ENDIF.
* El libro ya fue generado. Copiarlo en binario conserva hojas, columnas y textos.
   cl_gui_frontend_services=>gui_upload(
    EXPORTING filename = wa_l_file-path filetype = 'BIN'
    IMPORTING filelength = v_l_bytes
    CHANGING data_tab = it_l_binary EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1 ).
   IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR v_l_bytes <= 0.
    MESSAGE 'No se pudo leer el archivo generado; comprobar que siga en la carpeta original' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. RETURN.
   ENDIF.
   cl_gui_frontend_services=>gui_download(
    EXPORTING filename = v_l_fullpath filetype = 'BIN' bin_filesize = v_l_bytes
     confirm_overwrite = abap_true
    CHANGING data_tab = it_l_binary EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1 ).
   IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE 'No se pudo guardar el archivo; revisar permisos o si esta abierto en Excel' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. RETURN.
   ENDIF.
   log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'ARCHIVO' v_i_message = |Copia guardada correctamente: { v_l_fullpath }| ).
   MESSAGE 'Archivo guardado correctamente en la ubicacion seleccionada' TYPE 'S'.
  CATCH cx_root INTO v_lx.
   MESSAGE v_lx->get_text( ) TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
  ENDTRY.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo pdf: crear el resumen de cabeceras mediante spool y convertirlo a pdf.
*
 METHOD pdf.
  TYPES ty_char132 TYPE c LENGTH 132.
  DATA: it_l_print TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_char132,
   v_l_line TYPE ty_char132, wa_l_head TYPE ty_head,
   v_l_spool TYPE tsp01-rqident, v_l_error TYPE string,
   v_l_binary TYPE xstring, it_l_empty TYPE ty_strings.
  IF v_g_total > p_limit.
   log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'PDF' v_i_message = 'PDF no generado: supera P_LIMIT; usar XLSX/TXT' ).
   MESSAGE 'PDF supera limite de consulta; reducir seleccion o usar Excel/TXT' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. RETURN.
  ENDIF.
  IF lines( it_g_head ) < v_g_total.
   log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'PDF' v_i_message = 'PDF no generado: ALV contiene muestra parcial' ).
   MESSAGE 'PDF no incluye muestra parcial. Ampliar P_PREV o reducir seleccion' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. RETURN.
  ENDIF.
  APPEND |Extraccion ECC { v_g_run } - resumen de cabeceras| TO it_l_print.
  LOOP AT it_g_head INTO wa_l_head.
   APPEND |{ wa_l_head-aufnr } { wa_l_head-auart } { wa_l_head-ktext }| TO it_l_print.
   APPEND |Centro { wa_l_head-werks } Creacion { wa_l_head-erdat DATE = USER } Estados { wa_l_head-status }| TO it_l_print.
  ENDLOOP.
  EXPORT it_l_print = it_l_print TO MEMORY ID 'ZSM_OS_PRINT_INPUT'.
  FREE MEMORY ID 'ZSM_OS_PRINT_RESULT'.
  SUBMIT zmpi0013 WITH p_dest = p_dest AND RETURN.
  IMPORT v_l_spool = v_l_spool v_l_error = v_l_error FROM MEMORY ID 'ZSM_OS_PRINT_RESULT'.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
   v_l_error = 'ZSMPI0013 no devolvio el resultado de impresion'.
  ENDIF.
  FREE MEMORY ID 'ZSM_OS_PRINT_INPUT'. FREE MEMORY ID 'ZSM_OS_PRINT_RESULT'.
  IF v_l_spool IS INITIAL OR v_l_error IS NOT INITIAL.
   IF v_l_error IS INITIAL. v_l_error = 'Spool sin identificador'. ENDIF.
   IF v_g_handle IS INITIAL. bal_start( ). ENDIF.
   log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'PDF' v_i_message = v_l_error ).
   bal_save( ).
   MESSAGE v_l_error TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. RETURN.
  ENDIF.
  CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERT_ABAPSPOOLJOB_2_PDF'
   EXPORTING src_spoolid = v_l_spool no_dialog = 'X' pdf_destination = 'X'
   IMPORTING bin_file = v_l_binary EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR v_l_binary IS INITIAL.
   IF v_g_handle IS INITIAL. bal_start( ). ENDIF.
   log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'PDF'
    v_i_message = |No se pudo convertir el spool { v_l_spool } a PDF| ).
   bal_save( ).
   MESSAGE 'No se pudo convertir spool PDF; revisar log SLG1' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
   RETURN.
  ENDIF.
  emit( v_i_entity = 'RESUMEN_CONSULTA' v_i_kind = 'PDF'
   v_i_records = lines( it_g_head ) v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_empty ).
  bal_save( ). IF sy-batch IS INITIAL. download( 'PDF' ). ENDIF.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo close: liberar los controles de la pantalla al terminar la consulta.
*
 METHOD close.
  IF v_go_top IS BOUND. v_go_top->free( ). ENDIF.
  IF v_go_bottom IS BOUND. v_go_bottom->free( ). ENDIF.
  IF v_go_split IS BOUND. v_go_split->free( ). ENDIF.
  IF v_go_container IS BOUND. v_go_container->free( ). ENDIF.
  CLEAR: v_go_top, v_go_bottom, v_go_split, v_go_container, v_go_events.
 ENDMETHOD.
*
* @001 metodo run: coordinar seleccion por paquetes, extraccion, archivos y cierre del log.
*
 METHOD run.
* @007 aplicar SY-LANGU despues de cargar cualquier variante.
  p_lang = sy-langu.
  CLEAR: v_g_stat_pos, v_g_stat_part, v_g_stat_ppart, v_g_stat_nhead,
   v_g_stat_npos, v_g_stat_resp, v_g_stat_order_details.
  CLEAR: it_g_exists, it_g_origin.
* @011 esta entrega habilita el borrador de posiciones aunque una variante antigua lo deje vacio.
  p_pos = abap_true.
  IF sy-batch = abap_true OR v_g_pc_dir IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE 'Esta version requiere ejecucion en dialogo y una carpeta del PC' TYPE 'E'.
  ENDIF.
  DATA: it_l_aufk TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF aufk, wa_l_aufk TYPE aufk,
   v_l_last TYPE aufnr, v_l_uuid TYPE sysuuid_c32,
   it_l_selection TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF rsparams,
   it_l_lines TYPE ty_strings, v_l_binary TYPE xstring,
   v_l_rc TYPE sysubrc, v_l_state TYPE string, v_lx TYPE REF TO cx_root,
   wa_l_export TYPE ty_file, v_l_exported TYPE i, v_l_excel_files TYPE i.
  AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT 'S_APPL_LOG' ID 'ALG_OBJECT' FIELD 'ZSM_OS'
   ID 'ALG_SUBOBJ' FIELD 'EXTRACCION' ID 'ACTVT' FIELD '01'.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. MESSAGE e009(zsm_os). ENDIF.
  CLEAR: it_g_head, it_g_files, it_g_notes, it_g_log, v_g_batch, v_g_total,
   v_g_errors, v_g_warnings, v_g_failed,
   it_g_eval, it_g_diag, v_g_eval_saved, v_g_candidates,
   v_g_accepted, v_g_excluded, v_g_unevaluated.
  TRY. v_l_uuid = cl_system_uuid=>create_uuid_c32_static( ).
   CATCH cx_uuid_error. MESSAGE e010(zsm_os). ENDTRY.
  v_g_run = 'OS2_' && sy-datum && '_' && v_l_uuid.
  CLEAR: it_g_wip, it_g_wip_view, it_g_pending, it_g_fuentes,
   it_g_validas, it_g_validas_view, v_g_wip_r, v_g_wip_n, v_g_fuentes_limite.
  p_obj = abap_true.
  bal_start( ).
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'INICIO'
   v_i_message = 'Inicio de extraccion de ordenes de servicio ECC. Version ECC_TEC_20261119_10' ).
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'INICIO'
   v_i_message = |Sistema { sy-sysid }; mandante { sy-mandt }; usuario { sy-uname }; programa { sy-repid }| ).
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'INICIO'
   v_i_message = |Fecha de corte { p_corte DATE = USER }; tolerancia { p_toler NUMBER = RAW }; modo simulado { p_simul }| ).
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'VERSION_TECNICA'
   v_i_message = 'ECC_TEC_20261119_10: Excel tecnico A1-A10; referencia para llenado manual; controles existentes' ).
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'CONTEXTO_SAP'
   v_i_message = |SID={ sy-sysid }; MANDT={ sy-mandt }; SAPRL={ sy-saprl }; USUARIO={ sy-uname }; CORTE={ p_corte }; TOL| &&
    |ERANCIA={ p_toler NUMBER = RAW }| ).
* @017 alcance temporal: tablas Z de WIP/equivalencias pendientes de definicion.
  log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'ALCANCE_ACTUAL'
   v_i_message = `Extraccion tecnica existente; consulta WIP parcial independiente. Sin tablas Z, homolo` &&
    `gaciones ni decisiones finales de migracion` ).

  CALL FUNCTION 'RS_REFRESH_FROM_SELECTOPTIONS'
   EXPORTING curr_report = sy-repid TABLES selection_table = it_l_selection
   EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0. MESSAGE e011(zsm_os). ENDIF.
  DATA wa_l_selection TYPE rsparams.
  LOOP AT it_l_selection INTO wa_l_selection.
   log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'SELECCION'
    v_i_message = |{ wa_l_selection-selname } { wa_l_selection-sign } { wa_l_selection-option } { wa_l_selection-low } | &&
     |{ wa_l_selection-high }| ).
  ENDLOOP.
  it_l_lines = serialize( it_l_selection ).
  emit( v_i_entity = 'SELECCION' v_i_kind = 'TXT' v_i_records = lines( it_l_selection )
   v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'REGLA'
   v_i_message = 'Abiertas: excluir LOEKZ y estados activos I0045 I0046 I0076 I0013; validar regla funcional' ).
  log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'PLANTILLA'
   v_i_message = 'XLSX conservado como archivo aportado. Introduccion cita XML 2003; validar formato real con Cockpit' ).
  bal_save( ). CLEAR it_g_log.
  TRY.
   DO.
    CLEAR it_l_aufk.
    SELECT * FROM aufk INTO TABLE it_l_aufk UP TO p_pack ROWS
     WHERE autyp = '30' AND aufnr > v_l_last AND aufnr IN s_aufnr AND auart IN s_auart
       AND werks IN s_werks AND bukrs IN s_bukrs
       AND erdat IN s_erdat AND loekz = space
     ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY.
    IF it_l_aufk IS INITIAL. EXIT. ENDIF.
    READ TABLE it_l_aufk INDEX lines( it_l_aufk ) INTO wa_l_aufk.
    v_l_last = wa_l_aufk-aufnr. ADD 1 TO v_g_batch. bal_start( ).
    log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'PAQUETE'
     v_i_message = |Cabeceras candidatas { lines( it_l_aufk ) }; hasta { v_l_last }| ).
    wip_prepare( it_l_aufk ).
    process( it_l_aufk ).
    wip_save( ).
    eval_save( ).
    it_l_lines = serialize( it_g_log ).
    emit( v_i_entity = 'LOG_PAQUETE' v_i_kind = 'TXT' v_i_records = lines( it_g_log )
     v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
    bal_save( ). CLEAR it_g_log.
    IF v_g_failed = abap_true. EXIT. ENDIF.
   ENDDO.
  CATCH cx_root INTO v_lx.
   v_g_failed = abap_true.
   IF v_g_handle IS INITIAL. bal_start( ). ENDIF.
   log( v_i_type = 'E' v_i_entity = 'RUN' v_i_message = v_lx->get_text( ) ).
   eval_save( ).
  ENDTRY.
* @006 pdf automatico de fondo desactivado; mantener boton pdf en dialogo.
*   IF p_pdfbg = 'X' AND v_g_failed = abap_false.
*    IF v_g_handle IS INITIAL. ADD 1 TO v_g_batch. bal_start( ). ENDIF.
*    pdf( ).
*   ENDIF.
  IF v_g_handle IS INITIAL. ADD 1 TO v_g_batch. bal_start( ). ENDIF.
* @013 cierre de evaluacion; no confundir aceptada por filtro con orden exportada.
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'CONCILIACION'
   v_i_message = |Total candidatas { v_g_candidates } = aceptadas { v_g_accepted } + excluidas { v_g_excluded } + no eva| &&
    |luadas { v_g_unevaluated }| ).
  CLEAR it_l_lines.
  APPEND 'CANDIDATAS;ACEPTADAS_FILTRO;EXCLUIDAS;NO_EVALUADAS;SIMULACION' TO it_l_lines.
  APPEND |{ v_g_candidates };{ v_g_accepted };{ v_g_excluded };{ v_g_unevaluated };{ p_simul }| TO it_l_lines.
  CLEAR v_l_binary.
  DATA it_l_wsum TYPE ty_strings.
  APPEND 'TOTAL;N;R;CORTE;TOLERANCIA;MODO' TO it_l_wsum.
  APPEND |{ v_g_wip_n + v_g_wip_r };{ v_g_wip_n };{ v_g_wip_r };{ p_corte };{ p_toler NUMBER = RAW };CONSULTA_NO| &&
   |_FILTRA_DESCARGA| TO it_l_wsum.
  emit( v_i_entity = 'WIP_RESUMEN' v_i_kind = 'TXT' v_i_records = 1
   v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_wsum ).
  emit( v_i_entity = 'RESUMEN_EVALUACION' v_i_kind = 'TXT' v_i_records = 1
   v_i_binary = v_l_binary it_lines = it_l_lines ).
  IF v_g_total = 0. log( v_i_type = 'W' v_i_entity = 'CIERRE' v_i_message =
   'Sin ordenes aceptadas por los filtros tecnicos actuales; revisar EVALUACION_ORDENES' ). ENDIF.
  IF v_g_failed = abap_true. v_l_state = 'INCOMPLETA'. ELSEIF p_simul = abap_true. v_l_state = 'SIMULACION TECNICA'.
  ELSE. v_l_state = 'EXTRAIDA PENDIENTE VALIDACION'. ENDIF.
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'CIERRE'
   v_i_message = |{ v_l_state }; ordenes { v_g_total }; errores { v_g_errors }; avisos { v_g_warnings }| ).
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'RESUMEN'
   v_i_message = |Ejecucion { v_g_run }; SID { sy-sysid }; mandante { sy-mandt }; estado { v_l_state }| ).
  log( v_i_type = 'I' v_i_entity = 'RESUMEN'
   v_i_message = |Evaluadas { v_g_candidates }; aceptadas por filtro { v_g_accepted }; excluidas { v_g_excluded }; sin e| &&
    |valuar { v_g_unevaluated }| ).
  CLEAR: v_l_exported, v_l_excel_files.
  LOOP AT it_g_files INTO wa_l_export WHERE kind = 'XLSX' AND state = 'OK'.
